import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';

import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/email_phone/phone_signin.dart';

//80001fcxvoid
//

void main() {
  runApp(
    ChangeNotifierProvider(
      create: (context) => SharedProvider(),
      child: const MyApp(),
    ),
  );
}

class MyApp extends StatelessWidget {
  const MyApp({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return AdaptiveTheme(
      light: lightTH,
      dark: darkTH,
      initial: AdaptiveThemeMode.light,
      overrideMode: AdaptiveThemeMode.light,
      builder:
          (theme, darkTheme) => MaterialApp(
            debugShowCheckedModeBanner: false,
            title: 'Flexi Phones',
            theme: theme,

            builder: (context, child) {
              final MediaQueryData data = MediaQuery.of(context);
              return MediaQuery(
                data: data.copyWith(
                  textScaler: TextScaler.noScaling,
                ), // Use TextScaler.noScaling for Flutter 3.29+
                child: child!,
              );
            },
            home:  InitScreen(),
          ),
    );
  }


}

var lightTH = ThemeData(
  useMaterial3: true,

  splashColor: Colors.lightBlueAccent.withOpacity(0.1), // Remove splash ripple
  highlightColor: Colors.blueAccent[50], // Remove highlight on tap
  hoverColor: Colors.transparent,
  colorScheme: ColorScheme.fromSeed(
    seedColor: Colors.blue,
    primary: AppColors.primaryColor,
    surface: const Color.fromARGB(255, 253, 254, 255),
  ),
);

=================== FILE: lib\extensions\value_validation.dart ===================
extension PhoneNumberValidator on String {
  bool get isValidPhoneNumber {
    final regex = RegExp(r'^[0-9]\d{8}$');
    return regex.hasMatch(this);
  }
}

extension GhanaCardDigitsValidator on String {
  bool get isValidGhanaCardDigits {
    final regex = RegExp(r'^[0-9]{9}-[0-9]$');
    return regex.hasMatch(this);
  }
}

extension EmailValidator on String {
  bool get isValidEmail {
    final regex = RegExp(
      r'^[a-zA-Z0-9._%+-]+@(?!(example\.com|example\.org|example\.net)$)[a-zA-Z0-9.-]+\.[a-zA-Z]{2,}$',
      caseSensitive: false,
    );
    return regex.hasMatch(this);
  }
}

extension PasswordValidator on String {
  bool get isValidPassword {
    final regex = RegExp(
      r'^(?=.*[A-Z])(?=.*[a-z])(?=.*\d)(?=.*[!@#\$%^&*(),.?":{}|<>]).{8,}$',
    );
    return regex.hasMatch(this);
  }
}

extension StringLetterCheck on String {
  bool get containsLetters => RegExp(r'[a-zA-Z]').hasMatch(this);
}

extension DateTimeThreshold on DateTime {
  /// Returns `true` if this DateTime is at least current time minus 30 seconds.
  bool isWithinLast30SecondsOrFuture() {
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final diff = now.difference(this);
    // If difference is negative: this > now → future: true
    // If difference is ≤ 30 seconds: true
    return diff.inSeconds <= 20;
  }
}


=================== FILE: lib\helpers\api_helper.dart ===================
import 'package:flutter_secure_storage/flutter_secure_storage.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

class DS {
  static final storage = FlutterSecureStorage();
  static final sharedStorage = SharedPreferences.getInstance();
}

class ApiUrls {
  static const base = "https://api.flexiphones.app/api";
  // static const base = "https://portal.flexiphones.app/api";
  // static const base = "http://192.168.100.25:8005/api";
  static const ImageBase = "https://api.flexiphones.app/assets/images/";
  // static const ImageBase = "https://portal.flexiphones.app/assets/images/";
  //?USER
  static const customer = "$base/customer";
  static const emailVerificationPST = "$customer/email-verification";
  static const otpVerificationPST = "$customer/email-otp-verification";
  static const finalRegistrationPST = "$customer/registration";
  static const loginPST = "$customer/login";
  static const forgotPasswordPST = "$customer/forgot-password";
  static const resetPasswordPST = "$customer/create-new-password";
  static const updateGHCard = "$customer/update-ghana-card/";
  static const updateUSER = "$customer/profile/update";
  static const userNotifications = "$base/v1/notifications";
  //?Products
  static const getProducts = "$base/v1/products";
  static const getProductsOFF = "$base/v1/products";

  // NOTE: two different product-detail routes exist on the backend.
  // getProductsDetailsOFF ("/v1/product/{id}") is the confirmed
  // customer-facing one (tested manually and it works). getProductsDetails
  // ("/v1/get-product/{id}") is a different route and is NOT what
  // getProductDetails() in products.dart calls anymore — see that file.
  static const getProductsDetails = "$base/v1/product";
  static const getProductsDetailsOFF = "$base/v1/product";

  static const searchProducts = "$base/v1/search";

  static const topDeals = "$base/v1/deals/get-top-deals";
  static const topDealsOFF = "$base/v1/deals/top-deals";

  static const hotDeals = "$base/v1/deals/hot-deals";
  static const hotDealsOFF = "$base/v1/deals/hot-deals";
  //? product Rating
  static const rateProduct = "$base/v1/create-product-review";
  //?Vendors
  static const getVendors = "$base/v1/get-vendors";
  static const getVendorDetails = "$base/v1/get-vendor";
  //?orders
  static const addOrders = "$base/v1/orders/create-order";
  static const getOrders = "$base/v1/orders/get-customer-orders";
  static const getOrder = "$base/v1/orders/get-order-by-id";
  static const cancelOrder = "$base/v1/orders/cancel-order";
  static const repaymentCL = "$base/v1/orders/get-repayment-history";
  //
  static const priceBrakeDown = "$base/v1/price-breakdown";

  //?PARTNER (Agent)
  // ADJUST THESE PATHS once you confirm from Postman — everything else
  // in agent.dart reads only from these constants, so fixing a wrong
  // path here is the only change needed.
  static const partner = "$base/v1/partner";
  static const partnerRegisterPST = "$partner/register";
  static const partnerLoginPST = "$partner/login";
  static const partnerProfileGET = "$partner/profile";
  static const partnerOnboardVendorPST = "$partner/onboard-vendor";
  static const partnerVendorsGET = "$partner/vendors";
  static const partnerWalletGET = "$partner/wallet";
  static const partnerWalletWithdrawPST = "$partner/wallet/withdraw";
  static const partnerTransactionsGET = "$partner/wallet/transactions";


  /// Some endpoints (e.g. product detail) return a full absolute image
  /// URL already (e.g. placeholder.com links), while others return a
  /// bare filename that needs [ImageBase] prefixed. Blindly prefixing
  /// an already-absolute URL produces a broken doubled-up link — this
  /// resolves both cases safely.
  static String resolveImage(dynamic path) {
    if (path == null) return "";
    final p = path.toString();
    if (p.isEmpty) return "";
    if (p.startsWith("http://") || p.startsWith("https://")) return p;
    return "$ImageBase$p";
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\models\agent_m.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';

/// Real partner/agent account data, as returned by
/// ApiUrls.partnerRegisterPST / partnerLoginPST / partnerProfileGET.
/// No plans, no tiers — every partner has the same features.
class PartnerData {
  final int id;
  final String fullName;
  final String phone;
  final String region;
  final String? ghanaCardNumber;
  final String status; // pending | approved | rejected
  final String? token;
  final int points;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  PartnerData({
    required this.id,
    required this.fullName,
    required this.phone,
    required this.region,
    this.ghanaCardNumber,
    required this.status,
    this.token,
    required this.points,
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory PartnerData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    // Some endpoints (login/register) wrap the record in "data" and put
    // the token alongside it; profile GET may return it flat. Handle both.
    final data = json['data'] is Map<String, dynamic> ? json['data'] : json;

    return PartnerData(
      id: data['id'],
      fullName: data['full_name'] ?? data['name'] ?? "",
      phone: data['phone']?.toString() ?? "",
      region: data['region'] ?? "",
      ghanaCardNumber: data['ghana_card_number'] ?? data['ghana_card'],
      status: data['status'] ?? "pending",
      token: json['token'] ?? data['token'],
      points: data['points'] is int
          ? data['points']
          : int.tryParse("${data['points']}") ?? 0,
      createdAt: data['created_at'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(data['created_at']) ?? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'full_name': fullName,
        'phone': phone,
        'region': region,
        'ghana_card_number': ghanaCardNumber,
        'status': status,
        'token': token,
        'points': points,
        'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      };
}

/// Vendor an agent submitted for onboarding — returned by
/// ApiUrls.partnerOnboardVendorPST / partnerVendorsGET.
class VendorOnboarding {
  final int? id;
  final String shopName;
  final String ownerName;
  final String ownerPhone;
  final String businessAddress;
  final int? estimatedMonthlyDevices;
  final String status; // pending | live | rejected
  final DateTime createdAt;

  VendorOnboarding({
    this.id,
    required this.shopName,
    required this.ownerName,
    required this.ownerPhone,
    required this.businessAddress,
    this.estimatedMonthlyDevices,
    this.status = "pending",
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory VendorOnboarding.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return VendorOnboarding(
      id: json['id'],
      shopName: json['shop_name'] ?? "",
      ownerName: json['owner_name'] ?? "",
      ownerPhone: json['owner_phone']?.toString() ?? "",
      businessAddress: json['business_address'] ?? "",
      estimatedMonthlyDevices: json['estimated_monthly_devices'],
      status: json['status'] ?? "pending",
      createdAt: json['created_at'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at']) ?? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'shop_name': shopName,
        'owner_name': ownerName,
        'owner_phone': ownerPhone,
        'business_address': businessAddress,
        'estimated_monthly_devices': estimatedMonthlyDevices,
        'status': status,
        'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      };
}

/// Partner wallet balance + recent transactions — ApiUrls.partnerWalletGET.
class PartnerWallet {
  final double balance;
  final double pendingBalance;
  final String currency;
  final List<WalletTransaction> transactions;

  PartnerWallet({
    required this.balance,
    required this.pendingBalance,
    required this.currency,
    required this.transactions,
  });

  factory PartnerWallet.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    final data = json['data'] is Map<String, dynamic> ? json['data'] : json;
    return PartnerWallet(
      balance: double.tryParse("${data['balance']}") ?? 0.0,
      pendingBalance: double.tryParse("${data['pending_balance']}") ?? 0.0,
      currency: data['currency'] ?? "GHS",
      transactions: data['transactions'] == null
          ? []
          : List<WalletTransaction>.from(
              (data['transactions'] as List)
                  .map((x) => WalletTransaction.fromJson(x)),
            ),
    );
  }
}

class WalletTransaction {
  final int id;
  final String type; // credit | debit
  final double amount;
  final String description;
  final String status;
  final DateTime createdAt;

  WalletTransaction({
    required this.id,
    required this.type,
    required this.amount,
    required this.description,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
  });

  factory WalletTransaction.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return WalletTransaction(
      id: json['id'],
      type: json['type'] ?? "credit",
      amount: double.tryParse("${json['amount']}") ?? 0.0,
      description: json['description'] ?? "",
      status: json['status'] ?? "completed",
      createdAt: json['created_at'] != null
          ? DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at']) ?? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.now(),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';

class Customer {
  final int id;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String phone;
  final String address;
  final String? ghanaCard;
  final String? profilePhoto;
  final String status;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  Customer({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.phone,
    required this.address,
    required this.ghanaCard,
    this.profilePhoto,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  factory Customer.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Customer(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'],
      email: json['email'],
      phone: json['phone'],
      address: json['address'],
      ghanaCard: json['ghana_card'],
      profilePhoto: "${ApiUrls.ImageBase}${json['profile_photo']}" ,
      status: json['status'],
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(json['updated_at']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'name': name,
        'email': email,
        'phone': phone,
        'address': address,
        'ghana_card': ghanaCard,
        'profile_photo': profilePhoto,
        'status': status,
        'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
        'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
      };
}

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';

class PromotedProductsResponse {
  final String status;
  final String message;
  final List<HotDeal> hotDeals;

  PromotedProductsResponse({
    required this.status,
    required this.message,
    required this.hotDeals,
  });

  factory PromotedProductsResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return PromotedProductsResponse(
      status: json['status'],
      message: json['message'],
      hotDeals: List<HotDeal>.from(
        json['hot_deals'].map((x) => HotDeal.fromJson(x)),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class HotDeal {
  final int id;
  final int vendorId;
  final String brand;
  final String model;
  final String fullName;
  final String colors;
  final String deviceSpecification;
  final String deviceType;
  final String image;
  final String status;
  final String rating;
  final String createdAt;
  final String updatedAt;
  final List<String> storages;

  HotDeal({
    required this.id,
    required this.vendorId,
    required this.brand,
    required this.model,
    required this.fullName,
    required this.colors,
    required this.deviceSpecification,
    required this.deviceType,
    required this.image,
    required this.status,
    required this.rating,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.storages,
  });

  factory HotDeal.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return HotDeal(
      id: json['id'],
      vendorId: json['vendor_id'],
      brand: json['brand'],
      model: json['model'],
      colors: json['colors'],
      fullName: "${ json['brand']} ${json['model']}",
      deviceSpecification: json['device_specification'],
      deviceType: json['device_type'],
      image: "${ApiUrls.ImageBase}${json['image']}",
      status: json['status'],
      rating: json['rating'],
      createdAt: json['created_at'],
      updatedAt: json['updated_at'],
      storages: List<String>.from(
        json['product_storages'].map((s) => s['storage']),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\models\packages.dart ===================
import 'package:intl/intl.dart';

class Package {
  final int id;
  final int partnerId;
  final String price; // monthly price (string from API, e.g. "35.00")
  final int onnetAllocation; // in minutes (monthly)
  final int offnetAllocation; // in minutes (monthly)
  final int dataAllocation; // in MB (monthly)
  final int smsAllocation; // (monthly)
  final int validity; // in days
  final int isActive;
  final int isStandard;

  Package({
    required this.id,
    required this.partnerId,
    required this.price,
    required this.onnetAllocation,
    required this.offnetAllocation,
    required this.dataAllocation,
    required this.smsAllocation,
    required this.validity,
    required this.isActive,
    required this.isStandard,
  });

  /// ========== MONTHLY FORMATTERS ==========
  String get formattedDataAllocation {
    final formatter = NumberFormat.decimalPattern();
    if (dataAllocation < 2000) {
      return "${formatter.format(dataAllocation)} MB";
    } else {
      double gb = dataAllocation / 1024;
      return "${gb.toStringAsFixed(2)} GB";
    }
  }

  String formatMinutes(int minutes) {
    if (minutes < 60) {
      return "$minutes min";
    } else {
      int hours = minutes ~/ 60;
      int mins = minutes % 60;
      if (mins == 0) {
        return "${hours}h";
      } else {
        return "${hours}h ${mins}m";
      }
    }
  }

  String get formattedOnnet => formatMinutes(onnetAllocation);
  String get formattedOffnet => formatMinutes(offnetAllocation);

  String get formattedSms {
    final formatter = NumberFormat.decimalPattern();
    if (smsAllocation < 2000) {
      return formatter.format(smsAllocation);
    } else {
      double kValue = smsAllocation / 1000;
      return "${kValue.toStringAsFixed(1)}K";
    }
  }

  /// ========== DAILY CALCULATIONS ==========
  int get dailyDataAllocation => (dataAllocation / validity).ceil();
  int get dailyOnnetAllocation => (onnetAllocation / validity).ceil();
  int get dailyOffnetAllocation => (offnetAllocation / validity).ceil();
  int get dailySmsAllocation => (smsAllocation / validity).ceil();

  /// Daily formatted values
  String get formattedDailyData {
    final formatter = NumberFormat.decimalPattern();
    if (dailyDataAllocation < 2000) {
      return "${formatter.format(dailyDataAllocation)} MB / day";
    } else {
      double gb = dailyDataAllocation / 1024;
      return "${gb.toStringAsFixed(2)} GB / day";
    }
  }

  String get formattedDailyOnnet =>
      "${formatMinutes(dailyOnnetAllocation)} / day";
  String get formattedDailyOffnet =>
      "${formatMinutes(dailyOffnetAllocation)} / day";

  String get formattedDailySms {
    final formatter = NumberFormat.decimalPattern();
    if (dailySmsAllocation < 2000) {
      return "${formatter.format(dailySmsAllocation)} / day";
    } else {
      double kValue = dailySmsAllocation / 1000;
      return "${kValue.toStringAsFixed(1)}K / day";
    }
  }

  /// ========== PRICE ==========
  double get monthlyPrice => double.tryParse(price) ?? 0.0;
  double get dailyPrice =>
      validity > 0 ? monthlyPrice / validity : monthlyPrice;

  String get formattedDailyPrice => "₵${dailyPrice.toStringAsFixed(2)} / day";

  factory Package.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Package(
      id: json['id'],
      partnerId: json['partner_id'],
      price: json['price'],
      onnetAllocation: json['onnet_allocation'],
      offnetAllocation: json['offnet_allocation'],
      dataAllocation: json['data_allocation'],
      smsAllocation: json['sms_allocation'],
      validity: (json['validity'] * 5).toInt(), // Convert months to days
      isActive: json['is_active'],
      isStandard: json['is_standard'],
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'partner_id': partnerId,
      'price': price,
      'onnet_allocation': onnetAllocation,
      'offnet_allocation': offnetAllocation,
      'data_allocation': dataAllocation,
      'sms_allocation': smsAllocation,
      'validity': validity,
      'is_active': isActive,
      'is_standard': isStandard,
    };
  }
}


=================== FILE: lib\models\product_m.dart ===================
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/customer_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/vendors.dart';

class ProductResponseModel {
  final String? status;
  final String message;
  final ProductListData? products;
  final ProductModel? product;

  ProductResponseModel({
    required this.status,
    required this.message,
    this.products,
    this.product,
  });

  factory ProductResponseModel.fromJson(json) {
    return ProductResponseModel(
      status: json['status'],
      message: json['message'],
      products: json['products'] != null
          ? ProductListData.fromJson(json['products'])
          : null,
      product:
          json['product'] != null ? ProductModel.fromJson(json['product']) : null,
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'status': status,
        'message': message,
        if (products != null) 'products': products!.toJson(),
        if (product != null) 'product': product!.toJson(),
      };
}

class ProductListData {
  final List<ProductModel> data;
  final String path;
  final int perPage;
  final dynamic nextCursor;
  final dynamic nextPageUrl;
  final dynamic prevCursor;
  final dynamic prevPageUrl;

  ProductListData({
    required this.data,
    required this.path,
    required this.perPage,
    this.nextCursor,
    this.nextPageUrl,
    this.prevCursor,
    this.prevPageUrl,
  });

  factory ProductListData.fromJson(json) {
    return ProductListData(
      data: List<ProductModel>.from(
        (json['data'] as List).map((x) => ProductModel.fromJson(x)),
      ),
      path: json['path'] ?? "",
      perPage: json['per_page'] ?? 20,
      nextCursor: json['next_cursor'],
      nextPageUrl: json['next_page_url'],
      prevCursor: json['prev_cursor'],
      prevPageUrl: json['prev_page_url'],
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'data': data.map((x) => x.toJson()).toList(),
        'path': path,
        'per_page': perPage,
        'next_cursor': nextCursor,
        'next_page_url': nextPageUrl,
        'prev_cursor': prevCursor,
        'prev_page_url': prevPageUrl,
      };
}

class ProductModel {
  final int id;
  final int? vendorId;
  final String brand;
  final double price;

  final String fullName;
  final String model;
  final String colors;
  final String? deviceSpecification;
  final String image;
  final List<dynamic>? images;
  final String status;
  final DateTime? addedAt;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;
  final List<ProductStorage>? productStorages;
  final Vendor? vendor;
  final List<Review>? reviews;

  ProductModel({
    required this.id,
    required this.vendorId,
    required this.brand,
    required this.model,
    required this.price,
    required this.colors,
    required this.deviceSpecification,
    required this.image,
    required this.images,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.addedAt,
    required this.productStorages,
    required this.vendor,
    required this.fullName,
    required this.reviews,
  });

  /// Normalizes colors coming in either of two shapes seen from the API:
  ///   - Postgres-array style:  "{WHITE}" / "{Black,Silver,Gold}"
  ///   - JSON-array-as-string:  "[\"Black\",\"Silver\",\"Gold\"]"
  /// Output is always the "{A,B,C}" form, since that's what the UI
  /// (product_details.dart, product_card.dart, product_storage_card.dart)
  /// already knows how to strip and split.
  static String _normalizeColors(dynamic raw) {
    if (raw == null) return "{}";
    String s = raw.toString().trim();
    if (s.isEmpty) return "{}";

    if (s.startsWith("[")) {
      try {
        final decoded = jsonDecode(s);
        if (decoded is List) {
          return "{${decoded.map((e) => e.toString()).join(",")}}";
        }
      } catch (_) {
        s = s.replaceAll("[", "").replaceAll("]", "").replaceAll('"', "");
        return "{$s}";
      }
    }

    if (!s.startsWith("{")) {
      return "{$s}";
    }
    return s;
  }

  /// Handles the "image" field being either a bare filename, a full
  /// absolute URL, or a List containing either of those.
  static String _resolveMainImage(dynamic rawImage) {
    dynamic single = rawImage;
    if (rawImage is List) {
      single = rawImage.isNotEmpty ? rawImage.first : null;
    }
    return ApiUrls.resolveImage(single);
  }

  factory ProductModel.fromJson(json) {
    final mainImage = _resolveMainImage(json['image']);

    return ProductModel(
      id: json['id'],
      vendorId: json['vendor_id'],
      price: json['price'] == null ? 0.0 : double.parse("${json['price']}"),
      brand: json['brand'] ?? "",
      fullName: "${json['brand'] ?? ''} ${json['model'] ?? ''}".trim(),
      model: json['model'] ?? "",
      colors: _normalizeColors(json['colors']),
      deviceSpecification: json['device_specification'],
      image: mainImage,
      images: json['images'] == null
          ? [mainImage]
          : (json['images'] as List).isEmpty
              ? [mainImage]
              : (json['images'] as List)
                  .map((x) => ApiUrls.resolveImage(x is Map ? x["image_path"] : x))
                  .toList(),
      status: json['status'] ?? "",
      addedAt: json['addedAt'] is DateTime
          ? json['addedAt']
          : (json['addedAt'] == null
              ? DateTime.now()
              : DateTime.tryParse(json['addedAt'].toString()) ?? DateTime.now()),
      createdAt:
          json['created_at'] == null ? null : DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt:
          json['updated_at'] == null ? null : DateTime.tryParse(json['updated_at']),
      productStorages: json['product_storages'] == null
          ? null
          : List<ProductStorage>.from(
              (json['product_storages'] as List)
                  .map((x) => ProductStorage.fromJson(x)),
            ),
      vendor: json['vendor'] == null ? null : Vendor.fromJson(json['vendor']),
      reviews: json['reviews'] == null
          ? null
          : List<Review>.from(
              (json['reviews'] as List).map((x) => Review.fromJson(x)),
            ),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'vendor_id': vendorId,
        'brand': brand,
        'model': model,
        'colors': colors,
        'device_specification': deviceSpecification,
        'image': image,
        'status': status,
        'created_at': createdAt?.toIso8601String(),
        'updated_at': updatedAt?.toIso8601String(),
        'product_storages': productStorages?.map((x) => x.toJson()).toList(),
        'vendor': vendor?.toJson(),
        'reviews': reviews,
      };
}

class ProductStorage {
  final int id;
  final int productId;
  final String storage;
  final String cashPrice;
  final String interestRate;
  final String threeMonthPrice;
  final String fourMonthPrice;
  final String fiveMonthPrice;
  final String sixMonthPrice;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  ProductStorage({
    required this.id,
    required this.productId,
    required this.storage,
    required this.cashPrice,
    required this.interestRate,
    required this.threeMonthPrice,
    required this.fourMonthPrice,
    required this.fiveMonthPrice,
    required this.sixMonthPrice,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  factory ProductStorage.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return ProductStorage(
      id: json['id'],
      productId: json['product_id'],
      storage: json['storage'] ?? "",
      cashPrice: "${json['cash_price'] ?? '0'}",
      interestRate: "${json['interest_rate'] ?? '0'}",
      threeMonthPrice: "${json['three_month_price'] ?? '0'}",
      fourMonthPrice: "${json['four_month_price'] ?? '0'}",
      fiveMonthPrice: "${json['five_month_price'] ?? '0'}",
      sixMonthPrice: "${json['six_month_price'] ?? '0'}",
      createdAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['updated_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'product_id': productId,
        'storage': storage,
        'cash_price': cashPrice,
        'interest_rate': interestRate,
        'three_month_price': threeMonthPrice,
        'four_month_price': fourMonthPrice,
        'five_month_price': fiveMonthPrice,
        'six_month_price': sixMonthPrice,
        'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
        'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
      };
}

class Review {
  final int id;
  final int customerId;
  final String reviewableType;
  final int reviewableId;
  final String comment;
  final int rating;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

    required this.id,
    required this.customerId,
    required this.reviewableType,
    required this.reviewableId,
    required this.comment,
    required this.rating,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.customer,
  });

  factory Review.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Review(
      id: json['id'],
      customerId: json['customer_id'],
      reviewableType: json['reviewable_type'] ?? "",
      reviewableId: json['reviewable_id'] ?? 0,
      comment: json['comment'] ?? "",
      rating: double.tryParse("${json['rating']}")?.toInt() ?? 0,
      createdAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['created_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      updatedAt: DateTime.tryParse(json['updated_at'] ?? '') ?? DateTime.now(),
      customer:
          json['customer'] == null ? null : Customer.fromJson(json['customer']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'customer_id': customerId,
        'reviewable_type': reviewableType,
        'reviewable_id': reviewableId,
        'comment': comment,
        'rating': rating,
        'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
        'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
        'customer': customer?.toJson(),
      };
}

        before: json['before'] as String,

        after: json['after'] as String,

  }

class SearchData {
  final List<SearchProduct> products;
  final String path;
  final int perPage;
  final String? nextCursor;
  final String? nextPageUrl;
  final String? prevCursor;
  final String? prevPageUrl;

  SearchData({
    required this.products,
    required this.path,
    required this.perPage,
    this.nextCursor,
    this.nextPageUrl,
    this.prevCursor,
    this.prevPageUrl,
  });

  factory SearchData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return SearchData(
      products: List<SearchProduct>.from(
        json['data'].map((x) => SearchProduct.fromJson(x)),
      ),
      path: json['path'],
      perPage: json['per_page'],
      nextCursor: json['next_cursor'],
      nextPageUrl: json['next_page_url'],
      prevCursor: json['prev_cursor'],
      prevPageUrl: json['prev_page_url'],
    );
  }
}

class SearchProduct {
  final int id;
  final int vendorId;
  final String brand;
  final String model;
  final String colors;
  final String deviceSpecification;
  final String deviceType;
  final String image;
  final String status;
  final String rating;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;

  final List<ProductStorage> productStorages;

  SearchProduct({
    required this.id,
    required this.vendorId,
    required this.brand,
    required this.model,
    required this.colors,
    required this.deviceSpecification,
    required this.deviceType,
    required this.image,
    required this.status,
    required this.rating,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    required this.vendor,
    required this.productStorages,
  });

      vendorId: json['vendor_id'],
      brand: json['brand'],
      model: json['model'],
      colors: json['colors'],
      deviceSpecification: json['device_specification'],
      deviceType: json['device_type'],

      status: json['status'],
      rating: json['rating'],
      createdAt: DateTime.parse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt: DateTime.parse(json['updated_at']),
      vendor: Vendor.fromJson(json['vendor']),
      productStorages: List<ProductStorage>.from(
        json['product_storages'].map((x) => ProductStorage.fromJson(x)),
      ),
    );
  }

=================== FILE: lib\models\user_m.dart ===================
class LoginResponse {
  final String status;
  final String? message;
  final String? token;
  final UserData data;

  LoginResponse({
    required this.status,
    required this.message,
    required this.token,
    required this.data,
  });

  factory LoginResponse.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return LoginResponse(
      status: json['status'],
      message: json['message'],
      token: json['token'],
      data: UserData.fromJson(json['data']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'status': status,
        'message': message,
        'token': token,
        'data': data.toJson(),
      };
}


class UserData {
  final int id;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String phone;
  final String? address;
  final String? ghanaCard;
  final String? profilePhoto;
  final String status;
  final DateTime? createdAt;
  final DateTime? updatedAt;

  UserData({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.phone,
    required this.address,
    required this.ghanaCard,
    required this.profilePhoto,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
  });

  factory UserData.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return UserData(
      id: json['id'] ?? json['customer_id'],
      name: json['name'],
      email: json['email'],
      phone: json['phone'],
      address: json['address'],
      ghanaCard: json['ghana_card'],
      profilePhoto: json['profile_photo'],
      status: json['status'],
      createdAt: json['created_at'] == null ? null : DateTime.parse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt: json['updated_at'] == null ? null : DateTime.parse(json['updated_at']),
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() => {
        'id': id,
        'name': name,
        'email': email,
        'phone': phone,
        'address': address,
        'ghana_card': ghanaCard,
        'profile_photo': profilePhoto,
        'status': status,
        'created_at': createdAt?.toIso8601String(),
        'updated_at': updatedAt?.toIso8601String(),
      };
}

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/product_m.dart';

class VendorsResponse {
  final String status;
  final String message;
  final VendorsData vendors;

  VendorsResponse({
    required this.status,
    required this.message,
    required this.vendors,
  });

  factory VendorsResponse.fromJson(json) {
    return VendorsResponse(
      status: json['status'],
      message: json['message'],
      vendors: VendorsData.fromJson(json['vendors']),
    );
  }
}

class VendorsData {
  final List<Vendor> data;
  final String path;
  final int perPage;
  final String? nextCursor;
  final String? nextPageUrl;
  final String? prevCursor;
  final String? prevPageUrl;

  VendorsData({
    required this.data,
    required this.path,
    required this.perPage,
    this.nextCursor,
    this.nextPageUrl,
    this.prevCursor,
    this.prevPageUrl,
  });

  factory VendorsData.fromJson(json) {
    return VendorsData(
      data: List<Vendor>.from(json['data'].map((x) => Vendor.fromJson(x))),
      path: json['path'],
      perPage: json['per_page'],
      nextCursor: json['next_cursor'],
      nextPageUrl: json['next_page_url'],
      prevCursor: json['prev_cursor'],
      prevPageUrl: json['prev_page_url'],
    );
  }
}

class Vendor {
  final int id;
  final String name;
  final String email;
  final String phoneNumber;
  final String? image;
  final String location;

  /// NOTE: The backend legitimately returns `null` for these when a vendor
  /// hasn't set business hours yet (confirmed in the live /v1/product/{id}
  /// response). These MUST stay nullable — treating them as non-null String
  /// crashes JSON parsing with "type 'Null' is not a subtype of type
  /// 'String'" the instant a vendor without hours is fetched, which silently
  /// aborts getProductDetails()/ProductModel.fromJson() before loadingProduct
  /// is ever reset, leaving the UI stuck on its loading shimmer forever.
  final String? openingTime;
  final String? closingTime;

  final int? pickupAvailable;
  final int? deliveryAvailable;
  final String status;
  final DateTime createdAt;
  final DateTime updatedAt;
  final String? deletedAt;

  Vendor({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    required this.email,
    required this.phoneNumber,
    this.image,
    required this.location,
    required this.pickupAvailable,
    required this.deliveryAvailable,
    this.openingTime,
    this.closingTime,
    required this.status,
    required this.createdAt,
    required this.updatedAt,
    this.deletedAt,
  });

  factory Vendor.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return Vendor(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'] ?? "",
      email: json['email'] ?? "",
      phoneNumber: json['phone_number']?.toString() ?? "",
      image: ApiUrls.resolveImage(json['image']),
      location: json['location'] ?? "",
      pickupAvailable: json['pickup_available'],
      deliveryAvailable: json['delivery_available'],
      openingTime: json['opening_time']?.toString(),
      closingTime: json['closing_time']?.toString(),
      status: json['status'] ?? "unknown",
      createdAt: json['created_at'] == null
          ? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.parse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt: json['updated_at'] == null
          ? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.parse(json['updated_at']),
      deletedAt: json['deleted_at'],
    );
  }

  Map<String, dynamic> toJson() {
    return {
      'id': id,
      'name': name,
      'email': email,
      'phone_number': phoneNumber,
      'image': image,
      'location': location,
      'opening_time': openingTime,
      'closing_time': closingTime,
      'status': status,
      'created_at': createdAt.toIso8601String(),
      'updated_at': updatedAt.toIso8601String(),
      'deleted_at': deletedAt,
    };
  }
}

class VendorDetails extends Vendor {
  final List<ProductModel> products;
  final List<Review> reviews;

  VendorDetails({
    required super.id,
    required super.name,
    required super.email,
    required super.phoneNumber,
    super.image,
    required super.location,
    super.openingTime,
    super.closingTime,
    required super.status,
    required super.createdAt,
    required super.updatedAt,
    super.deletedAt,
    required this.products,
    required this.reviews,
    required super.pickupAvailable,
    required super.deliveryAvailable,
  });

  factory VendorDetails.fromJson(Map<String, dynamic> json) {
    return VendorDetails(
      id: json['id'],
      name: json['name'] ?? "",
      email: json['email'] ?? "",
      phoneNumber: json['phone_number']?.toString() ?? "",
      image: ApiUrls.resolveImage(json['image']),
      location: json['location'] ?? "",
      openingTime: json['opening_time']?.toString(),
      closingTime: json['closing_time']?.toString(),
      pickupAvailable: json['pickup_available'],
      deliveryAvailable: json['delivery_available'],
      status: json['status'] ?? "unknown",
      createdAt: json['created_at'] == null
          ? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.parse(json['created_at']),
      updatedAt: json['updated_at'] == null
          ? DateTime.now()
          : DateTime.parse(json['updated_at']),
      deletedAt: json['deleted_at'],
      products: json['products'] == null
          ? []
          : List<ProductModel>.from(
              json['products'].map((x) => ProductModel.fromJson(x)),
            ),
      reviews: json['reviews'] == null
          ? []
          : List<Review>.from(json['reviews'].map((x) => Review.fromJson(x))),
    );
  }
}
