// ignore_for_file: use_build_context_synchronously

import 'dart:async';

import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/no_internet.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/agent/agent.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/cart/cart.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/notify/notify.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/orders/orders.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/products/products.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/search/search.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/user/user.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/init_ob/stepsPage.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:connectivity_plus/connectivity_plus.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';
// ignore: depend_on_referenced_packages

enum RefreshParam { orders, products, vendors }

abstract class BaseNotifier extends ChangeNotifier with Products, Cart, Notify  {
  bool dialogueLoading = false;
  bool thmMode = false;

  Future<void> enDM(BuildContext context) async {
    thmMode = !thmMode;
    var sp = await DS.sharedStorage;
    sp.setBool("th", thmMode);

    await initTHM(context);
  }

  Future<void> initTHM(BuildContext context) async {
    var sp = await DS.sharedStorage;
    thmMode = sp.getBool("th") ?? true;
    notifyListeners();
    if (!thmMode) {
      AdaptiveTheme.of(context).setDark();
    } else {
      AdaptiveTheme.of(context).setLight();
    }
  }
}

class SharedProvider extends BaseNotifier
    with Cart, Products, Search, User, Orders, Agent {
  final ScrollController listScrollControllerP = ScrollController();
  final ScrollController listScrollControllerV = ScrollController();
  final ScrollController listScrollControllerO = ScrollController();
  bool connecting = false;
  bool refreshing_ = false;

  String connMSG = "Please check your connection and try again.";

Future<bool> hasInternetConnection(bool first, BuildContext context) async {
    connMSG = "Please check your connection and try again.";
    Timer(Duration(seconds: 15), () {
      if (connecting != false) {
        connMSG = "Failed to connect to the internet.";
      }
      connecting = false;
      notifyListeners();
    });
    connecting = true;
    notifyListeners();
    var connectivityResult = await Connectivity().checkConnectivity();

    if (connectivityResult.first.name == "none") {
      connecting = false;
      notifyListeners();
      connMSG =
          "Please 'ON' your data connection or 'CONNECT' to a Wifi for internet access";
      notifyListeners();
      first ? NavigationHelper.push(context, NoInternetPage()) : null;
      return false;
    }

    connMSG = "Connection Available";
    notifyListeners();
    first ? null : NavigationHelper.pop(context);
    await Future.delayed(Duration(seconds: 3), () {});
    connecting = false;
    notifyListeners();
    return true;
  }
  Future<void> moreData({
    required RefreshParam type,
    required BuildContext context, refresh = false
  }) async {
    if (refreshing_) return;

    switch (type) {
      case RefreshParam.orders:
        if (oNextPAGE == null && refresh == false) return;
        refreshing_ = true;
        notifyListeners();
        await getOrders(refresh: refresh, next: refresh ? false: true);

        break;
      case RefreshParam.products:
        if (pNextPAGE == null && refresh == false) return;
        refreshing_ = true;
        notifyListeners();
        await getProducts(context, refresh: refresh, next: refresh ? false: true);

        break;
      case RefreshParam.vendors:
        if (vNextPAGE == null && refresh == false) return;
        refreshing_ = true;
        notifyListeners();
        await getVendors(refresh: refresh, next: refresh ? false: true);

        break;
    }
    Timer(Duration(seconds: 2), () {
      refreshing_ ? {refreshing_ = false, notifyListeners()} : null;
    });
  }


    Future<void> checkINIT(context) async {
    final prefs = (await SharedPreferences.getInstance());


    bool ft = prefs.getBool("fts") ?? false;
    if (!ft) {

      showDialog(
        context: context,
        barrierDismissible :false,
        builder: (context) {
          return StepsPage(nit: true,);
        },
      );
    prefs.setBool("fts", true);

    }

  }

}

=================== FILE: lib\provider\mixins\agent\agent.dart ===================
// ignore_for_file: use_build_context_synchronously

import 'dart:io';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/snackbar_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/agent_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/mixins/user/user.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/vendor_onboarding/onboard_vendor_success.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/layout/layout.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:image_picker/image_picker.dart';

mixin Agent on User {
  PartnerData? partnerData;

  bool get isAgentAccount => partnerData != null;
  int get agentPoints => partnerData?.points ?? 0;

  // ---- agent signup form state (single step now — no plan picker) ----
  final agentFormKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  var agentFullNameController = TextEditingController();
  var agentPhoneController = TextEditingController();
  var agentRegionController = TextEditingController();
  var agentGhanaCardController = TextEditingController();

  File? agentGhanaCardPhoto;
  bool agentPhotoPicking = false;
  bool agentSubmitting = false;

  bool _agentPrefilled = false;

  void prefillAgentFieldsFromUser() {
    if (_agentPrefilled) return;
    if (userData?.data != null) {
      agentFullNameController.text = userData!.data.name;
      var phone = userData!.data.phone;
      agentPhoneController.text =
          phone.startsWith("0") ? phone.substring(1) : phone;
    }
    _agentPrefilled = true;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool get agentGhanaCardAlreadyLinked => userData?.data.ghanaCard != null;

  Future<void> pickGhanaCardPhoto() async {
    agentPhotoPicking = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final picked = await ImagePicker().pickImage(
        source: ImageSource.gallery,
        imageQuality: 80,
      );
      if (picked != null) {
        agentGhanaCardPhoto = File(picked.path);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    agentPhotoPicking = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void resetAgentFlow() {
    agentGhanaCardPhoto = null;
    agentGhanaCardController.clear();
    agentSubmitting = false;
    _agentPrefilled = false;
  }

  /// POST ApiUrls.partnerRegisterPST — multipart because Ghana Card
  /// photo may be attached. No plan field — every agent gets the same
  /// features.
  Future<void> submitAgentApplication(BuildContext context) async {
    if (!agentFormKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    if (!agentGhanaCardAlreadyLinked) {
      if (agentGhanaCardController.text.trim().isEmpty) {
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Ghana Card required",
          message: "Please enter your Ghana Card number to continue.",
        );
        return;
      }
      if (agentGhanaCardPhoto == null) {
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Photo required",
          message: "Please upload a clear photo of your Ghana Card.",
        );
        return;
      }
    }

    agentSubmitting = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var formData = FormData.fromMap({
        "full_name": agentFullNameController.text.trim(),
        "phone": "0${agentPhoneController.text.trim()}",
        "region": agentRegionController.text.trim(),
        if (!agentGhanaCardAlreadyLinked) ...{
          "ghana_card_number": agentGhanaCardController.text.trim(),
          if (agentGhanaCardPhoto != null)
            "ghana_card_photo":
                await MultipartFile.fromFile(agentGhanaCardPhoto!.path),
        },
      });

      debugPrint("=== [submitAgentApplication] REQUEST ===");
      debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.partnerRegisterPST}");
      debugPrint("==========================================");

      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.partnerRegisterPST,
        data: formData,
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      debugPrint("=== [submitAgentApplication] RESPONSE ===");
      debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
      debugPrint("===========================================");

      agentSubmitting = false;

      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        partnerData = PartnerData.fromJson(response.data);
        notifyListeners();

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "You're in!",
          message: "Your agent application has been received.",
        );

        NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, HomePage());
        resetAgentFlow();
      } else {
        String message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        }
        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Application Failed",
          message: message,
        );
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      agentSubmitting = false;
      notifyListeners();
      debugPrint("!!! [submitAgentApplication] DioException: ${e.message}");
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Application Failed",
        message: e.response?.data is Map
            ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
            : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    }
  }

  /// GET ApiUrls.partnerProfileGET
  Future<void> getPartnerProfile() async {
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        ApiUrls.partnerProfileGET,
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );
      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        partnerData = PartnerData.fromJson(response.data);
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ---- vendor onboarding form state ----
  final vendorFormKey = GlobalKey<FormState>();

  var vendorShopNameController = TextEditingController();
  var vendorOwnerNameController = TextEditingController();
  var vendorPhoneController = TextEditingController();
  var vendorAddressController = TextEditingController();
  var vendorEstimatedDevicesController = TextEditingController();

  File? vendorShopfrontPhoto;
  bool vendorPhotoPicking = false;
  bool vendorSubmitting = false;
  VendorOnboarding? lastVendorSubmission;
  List<VendorOnboarding> agentVendors = [];

  Future<void> pickVendorShopfrontPhoto() async {
    vendorPhotoPicking = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      final picked = await ImagePicker().pickImage(
        source: ImageSource.gallery,
        imageQuality: 80,
      );
      if (picked != null) {
        vendorShopfrontPhoto = File(picked.path);
      }
    } catch (_) {}
    vendorPhotoPicking = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void resetVendorForm() {
    vendorShopNameController.clear();
    vendorOwnerNameController.clear();
    vendorPhoneController.clear();
    vendorAddressController.clear();
    vendorEstimatedDevicesController.clear();
    vendorShopfrontPhoto = null;
    vendorSubmitting = false;
  }

  /// POST ApiUrls.partnerOnboardVendorPST
  Future<void> submitVendorOnboarding(BuildContext context) async {
    if (!vendorFormKey.currentState!.validate()) return;

    vendorSubmitting = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var formData = FormData.fromMap({
        "shop_name": vendorShopNameController.text.trim(),
        "owner_name": vendorOwnerNameController.text.trim(),
        "owner_phone": "0${vendorPhoneController.text.trim()}",
        "business_address": vendorAddressController.text.trim(),
        "estimated_monthly_devices":
            int.tryParse(vendorEstimatedDevicesController.text.trim()),
        if (vendorShopfrontPhoto != null)
          "shopfront_photo":
              await MultipartFile.fromFile(vendorShopfrontPhoto!.path),
      });

      debugPrint("=== [submitVendorOnboarding] REQUEST ===");
      debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.partnerOnboardVendorPST}");
      debugPrint("==========================================");

      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.partnerOnboardVendorPST,
        data: formData,
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      debugPrint("=== [submitVendorOnboarding] RESPONSE ===");
      debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
      debugPrint("===========================================");

      vendorSubmitting = false;

      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        lastVendorSubmission = VendorOnboarding.fromJson(
          response.data is Map && response.data["data"] != null
              ? response.data["data"]
              : response.data,
        );
        await getPartnerProfile();

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        NavigationHelper.pushReplacement(
          context,
          const OnboardVendorSuccess(),
        );
        resetVendorForm();
      } else {
        String message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        }
        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Failed To Submit Vendor",
          message: message,
        );
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      vendorSubmitting = false;
      notifyListeners();
      debugPrint("!!! [submitVendorOnboarding] DioException: ${e.message}");
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed To Submit Vendor",
        message: e.response?.data is Map
            ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
            : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    }
  }

  /// GET ApiUrls.partnerVendorsGET
  Future<void> getAgentVendors() async {
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        ApiUrls.partnerVendorsGET,
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );
      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        final list = response.data is Map && response.data["data"] != null
            ? response.data["data"]
            : response.data;
        agentVendors = List<VendorOnboarding>.from(
          (list as List).map((x) => VendorOnboarding.fromJson(x)),
        );
        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  // ---- wallet ----
  PartnerWallet? partnerWallet;
  bool loadingWallet = false;

  /// GET ApiUrls.partnerWalletGET
  Future<void> getPartnerWallet() async {
    loadingWallet = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        ApiUrls.partnerWalletGET,
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );
      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        partnerWallet = PartnerWallet.fromJson(response.data);
      }
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      loadingWallet = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  bool walletWithdrawLoading = false;

  /// POST ApiUrls.partnerWalletWithdrawPST
  Future<void> withdrawFromWallet(
    double amount,
    String momoNumber,
    BuildContext context,
  ) async {
    walletWithdrawLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.partnerWalletWithdrawPST,
        data: {"amount": amount, "momo_number": momoNumber},
        options: Options(
          headers: {'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}'},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      walletWithdrawLoading = false;

      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        await getPartnerWallet();
        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "Withdrawal Requested",
          message: "Your payout is on its way to $momoNumber.",
        );
      } else {
        String message = "Withdrawal failed. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        }
        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Withdrawal Failed",
          message: message,
        );
      }
      notifyListeners();
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      walletWithdrawLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Withdrawal Failed",
        message: e.response?.data is Map
            ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
            : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    }
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\provider\mixins\cart\cart.dart ===================
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:flexiphones/models/price_model.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/product_m.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

mixin Cart on ChangeNotifier {

  List cartItems = [];
  List<int> cartsToRemove = [];
  bool selectAll = false;
  int get selectedCount =>
      cartItems.isEmpty
          ? 0
          : cartItems.where((item) => item["isSelected"] == true).length;

  bool get checked =>
      cartItems.isEmpty
          ? false
          : cartItems.where((x) => x["isSelected"] == true).isNotEmpty;
  List get selectedItems =>
      cartItems.where((x) => x["isSelected"] == true).toList();


  // double get totalCost => cartItems.fold(
  //   0.0,
  //   (total, item) =>
  //       item["isSelected"] ? total + item["price"] * item["quantity"] : total,
  // );
  int get deviceUnit => cartItems.fold(
    0,
    (total, item) =>
        item["isSelected"] ? total + (item["quantity"] as int) : total,
  );

  /// THE FIX (typing): `breakDownPrice` is now explicitly typed as
  /// `BreakdownResponse?` instead of implicit `dynamic` — see earlier fix
  /// for why (a Map was previously passed here by mistake and it wasn't
  /// caught until runtime).
  ///
  /// THE FIX (null vendor): `v.vendor!.name` used to force-unwrap the
  /// product's vendor. The backend can legitimately return a product with
  /// no vendor attached, which crashed every add-to-cart attempt for that
  /// product with "Null check operator used on a null value". Falls back
  /// to a placeholder label instead of crashing.
  Future<void> addToCart(
    ProductModel v,
    ProductStorage ps,
    int tenure,
    String color,
    int? packageId,
    BreakdownResponse? breakDownPrice,
  ) async {
    if (breakDownPrice == null) {
      // Pricing hasn't loaded for the selected storage/tenure yet — bail
      // out instead of crashing. The UI should ideally disable the confirm
      // button until this is non-null (see StorageOMD's SmartButton).
      return;
    }

    dynamic newProduct = {
      'id': v.id,
      "vendor": v.vendor?.name ?? "Unknown Vendor",
      "productName": v.fullName,
      "image": v.image,
      "product_storages": ps.toJson(),
      "service_tenure": tenure,
      "device_type": "smartphone",
      "color": color,
      "quantity": 1,
      "isSelected": true,
      "package_id": packageId,
      "breakdowns": breakDownPrice.breakdowns.toJson(),
    };

    cartItems.add(newProduct);
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> removeCartItem(int value) async {
    cartItems.removeAt(value);
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> updateCartItem(int index, bool isIncrement) async {
    if (isIncrement) {
      cartItems[index]["quantity"]++;
    } else {
      if (cartItems[index]["quantity"] > 1) {
        cartItems[index]["quantity"]--;
      }
    }
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> selectCartItem(index) async {
    cartItems[index]["isSelected"] = !cartItems[index]["isSelected"];
    if (cartItems[index]["isSelected"] == true) {
      cartsToRemove.contains(index) ? null : cartsToRemove.add(index);
    } else {
      cartsToRemove.remove(index);
    }
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> selectAllCartItem() async {
    selectAll = !selectAll;

    cartsToRemove.clear();

    for (int i = 0; i < cartItems.length; i++) {
      cartItems[i]["isSelected"] = selectAll;
      if (cartItems[i]["isSelected"]) {
        if (!cartsToRemove.contains(i)) {
          cartsToRemove.add(i);
        }
      } else {
        cartsToRemove.remove(i);
      }
    }
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> removeSelectedItem() async {
    for (int value in cartsToRemove) {
      cartItems.removeAt(value);
    }
      
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> removeSelectedItems() async {
    for (int i = cartItems.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (cartItems[i]["isSelected"] == true) {
        cartItems.removeAt(i);
      }
    }
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  

  Future<void> clearCart() async {
    cartItems.clear();
    await saveCartItems();
  }

  Future<void> saveCartItems() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final encoded = jsonEncode(cartItems);
    await prefs.setString('cart_items', encoded);

    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> loadCartItems() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final data = prefs.getString('cart_items');
    if (data != null) {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(data);
      if (decoded is List) {
        cartItems = decoded;
      }
    }

    notifyListeners();
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\provider\mixins\orders\orders.dart ===================
// ignore_for_file: use_build_context_synchronously

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/snackbar_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/order_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/packages.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/repayment_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/order_confirmation/sub_pages/order_success.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/layout/layout.dart';
import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

mixin Orders on BaseNotifier {
  List<Order> orders = [];
  List<RepaymentEntry> repaymentCL = [];

  DateTime selectedData = DateTime.now();
  bool loadingOrder = false;
  int showP = 0;
  //
  String? oNextPAGE;
  String? oPrevPAGE;
  String? rNextPAGE;
  String? rPrevPAGE;
  bool orderLoading = false;

  List<Order> get ordersStatusPlaced =>
      orders
          .where((element) => element.orderStatus.toLowerCase() == "placed")
          .toList();
  List<Order> get ordersStatusCancelled =>
      orders
          .where((element) => element.orderStatus.toLowerCase() == "cancelled")
          .toList();

  List get ordersStatusDelivered =>
      orders
          .where((element) => element.orderStatus.toLowerCase() == "delivered")
          .toList();

  Future<void> selectDate(DateTime v) async {
    selectedData = v;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> switchOrders(int v) async {
    showP = v;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// FIX + DEBUG LOGGING:
  /// - Wrapped in try/catch so a DioException (timeout, connection error,
  ///   or an upstream failure like get-customer-orders/{phoneNumber}
  ///   cascading) doesn't crash silently — it now surfaces a Snackbar and
  ///   a console log instead.
  /// - `orderLoading` is reset to false BEFORE navigation, and in a
  ///   `finally` block, so the button never gets stuck.
  /// - `context.mounted` checked after every await before touching context.
  /// - Full request/response logging so you can see exactly what's sent
  ///   for a multi-device order and exactly what the backend returns.
  Future<void> addOrder(
    List products,
    String location,
    bool pickup,
    BuildContext context,
  ) async {
    orderLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    var data = {
      "products": products,
      "delivery_option": "pickup", // pickup ? "pickup" : "delivery",
      "delivery_address": location,
    };

    debugPrint("=== [addOrder] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.addOrders}");
    debugPrint("Item count: ${products.length}");
    debugPrint("Data: ${jsonEncode(data)}");
    debugPrint("Token present: ${userData?.token != null && userData!.token!.isNotEmpty}");
    debugPrint("===========================");

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.addOrders,
        data: data,
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      debugPrint("=== [addOrder] RESPONSE ===");
      debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
      debugPrint("============================");

      orderLoading = false;

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        debugPrint(">>> [addOrder] SUCCESS — refreshing order list");
        try {
          await getOrders(refresh: true, next: false);
        } catch (e) {
          // Order already succeeded — don't let a refresh failure
          // (e.g. get-customer-orders lookup) block the success screen.
          debugPrint("!!! [addOrder] getOrders() refresh failed after successful order: $e");
        }

        await removeSelectedItems();

        if (!context.mounted) {
          debugPrint("!!! [addOrder] context unmounted before navigation, aborting nav");
          return;
        }
        NavigationHelper.push(context, OrderPlacedSuccess());
      } else {
        String message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        } else if (body is String && body.isNotEmpty) {
          message = body;
        }

        debugPrint(">>> [addOrder] FAILED — status ${response.statusCode}, message: $message");

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Failed To Create Order",
          message: message,
        );
      }
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint("!!! [addOrder] DioException");
      debugPrint("!!! URL: ${e.requestOptions.uri}");
      debugPrint("!!! Status: ${e.response?.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("!!! Body: ${e.response?.data}");
      debugPrint("!!! Message: ${e.message}");

      orderLoading = false;
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed To Create Order",
        message: e.response?.data is Map
            ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
            : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    } finally {
      orderLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> removeOrder(
    dynamic id,
    BuildContext context, {
    bool allOrder = false,
  }) async {
    dialogueLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    debugPrint("=== [removeOrder] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.cancelOrder}");
    debugPrint("Data: ${allOrder ? {"application_uuid": id} : {"application_id": id}}");
    debugPrint("==============================");

    var response = await dio.post(
      ApiUrls.cancelOrder,
      data: allOrder ? {"application_uuid": id} : {"application_id": id},
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    debugPrint("=== [removeOrder] RESPONSE ===");
    debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
    debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
    debugPrint("===============================");

    await getOrders(refresh: true, next: false);
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      SnackbarHelper.success(
        context: context,
        title: "Order Canceled",
        message: "You've cancelled your order(s) successfully",
      );
      NavigationHelper.push(context, HomePage(initScreen: 3));
    } else {
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Order Failed To Canceled",
        message: "Failed to process order request",
      );
      NavigationHelper.pop(context);
    }
    //
    dialogueLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> getOrders({required bool refresh, required bool next}) async {
    if (next && oNextPAGE == null) {
      return;
    }
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();

    final url = next ? "${ApiUrls.getOrders}?cursor=$oNextPAGE" : ApiUrls.getOrders;

    debugPrint("=== [getOrders] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: $url");
    debugPrint("============================");

    var response = await dio.get(
      url,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    debugPrint("=== [getOrders] RESPONSE ===");
    debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
    debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
    debugPrint("=============================");

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      if (refresh) {
        orders = OrderResponse.fromJson(response.data).orders;
      } else {
        orders.addAll(OrderResponse.fromJson(response.data).orders);
      }
      orders.sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.compareTo(b.createdAt));
      prefs.setString("orders", jsonEncode(response.data));
      oNextPAGE = response.data['orders']["next_cursor"];
      oPrevPAGE = response.data['orders']["prev_cursor"];
    } else {
      debugPrint("!!! [getOrders] FAILED with status ${response.statusCode}");
    }

    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> getRepaymentCL({
    required bool refresh,
    required bool next,
  }) async {
    if (next && rNextPAGE == null) {
      return;
    }
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();

    final url = next
        ? "${ApiUrls.repaymentCL}? cursor=$rNextPAGE"
        : ApiUrls.repaymentCL;

    debugPrint("=== [getRepaymentCL] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: $url");
    debugPrint("=================================");

    var response = await dio.get(
      url,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    debugPrint("=== [getRepaymentCL] RESPONSE ===");
    debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
    debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
    debugPrint("==================================");

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      var nw =
          RepaymentHistoryResponse.fromJson(
            response.data,
          ).repaymentHistory.data;
      if (refresh) {
        repaymentCL = nw;
      } else {
        repaymentCL.addAll(nw);
      }
      prefs.setString("repayments", jsonEncode(response.data));
      rNextPAGE = response.data['repayment_history']["next_cursor"];
      rPrevPAGE = response.data['repayment_history']["prev_cursor"];
    }

    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> getOrder(int id) async {
    loadingOrder = true;
    notifyListeners();

    final url = "${ApiUrls.getOrder}/$id";
    debugPrint("=== [getOrder] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: $url");
    debugPrint("===========================");

    var response = await dio.get(
      url,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    debugPrint("=== [getOrder] RESPONSE ===");
    debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
    debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
    debugPrint("============================");

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      orderDataPackages =
          response.data["orders"]["partner_package"] == null
              ? null
              : Package.fromJson(response.data["orders"]["partner_package"]);

      orderData = Order.fromJson(response.data["orders"]);
    }
    loadingOrder = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\provider\mixins\products\products.dart ===================
// ignore_for_file: use_build_context_synchronously

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:dio/dio.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/api_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/snackbar_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/hot_sales.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/notifications_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/order_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/packages.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/price_model.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/product_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/top_deals_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/user_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/vendors.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/init_screen.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:shared_preferences/shared_preferences.dart';

mixin Products on ChangeNotifier {
  Dio dio = Dio();
  LoginResponse? userData;
  Order? orderData;
  Package? orderDataPackages;
  BreakdownResponse? breakDownPrice;
  ProductStorage? selectedStorage;
  List<ProductModel> products = [];
  List<ProductModel> recentlyViewedProducts = [];

  List<Vendor> vendors = [];
  List<Deal> topDeals = [];
  List<HotDeal> hotDeals = [];

  List<NotificationData> notifications = [];
  List<NotificationData> get unreadNTF => notifications
      .where((element) => element.status.toLowerCase() == "unread")
      .toList();
  List<NotificationData> get readNTF => notifications
      .where((element) => element.status.toLowerCase() != "unread")
      .toList();

  ProductModel? productData;
  Package? productDataPackages;
  VendorDetails? vendorData;
  bool productsVariations = false;
  bool loadingProduct = true;
  bool loadingVendor = false;
  bool loadingPBD = false;
  int slTenure = 3;

  /// Surfaced to the UI so a genuinely failed fetch can show something
  /// other than an endless shimmer.
  String? productDetailsError;

  String? pNextPAGE;
  String? vNextPAGE;
  String? tdNextPAGE;
  String? pPrevPAGE;
  String? vPrevPAGE;
  String? tdPrevPAGE;

  var commentController = TextEditingController();

  String colorSL = "";
  void selectColor(String t) {
    colorSL = t;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void selectTenure(int t) {
    slTenure = t;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void selectStorage(ProductStorage stg) async {
    await getProductPB(stg.id);
    selectedStorage = stg;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void togglePV() {
    productsVariations = !productsVariations;
  }

  Future<void> getProducts(
    BuildContext context, {
    required bool refresh,
    required bool next,
  }) async {
    bool ofl = userData == null;
    if (next && pNextPAGE == null) {
      return;
    }

    var response = await dio.get(
      next
          ? "${ofl ? ApiUrls.getProductsOFF : ApiUrls.getProducts}?cursor=$pNextPAGE"
          : (ofl ? ApiUrls.getProductsOFF : ApiUrls.getProducts),
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    if (response.data.toString().toLowerCase().contains("html")) {
      await DS.storage.deleteAll();
      await (await SharedPreferences.getInstance()).clear();
      NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, InitScreen());
      return;
    }

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      var newD = ProductResponseModel.fromJson(response.data).products!;
      if (refresh) {
        products = newD.data;
      } else {
        products.addAll(newD.data);
      }
      DS.storage.write(key: "products", value: '');
      pNextPAGE = newD.nextCursor;
      pPrevPAGE = newD.prevCursor;
    }
  }

  Future<void> getVendors({required bool refresh, required bool next}) async {
    if (next && vNextPAGE == null) {
      return;
    }
    var response = await dio.get(
      next ? "${ApiUrls.getVendors}?cursor=$vNextPAGE" : ApiUrls.getVendors,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      var newV = VendorsResponse.fromJson(response.data).vendors;
      if (refresh) {
        vendors = newV.data;
      } else {
        vendors.addAll(newV.data);
      }
      vNextPAGE = newV.nextCursor;
      vPrevPAGE = newV.prevCursor;
      DS.storage.write(key: "vendors", value: '');
    }
  }

  Future<void> getTopDeals({required bool refresh, required bool next}) async {
    bool ofl = userData == null;
    if (next && tdNextPAGE == null) {
      return;
    }
    var response = await dio.get(
      next
          ? "${ofl ? ApiUrls.topDealsOFF : ApiUrls.topDeals}?cursor=$tdNextPAGE"
          : (ofl ? ApiUrls.topDealsOFF : ApiUrls.topDeals),
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      var newV = TopDealsResponse.fromJson(response.data).topDeals;
      if (refresh) {
        topDeals = newV.data;
      } else {
        topDeals.addAll(newV.data);
      }
      tdNextPAGE = newV.nextCursor;
      tdPrevPAGE = newV.prevCursor;
      DS.storage.write(key: "topDeals", value: '');
    }
  }

  Future<void> getHotDeals() async {
    bool ofl = userData == null;
    var response = await dio.get(
      ofl ? ApiUrls.hotDealsOFF : ApiUrls.hotDeals,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      hotDeals = PromotedProductsResponse.fromJson(response.data).hotDeals;
      DS.storage.write(key: "hotDeals", value: '');
    }
  }

  /// THE FIX: previously any exception thrown while parsing the response
  /// (e.g. a null `opening_time`, a null `packages`, an empty
  /// `productStorages`) propagated straight out of this function BEFORE
  /// `loadingProduct = false` ran — so the UI stayed on its loading shimmer
  /// forever with no error, no crash log the user would see, nothing.
  /// Wrapping the parse in try/catch/finally guarantees loadingProduct
  /// always resolves, and a real failure now surfaces as a message instead
  /// of an infinite spinner.
  Future<void> getProductDetails(int id, BuildContext context) async {
    bool ofl = userData == null;
    loadingProduct = true;
    productDetailsError = null;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        ofl
            ? "${ApiUrls.getProductsDetailsOFF}/$id"
            : "${ApiUrls.getProductsDetails}/$id",
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      if (response.data.toString().toLowerCase().contains("<html")) {
        await DS.storage.deleteAll();
        await (await SharedPreferences.getInstance()).clear();
        NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, InitScreen());
        return;
      }

      if (response.statusCode == 200 &&
          response.data is Map &&
          response.data["product"] != null) {
        productDataPackages = response.data["packages"] == null
            ? null
            : Package.fromJson(response.data["packages"]);

        productData = ProductModel.fromJson(response.data["product"]);

        final storages = productData?.productStorages;
        selectedStorage = (storages != null && storages.isNotEmpty)
            ? storages.first
            : null;

        addRecentViewed(productData!);
      } else {
        productDetailsError =
            (response.data is Map ? response.data["message"] : null) ??
                "Failed to load product (status ${response.statusCode})";
      }
    } catch (e) {
      productDetailsError = "Something went wrong loading this product: $e";
    } finally {
      loadingProduct = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> getVendorDetails(int id) async {
    loadingVendor = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        "${ApiUrls.getVendorDetails}/$id",
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        vendorData = VendorDetails.fromJson(response.data["vendor"]);
      }
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      loadingVendor = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> rateProduct(int rate, BuildContext context) async {
    var data = {
      "product_id": orderData!.product.id,
      "rating": rate.toString(),
      "comment": commentController.text.trim(),
    };
    var response = await dio.post(
      ApiUrls.rateProduct,
      data: data,
      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
      SnackbarHelper.success(
        context: context,
        title: "Product Rated!",
        message: "Your review has been added successfully",
      );
      commentController.clear();
      NavigationHelper.pop(context);
    } else {
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed To Rate Product",
        message: "Unable to add review",
      );
    }
  }

  Future<void> getProductPB(id) async {
    loadingPBD = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        "${ApiUrls.priceBrakeDown}/$id",
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        breakDownPrice = BreakdownResponse.fromJson(response.data);
      }
    } catch (_) {
    } finally {
      loadingPBD = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> addRecentViewed(ProductModel p) async {
    recentlyViewedProducts.removeWhere((x) => x.id == p.id);
    var mpData = p.toJson();
    mpData["addedAt"] = DateTime.now().toIso8601String();
    recentlyViewedProducts.insert(0, ProductModel.fromJson(mpData));
    var data = jsonEncode(
      recentlyViewedProducts.take(20).map((x) => x.toJson()).toList(),
    );
    var str = await DS.sharedStorage;
    str.setString("recent_viewed", data);
    await getRecentViewed();
  }

  Future<void> getRecentViewed() async {
    var str = await DS.sharedStorage;
    try {
      recentlyViewedProducts =
          (jsonDecode(str.getString("recent_viewed") ?? "[]") as List)
              .map((x) => ProductModel.fromJson(x))
              .toList();
    } catch (_) {
      recentlyViewedProducts = [];
    }
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

  Future<void> getUserDetails() async {

        (await DS.storage.read(key: "user")) != null
            ? LoginResponse.fromJson(rtDATA)

  Future<void> phoneSignin(BuildContext context) async {
    userUDLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    await Future.delayed(Duration(seconds: 2));

    if (phoneController.text.isValidPhoneNumber) {
      phoneInValid = false;
      notifyListeners();
      NavigationHelper.push(
        context,
        OTPScreen(
          email:
              '$countryCode ${phoneController.text.trim()}',
          su: true, 
        ),
      );
    } else {
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Invalid Phone Number",
        message: "Please enter a valid phone number excluding the first zero.",
      );
      phoneInValid = true;
      notifyListeners();
    }

    userUDLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> emailSignIn(BuildContext context) async {
    await DS.storage.deleteAll();
    userUDLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    var response = await dio.post(
      ApiUrls.loginPST,
      data: {
        "email": emailEditingController.text.trim(),
        "password": passwordController.text.trim(),
      },
      options: Options(
        headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      await DS.storage.write(key: "user", value: jsonEncode(response.data));
      await getUserDetails();

      NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, InitScreen());

      Timer(Duration(seconds: 3), () {
        emailEditingController.clear();
        passwordController.clear();
        rePasswordController.clear();
        fullNameController.clear();
        locationController.clear();
        phoneController.clear();
      });
    } else {
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed to sign in",
        message: "${response.data["message"]}",
      );
    }

    userUDLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> verifyEmail(BuildContext context, ColorScheme ch) async {
    emailFocusNode.unfocus();
    if (!emailEditingController.text.isValidEmail) {
      emailEditingController.text.isEmpty
          ? emailFocusNode.requestFocus()
          : Notify.invalidEmail(context);

      return;
    }
    otpEditingController.clear();
    emailVerification = true;
    notifyListeners();

    // try {
    var response = await dio.post(
      ApiUrls.emailVerificationPST,
      data: {"email": emailEditingController.text.trim()},
      options: Options(
        headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );
    emailVerification = false;
    notifyListeners();
    if (response.statusCode == 200) {
      Notify.validEmail(context, ch);
      emailVerification = false;
      notifyListeners();
      NavigationHelper.push(
        context,
        OTPScreen(email: emailEditingController.text, su: true),
      );
    } else {
      SnackbarHelper.info(
        context: context,
        title: "Email Registered",
        message: "${response.data["message"]}",
        ch: ch,
      );
    }
    // } catch (e) {
    //   //print("MIKE88 $e");
    //   // emailEditingController.clear();

    //   NavigationHelper.push(context, EmailSignin());
    // }
  }

  Future<void> verifyOTP(BuildContext context, {required bool su}) async {
    otpLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.otpVerificationPST,
        data: {
          "email": emailEditingController.text.trim(),
          "otp": otpEditingController.text,
        },
        options: Options(
          headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        validOTP = true;
        notifyListeners();
        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "OTP Verified!",
          message: "Email verified successfully.",
        );

        if (su) {
          NavigationHelper.push(context, UserCredentialPage());
        } else {
          NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, ResetPassword());
        }
      } else {
        validOTP = false;
        notifyListeners();
        HapticFeedback.vibrate();
        Notify.invalidOTP(context);
      }

      otpLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    } catch (e) {
      otpLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
    }
  }

  Future<void> finalRegistration(BuildContext context) async {
    userUDLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    Response response;

    if (formKey_2.currentState!.validate()) {
      response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.finalRegistrationPST,
        data: {
          "email": emailEditingController.text.trim(),
          "name": fullNameController.text.trim(),
          "phone": "0${phoneController.text.trim()}",
          "address": locationController.text.trim(),
          "password": passwordController.text.trim(),
          "password_confirmation": rePasswordController.text.trim(),
        },
        options: Options(
          headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"},
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );
      userUDLoading = false;
      notifyListeners();
      if (response.statusCode == 200 || response.statusCode == 201) {
        await DS.storage.write(key: "user", value: jsonEncode(response.data));

        NavigationHelper.push(context, GHCardVerification());

        Timer(Duration(seconds: 3), () {
          emailEditingController.clear();
          passwordController.clear();
          rePasswordController.clear();
          fullNameController.clear();
          locationController.clear();
          phoneController.clear();
        });

        return;
      }

      if (response.data["message"].contains("already completed")) {
        await getUserDetails();
        NavigationHelper.push(context, EmailSignin());
        userUDLoading = false;
        notifyListeners();
        return;
      }
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Registration Failed",
        message: "${response.data["message"]}",
      );
    }

    userUDLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> validateGHC(BuildContext context) async {
    ghCardVrLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        "${ApiUrls.updateGHCard}${userData!.data.id}",
        data: {
          "ghana_card":
              "GHA-${ghCardEditingController.text.trim()}-${ghCardEditingController2.text.trim()}",
        },
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
        ),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        var newData = {
          "status": response.data["status"],
          "message": response.data["message"],
          "token": userData?.token,
          "data": response.data["data"],
        };

        await DS.storage.write(key: "user", value: jsonEncode(newData));
        await getUserDetails();

        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "Ghana Card Updated",
          message: "You've successfully verified your ghana card",
        );

        NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, VerificationSuccess());
      }
    } catch (_) {}

    ghCardVrLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// FIX:
  /// - No longer assumes response.data["data"] exists in exactly that
  ///   shape. Some endpoints wrap the updated record under "data",
  ///   some under "customer", some return it flat at the top level.
  ///   We try each in order; if none match, we still know the update
  ///   itself succeeded (status 200), so we patch the name locally
  ///   from what we sent rather than silently failing.
  /// - Real try/catch branches (DioException + generic) instead of a
  ///   bare `catch (_) {}` — every failure path now shows a Snackbar
  ///   and logs to console, so this can never again look like "nothing
  ///   happened" when it actually errored.
  /// - Pops the dialog itself on success instead of stacking a whole
  ///   separate InitScreen splash/reload flow on top of an open modal.
  Future<void> updateName(BuildContext context) async {
    dialogueLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    debugPrint("=== [updateName] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.updateUSER}");
    debugPrint("Data: {name: ${fullNameController.text.trim()}}");
    debugPrint("=============================");

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.updateUSER,
        data: {"name": fullNameController.text.trim()},
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      debugPrint("=== [updateName] RESPONSE ===");
      debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
      debugPrint("==============================");

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        Map<String, dynamic>? updated;
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          if (body["data"] is Map<String, dynamic>) {
            updated = body["data"];
          } else if (body["customer"] is Map<String, dynamic>) {
            updated = body["customer"];
          } else if (body["name"] != null) {
            updated = body;
          }
        }

        if (updated == null) {
          debugPrint(
            "!!! [updateName] 200 OK but couldn't find the updated user object "
            "in the response body — falling back to a local name patch.",
          );
          updated = userData!.data.toJson();
          updated["name"] = fullNameController.text.trim();
        }

        var newData = {
          "status": userData!.status,
          "message": userData!.message,
          "token": userData?.token,
          "data": updated,
        };

        await DS.storage.write(key: "user", value: jsonEncode(newData));
        await getUserDetails();
        fullNameController.clear();

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        Navigator.of(context).pop();

        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "Profile updated",
          message: "Your Name has been updated successfully.",
        );
      } else {
        String message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        }
        debugPrint(
          ">>> [updateName] FAILED — status ${response.statusCode}, message: $message",
        );

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Failed Update",
          message: message,
        );
      }
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint("!!! [updateName] DioException");
      debugPrint("!!! URL: ${e.requestOptions.uri}");
      debugPrint("!!! Status: ${e.response?.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("!!! Body: ${e.response?.data}");
      debugPrint("!!! Message: ${e.message}");

      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed Update",
        message:
            e.response?.data is Map
                ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
                : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("!!! [updateName] Unexpected error: $e");
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed Update",
        message: "Something went wrong. Please try again.",
      );
    }

    dialogueLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Same shape as [updateName] above, for the phone number field.
  /// Sends the number the same way finalRegistration does — a leading
  /// "0" instead of the "+233" country code — since that's the format
  /// the backend already accepts elsewhere in this app.
  Future<void> updatePhone(BuildContext context) async {
    dialogueLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();

    debugPrint("=== [updatePhone] REQUEST ===");
    debugPrint("URL: ${ApiUrls.updateUSER}");
    debugPrint("Data: {phone: 0${phoneController.text.trim()}}");
    debugPrint("==============================");

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.updateUSER,
        data: {"phone": "0${phoneController.text.trim()}"},
        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
          validateStatus: (status) => true,
        ),
      );

      debugPrint("=== [updatePhone] RESPONSE ===");
      debugPrint("Status: ${response.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("Body: ${response.data}");
      debugPrint("===============================");

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        Map<String, dynamic>? updated;
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map<String, dynamic>) {
          if (body["data"] is Map<String, dynamic>) {
            updated = body["data"];
          } else if (body["customer"] is Map<String, dynamic>) {
            updated = body["customer"];
          } else if (body["phone"] != null) {
            updated = body;
          }
        }

        if (updated == null) {
          debugPrint(
            "!!! [updatePhone] 200 OK but couldn't find the updated user object "
            "in the response body — falling back to a local phone patch.",
          );
          updated = userData!.data.toJson();
          updated["phone"] = "0${phoneController.text.trim()}";
        }

        var newData = {
          "status": userData!.status,
          "message": userData!.message,
          "token": userData?.token,
          "data": updated,
        };

        await DS.storage.write(key: "user", value: jsonEncode(newData));
        await getUserDetails();
        phoneController.clear();
        phoneInValid = false;

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        Navigator.of(context).pop();

        SnackbarHelper.success(
          context: context,
          title: "Profile updated",
          message: "Your phone number has been updated successfully.",
        );
      } else {
        String message = "Something went wrong. Please try again.";
        final body = response.data;
        if (body is Map && body["message"] != null) {
          message = "${body["message"]}";
        }
        debugPrint(
          ">>> [updatePhone] FAILED — status ${response.statusCode}, message: $message",
        );

        if (!context.mounted) return;
        SnackbarHelper.danger(
          context: context,
          title: "Failed Update",
          message: message,
        );
      }
    } on DioException catch (e) {
      debugPrint("!!! [updatePhone] DioException");
      debugPrint("!!! URL: ${e.requestOptions.uri}");
      debugPrint("!!! Status: ${e.response?.statusCode}");
      debugPrint("!!! Body: ${e.response?.data}");
      debugPrint("!!! Message: ${e.message}");

      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed Update",
        message:
            e.response?.data is Map
                ? "${e.response?.data["message"] ?? e.message}"
                : "${e.message ?? "Network error, please try again."}",
      );
    } catch (e) {
      debugPrint("!!! [updatePhone] Unexpected error: $e");
      if (!context.mounted) return;
      SnackbarHelper.danger(
        context: context,
        title: "Failed Update",
        message: "Something went wrong. Please try again.",
      );
    }

    dialogueLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> checkValidGhCard() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    validGhCard = prefs.getBool("gh-card-verified") ?? false;
  }

  Future<void> forgottenPassword(BuildContext context, ColorScheme ch) async {
    emailFocusNode.unfocus();
    if (!emailEditingController.text.isValidEmail) {
      emailEditingController.text.isEmpty
          ? emailFocusNode.requestFocus()
          : Notify.invalidEmail(context);

      return;
    }
    otpEditingController.clear();
    emailVerification = true;
    notifyListeners();

    try {
      var response = await dio.post(
        ApiUrls.forgotPasswordPST,
        data: {"email": emailEditingController.text.trim()},
        options: Options(headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"}),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        Notify.validEmail(context, ch);
        emailVerification = false;
        notifyListeners();

        NavigationHelper.push(
          context,
          OTPScreen(email: emailEditingController.text, su: false),
        );
      }
    } catch (e) {
      emailVerification = false;
      notifyListeners();

      Notify.invalidEmail(context);
    }
  }

  Future<void> resetPassword(BuildContext context, ColorScheme ch) async {
    userUDLoading = true;
    notifyListeners();
    if (formKey_3.currentState!.validate()) {
      try {
        var response = await dio.post(
          ApiUrls.resetPasswordPST,
          data: {
            "email": emailEditingController.text.trim(),
            "password": passwordController.text.trim(),
            "password_confirmation": rePasswordController.text.trim(),
          },
          options: Options(headers: {"Content-Type": "application/json"}),
        );

        if (response.statusCode == 200) {
          SnackbarHelper.success(
            context: context,
            title: "Password Changed!",
            message: "You've successfully reset your password.",
          );
          userUDLoading = false;
          notifyListeners();
          userData == null
              ? NavigationHelper.push(context, EmailSignin())
              : NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(
                context,
                HomePage(initScreen: 4),
              );
          Timer(Duration(seconds: 3), () {
            passwordController.clear();
            rePasswordController.clear();
          });
        }
      } catch (_) {}
    }
    userUDLoading = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  Future<void> getNotifications() async {
    try {
      var response = await dio.get(
        ApiUrls.userNotifications,

        options: Options(
          headers: {
            "Content-Type": "application/json",
            'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
          },
        ),
      );

      if (response.statusCode == 200) {
        notifications = NotificationResponse.fromJson(response.data).data;

        for (var dt in unreadNTF) {
          if (DateTime.parse(dt.createdAt).isWithinLast30SecondsOrFuture() &&
              !notified.contains(dt.id)) {
            notified.add(dt.id);

            await showLocalNotification(
              id: dt.id.hashCode,
              title: dt.data.title,
              body: "${dt.data.message.split(" ").take(2).join(" ")}...",
            );
          }
        }

        notifyListeners();
      }
    } catch (_) {}
  }

  Future<void> readNotification(uid) async {
    var res = await dio.patch(
      "${ApiUrls.userNotifications}/$uid/read",

      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    await getNotifications();
  }

  Future<void> readAllNotification() async {
    readAlLoad = true;
    notifyListeners();
    await dio.patch(
      "${ApiUrls.userNotifications}/read-all",

      options: Options(
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          'Authorization': 'Bearer ${userData?.token}',
        },
        validateStatus: (status) => true,
      ),
    );

    await getNotifications();
    readAlLoad = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  bool adSeenThisWeek = false;

  Future<void> hasSeenAdThisWeek() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final now = DateTime.now();
    final currentWeek = ((now.dayOfYear - now.weekday + 10) / 7).floor();
    final currentYear = now.year;

    final storedData = prefs.getString('showAd') ?? '';
    final parts = storedData.split('-');

    if (parts.length == 3) {
      final lastYear = int.tryParse(parts[0]) ?? 0;
      final lastWeek = int.tryParse(parts[1]) ?? 0;
      final hasSeen = parts[2] == 'true';

      adSeenThisWeek =
          (lastYear == currentYear && lastWeek == currentWeek && hasSeen);
      notifyListeners();
      return;
    }

    adSeenThisWeek = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  /// Marks the ad as shown for this week
  Future<void> markAdAsShown() async {
    final prefs = await SharedPreferences.getInstance();
    final now = DateTime.now();

    final currentWeek = ((now.dayOfYear - now.weekday + 10) / 7).floor();
    final currentYear = now.year;

    await prefs.setString('showAd', '$currentYear-$currentWeek-true');

    adSeenThisWeek = true;
    notifyListeners();
  }

  void markAdAsNotShown() {
    adSeenThisWeek = false;
    notifyListeners();
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\screens\init_screen.dart ===================
// ignore_for_file: use_build_context_synchronously

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/assets_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/layout/layout.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class InitScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const InitScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<InitScreen> createState() => _InitScreenState();
}

class _InitScreenState extends State<InitScreen> {
  bool _navigated = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((x) async {
      await checkUser();
    });

    super.initState();
  }

  /// Sends the user to Home no matter what happened above — offline,
  /// a slow/erroring endpoint, or a genuinely successful load all end
  /// the same way: the splash never gets left stuck.
  void _goHome() {
    if (_navigated || !mounted) return;
    _navigated = true;
    NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, HomePage());
  }

  Future<void> checkUser() async {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context, listen: false);

    try {
      final hasConnection = await sharedProvider
          .hasInternetConnection(true, context)
          .timeout(const Duration(seconds: 12), onTimeout: () => false);

      // No internet: NoInternetPage is already showing (pushed inside
      // hasInternetConnection). Stop here — don't fire off a chain of
      // network calls that have nothing to talk to.
      if (!hasConnection) return;

      if (!mounted) return;

      await sharedProvider.getUserDetails();
      await sharedProvider.hasSeenAdThisWeek();
      await sharedProvider.initTHM(context);

      if (!mounted) return;

      if (sharedProvider.userData == null) {
        await _loadGuestData(sharedProvider);
      } else {
        await _loadUserData(sharedProvider);
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // Whatever failed (timeout, bad response, unfinished endpoint on
      // the backend, etc.) — don't leave the splash frozen. Home still
      // works fine with whatever data did load; individual screens
      // handle their own empty/error states.
    } finally {
      _goHome();
    }
  }

  Future<void> _loadGuestData(SharedProvider sharedProvider) async {
    await Future.wait([
      sharedProvider.getProducts(context, refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getHotDeals(),
      sharedProvider.getTopDeals(refresh: true, next: false),
    ]).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 12), onTimeout: () => []);
  }

  Future<void> _loadUserData(SharedProvider sharedProvider) async {
    await Future.wait([
      sharedProvider.getProducts(context, refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getHotDeals(),
      sharedProvider.getTopDeals(refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getVendors(refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getOrders(refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getRepaymentCL(refresh: true, next: false),
      sharedProvider.getRecentViewed(),
      sharedProvider.loadCartItems(),
      sharedProvider.getNotifications(),
    ]).timeout(const Duration(seconds: 12), onTimeout: () => []);
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    return Scaffold(
      appBar: AppBar(
        automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
        excludeHeaderSemantics: true,
        backgroundColor: ch.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: SizedBox(
        height: 40,
        width: double.infinity,
        child: Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
          children: [
            Center(
              child: SizedBox(width: 100, child: LinearProgressIndicator()),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
      body: Center(
        child: Image.asset(
          AssetsHelper.logoImage,
          width: 150,
          fit: BoxFit.contain,
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _StatBlock extends StatelessWidget {
  const _StatBlock({
    required this.label,
    required this.value,
    this.valueColor,
  });
  final String label;
  final String value;
  final Color? valueColor;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Text(
          label,
          style: ptstyle(
            size: 10,
            weight: 600,
            color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.5),
          ).copyWith(letterSpacing: 1),
        ),
        SizedBox(height: 4),
        Text(
          value,
          style: ptstyle(
            size: 16,
            weight: 700,
            color: valueColor ?? Colors.white,
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _ActionCard extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ActionCard({
    required this.icon,
    required this.iconBg,
    required this.iconColor,
    required this.title,
    required this.subtitle,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  final IconData icon;
  final Color iconBg;
  final Color iconColor;
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return InkWell(
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
      onTap: onTap,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          border: Border.all(color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.15)),
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: iconBg,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              ),
              child: Icon(icon, color: iconColor, size: 20),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              title,
              style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurface),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 2),
            Text(
              subtitle,
              style: ptstyle(
                size: 11,
                weight: 300,
                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ActivityRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ActivityRow({
    required this.name,
    required this.date,
    required this.status,
    this.trailingText,
    this.trailingPoints,
  });

  final String name;
  final String date;
  final String status;
  final String? trailingText;
  final String? trailingPoints;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10),
      child: Row(
        children: [
          Container(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
            ),
            child: Icon(
              Icons.storefront_outlined,
              size: 18,
              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(width: 12),
          Expanded(
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                Text(
                  name,
                  style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurface),
                ),
                Text(
                  "$date · $status",
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 11,
                    weight: 300,
                    color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (trailingText != null)
            Text(
              trailingText!,
              style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 700, color: Colors.green),
            ),
          if (trailingPoints != null)
            Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                horizontal: 10,
                vertical: 4,
              ),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: _agentGold.withOpacity(0.15),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(30),
              ),
              child: Text(
                trailingPoints!,
                style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: _agentGold),
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\screens\agent_dashboard\agent_home_shell.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/agent_dashboard.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/agent_earnings.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/agent_me.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/agent_referrals.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/agent_share.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/agent_dashboard/components/agent_bottom_nav.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

/// Hosts the whole agent-mode experience: its own bottom nav
/// (Home / Referrals / Share / Earnings / Me), separate from the
/// customer bottom nav in HomePage. Pushed as its own route from the
/// "Agent" tab in the customer nav — the system back button (or the
/// switch button in the "Me" tab) drops the user back into the normal
/// customer nav automatically, no shared state needed.
class AgentHomeShell extends StatefulWidget {
  const AgentHomeShell({super.key});

  @override
  State<AgentHomeShell> createState() => _AgentHomeShellState();
}

class _AgentHomeShellState extends State<AgentHomeShell> {
  int _selectedIndex = 0;

  final List<Widget> _pages = const [
    AgentDashboard(),
    AgentReferrals(),
    AgentShare(),
    AgentEarnings(),
    AgentMe(),
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      body: AnimatedSwitcher(
        duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 200),
        child: _pages[_selectedIndex],
      ),
      bottomNavigationBar: AgentBottomNavBar(
        currentIndex: _selectedIndex,
        onTap: (index) => setState(() => _selectedIndex = index),
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AgentBottomNavBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int currentIndex;
  final Function(int) onTap;

  const AgentBottomNavBar({
    super.key,
    required this.currentIndex,
    required this.onTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return BottomNavigationBar(
      backgroundColor:
          AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
              ? ch.surface
              : const Color.fromARGB(255, 250, 252, 255),
      currentIndex: currentIndex,
      onTap: onTap,
      type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
      enableFeedback: false,
      unselectedLabelStyle: ptstyle(size: 10, weight: 300),
      selectedLabelStyle: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400),
      selectedItemColor: ch.primary,
      unselectedItemColor: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7),
      items: const [
        BottomNavigationBarItem(icon: Icon(Icons.home_outlined), label: 'Home'),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.groups_outlined),
          label: 'Referrals',
        ),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.public_outlined),
          label: 'Share',
        ),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.payments_outlined),
          label: 'Earnings',
        ),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
          label: 'Me',
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}
