@override
class _BrowseState extends State<Browse> with SingleTickerProviderStateMixin {
  TabController? tabController;
  @override
  void initState() {
    tabController = TabController(initialIndex: 0, length: 2, vsync: this);
    super.initState();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      appBar:
          sharedProvider.userData == null
              ? null
              : AppBar(
                toolbarHeight: 40,
                leadingWidth: 80.w,
                leading: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
                  child: TabBar(
                    dividerColor: Colors.transparent,

                        child: Text(
                          'Devices',
                          style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400),
                        ),
                      ),

                      Tab(
                        child: Text(
                          'Vendors',
                          style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                    controller: tabController,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
      bottomNavigationBar:
          sharedProvider.refreshing_ ? LoadingListIndicator() : null,
      body: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
            child:
                sharedProvider.products.isEmpty ||
                        (sharedProvider.vendors.isEmpty &&
                            sharedProvider.userData != null)
                    ? Padding(
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 20.0),
                      child: ListView(
                        children: List.generate(
                          10,
                          (index) => PDShimmer(ch: ch),
                        ),
                      ),
                    )
                    : TabBarView(
                      controller: tabController,

                      children: [
                        AnimationList(
                          animationDirection: AnimationDirection.vertical,
                          duration: 200,
                          reBounceDepth: 0,
                          controller: sharedProvider.listScrollControllerP,
                          // separatorBuilder: (context, index) => SDivider(),
                          padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                            top: sharedProvider.userData == null ? 5 : 10,
                          ),

                          children: List.generate(
                            sharedProvider.products.length + 1,
                            (index) {
                              if (sharedProvider.products.length == index) {
                                return Center(
                                  child: SizedBox(
                                    height: 30,
                                    width: 30,
                                    child: IconButton.filledTonal(
                                      style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                                        backgroundColor: ch.primaryFixed
                                            .withOpacity(0.2),
                                        padding: EdgeInsets.all(0),
                                      ),
                                      onPressed:
                                          () => sharedProvider.moreData(
                                            type: RefreshParam.products,
                                            context: context,
                                            refresh: true,
                                          ),
                                      icon: Icon(
                                        Icons.refresh_rounded,
                                        size: 24,
                                        color: ch.primary,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                );
                              }
                              final product = sharedProvider.products[index];
                              return VisibilityDetector(
                                onVisibilityChanged: (info) {
                                  if (index ==
                                          sharedProvider.products.length - 1 &&
                                      !sharedProvider.refreshing_) {
                                    sharedProvider.moreData(
                                      type: RefreshParam.products,
                                      context: context,
                                    );
                                  }
                                },
                                key: UniqueKey(),
                                child: ProductCard(
                                  status: product.status,
                                  imageUrl: product.image,
                                  productName: product.fullName,
                                  price: product.price,
                                  deviceSpecification:
                                      product.deviceSpecification ?? "",
                                  initialDeposit: 0.0,
                                  onPressed: () async {
                                    sharedProvider.getProductDetails(
                                      product.id,
                                      context,
                                    );
                                    NavigationHelper.push(
                                      context,
                                      ProductDetailsPage(productId: product.id),
                                    );
                                  },
                                  colors: product.colors,
                                ),
                              );
                            },
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (sharedProvider.userData != null)
                          AnimationList(
                            animationDirection: AnimationDirection.vertical,
                            duration: 200,
                            reBounceDepth: 0,
                            controller: sharedProvider.listScrollControllerV,
                            // children: (context, index) => SDivider(),
                            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 10),
                            //    itemCount: sharedProvider.vendors.length,
                            children: List.generate(
                              sharedProvider.vendors.length + 1,
                              (index) {
                                if (sharedProvider.vendors.length == index) {
                                  return Center(
                                    child: SizedBox(
                                      height: 30,
                                      width: 30,
                                      child: IconButton.filledTonal(
                                        style: IconButton.styleFrom(
                                          backgroundColor: ch.primaryFixed
                                              .withOpacity(0.2),
                                          padding: EdgeInsets.all(0),
                                        ),
                                        onPressed:
                                            () => sharedProvider.moreData(
                                              type: RefreshParam.vendors,
                                              context: context,
                                              refresh: true,

                                        icon: Icon(

                                          color: ch.primary,
                                        ),

                                    ),
                                  );
                                }

                                return VisibilityDetector(
                                  onVisibilityChanged: (info) {
                                    if (index ==
                                            sharedProvider.vendors.length - 1 &&
                                        !sharedProvider.refreshing_) {
                                      sharedProvider.moreData(
                                        type: RefreshParam.vendors,
                                        context: context,
                                      );
                                    }
                                  },
                                  key: UniqueKey(),
                                  child: StoreCard(
                                    name: store.name,
                                    image: store.image ?? "",
                                    address: store.location,
                                    state: store.status,
                                    // FIX: closingTime is nullable (vendors may
                                    // not have set business hours yet — see
                                    // Vendor model). Use a null-aware call so
                                    // this no longer fails to compile, and
                                    // fall back to a placeholder when a vendor
                                    // has no closing time set.
                                    closing:
                                        store.closingTime?.toAmPm() ?? "N/A",
                                    onPressed: () async {
                                      await sharedProvider.getVendorDetails(
                                        store.id,
                                      );
                                      NavigationHelper.push(
                                        context,
                                        StoreInfoPage(store_id: store.id),
                                      );
                                    },
                                  ),
                                );
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
          ),

          AnimatedSwitcher(
            duration: Duration(milliseconds: 400),

            child:
                sharedProvider.adSeenThisWeek
                    ? SizedBox.shrink()
                    : VendorBanner(
                      title: 'Earn more as a FlexiPhones vendor',
                      subtitle:
                          'List your devices, offer flexible payment plans, and connect with buyers ready to purchase. Our platform helps vendors increase sales while keeping transactions simple.',
                      close: () async => await sharedProvider.markAdAsShown(),
                    ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PDShimmer extends StatelessWidget {

            period: Duration(milliseconds: 1000),
            baseColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.1),
            highlightColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                // Image Placeholder
                Container(
                  width: 80,
                  height: 80,
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: Colors.white,
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                  ),
                ),
                const SizedBox(height: 10),

                // Title + Price
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8.0),
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.start,
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Container(height: 10, width: 200, color: Colors.white),
                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      Container(height: 10, width: 150, color: Colors.white),

                      const SizedBox(height: 10),
                      Container(height: 10, width: 150, color: Colors.white),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          SDivider(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/smart_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:shimmer/shimmer.dart';
import 'package:smooth_page_indicator/smooth_page_indicator.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

/// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
/// DESIGN TOKENS
/// A small, deliberate system so every screen in this file pulls from
/// the same scale instead of inventing paddings/radii ad hoc. Modeled on
/// Apple's product pages: quiet neutral surfaces, one restrained accent,
/// soft ambient shadows instead of hard borders, and a confident,
/// tightly-tracked type scale for headlines.
/// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
class _T {
  // Spacing scale
  static const double s4 = 4;
  static const double s8 = 8;
  static const double s12 = 12;
  static const double s16 = 16;
  static const double s20 = 20;
  static const double s24 = 24;
  static const double s32 = 32;
  static const double s40 = 40;

  // Radii
  static const double rCard = 28;
  static const double rTile = 18;
  static const double rPill = 100;
  static const double rSheet = 32;

  // Signature accent — a single restrained system-blue, used only for
  // the primary purchase action and selection states. Everything else
  // stays neutral so the accent actually reads as intentional.
  static const Color accent = Color(0xFF0A84FF);
  static const Color accentDeep = Color(0xFF0066D6);

  static List<BoxShadow> softShadow(BuildContext context, {double opacity = 0.06}) {
    final isDark = AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark;
    return [
      BoxShadow(
        color: Colors.black.withOpacity(isDark ? opacity * 1.6 : opacity),
        blurRadius: 24,
        offset: const Offset(0, 10),
        spreadRadius: -6,
      ),
    ];
  }

  static TextStyle display(BuildContext context,
      {required double size, required int weight, Color? color, double? letterSpacing, double? height}) {
    final ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return ptstyle(size: size, weight: weight, color: color ?? ch.onSurfaceVariant)
        .copyWith(letterSpacing: letterSpacing ?? -0.4, height: height);
  }
}

/// Parses the `colors` field which the backend has been observed to send in
/// two different shapes:
///   - legacy Postgres-array style: "{Black,Silver,Gold}"
///   - JSON-array style:            "[\"Black\",\"Silver\",\"Gold\"]"
/// Falls back gracefully (empty list) instead of throwing on anything else.
List<String> parseProductColors(String? raw) {
  if (raw == null || raw.trim().isEmpty) return [];

  try {
    final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
    if (decoded is List) {
      return decoded
          .map((e) => e.toString().trim().toUpperCase())
          .where((e) => e.isNotEmpty)
          .toList();
    }
  } catch (_) {
    // Not JSON — fall through to legacy parsing.
  }

  return raw
      .replaceAll("{", "")
      .replaceAll("}", "")
      .replaceAll("[", "")
      .replaceAll("]", "")
      .replaceAll('"', '')
      .split(",")
      .map((e) => e.trim().toUpperCase())
      .where((e) => e.isNotEmpty)
      .toList();
}

/// Best-effort mapping of a color name to a swatch dot. Falls back to a
/// neutral grey for anything unrecognized rather than guessing wrong.
Color _swatchFor(String name) {
  final n = name.toLowerCase();
  const map = <String, Color>{
    'black': Color(0xFF1D1D1F),
    'white': Color(0xFFF5F5F7),
    'silver': Color(0xFFE3E4E6),
    'gold': Color(0xFFEBD9B4),
    'blue': Color(0xFF3E6AE1),
    'red': Color(0xFFD5432A),
    'green': Color(0xFF4C8C5B),
    'purple': Color(0xFF8C6FB0),
    'pink': Color(0xFFE7B7C4),
    'gray': Color(0xFF8E8E93),
    'grey': Color(0xFF8E8E93),
    'yellow': Color(0xFFE3C468),
    'graphite': Color(0xFF54524F),
    'midnight': Color(0xFF1B1F26),
    'starlight': Color(0xFFF0E6D4),
  };
  for (final key in map.keys) {
    if (n.contains(key)) return map[key]!;
  }
  return const Color(0xFFB0B0B5);
}

class ProductDetailsPage extends StatefulWidget {
  ProductDetailsPage({super.key, required this.productId, this.fromStor = false});
  final dynamic productId;
  bool fromStor;

  @override
  State<ProductDetailsPage> createState() => _ProductDetailsPageState();
}

class _ProductDetailsPageState extends State<ProductDetailsPage> {
  bool buynow = false;
  int cp = 0;
  final pageController = PageController(initialPage: 0);

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // Always (re)fetch this specific product when the page opens — don't
    // depend on whatever happened to already be sitting in productData from
    // a previous screen.
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context, listen: false)
          .getProductDetails(widget.productId, context);
    });
  }

  void callPhone(tel) async {
    final Uri phoneUri = Uri(scheme: 'tel', path: tel);
    if (await canLaunchUrl(phoneUri)) {
      await launchUrl(phoneUri);
    } else {
      throw 'Could not launch $phoneUri';
    }
  }

  final GlobalKey<ScaffoldState> _scaffoldKey = GlobalKey<ScaffoldState>();
  bool showAll = false;
  SharedProvider? shd;

  @override
  void dispose() {
    shd?.productsVariations = false;
    super.dispose();
  }

  int selectedSTG = 0;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    shd = sharedProvider;

    // Still loading, or loaded but doesn't (yet) match the product we asked
    // for (e.g. mid-navigation from a different product's page): shimmer.
    final matchesRequestedProduct = sharedProvider.productData != null &&
        "${sharedProvider.productData!.id}" == "${widget.productId}";

    if (sharedProvider.loadingProduct || !matchesRequestedProduct) {
      if (sharedProvider.productDetailsError != null && !sharedProvider.loadingProduct) {
        return _ErrorScreen(
          ch: ch,
          message: sharedProvider.productDetailsError!,
          onRetry: () => sharedProvider.getProductDetails(widget.productId, context),
        );
      }
      return PDShimmer(ch: ch);
    }

    final productData = sharedProvider.productData!;

    final List<String> productImages = (productData.images as List<dynamic>? ?? [])
        .map((e) => e?.toString() ?? '')
        .where((s) => s.isNotEmpty)
        .toList();

    final List<ProductStorage> productStorages = productData.productStorages ?? [];

    buyNow(ProductModel product, ProductStorage ps) {
      var productDataMap = {
        'id': product.id,
        "vendor": product.vendor?.name,
        "productName": "${product.brand.toLowerCase()} ${product.model.toLowerCase()}",
        "image": product.image,
        "product_storages": ps.toJson(),
        "quantity": 1,
        "device_type": "smartphone",
        "service_tenure": sharedProvider.slTenure,
        "color": sharedProvider.colorSL,
        "isSelected": true,
        "package_id": null,
        "breakdowns": sharedProvider.breakDownPrice?.breakdowns.toJson(),
      };
      NavigationHelper.pop(context);
      NavigationHelper.push(context, OrderConfirmation(items: [productDataMap]));
    }

    bool addedToCart =
        sharedProvider.cartItems.where((x) => x["id"] == productData.id).toList().isNotEmpty;

    final reviews = productData.reviews ?? [];
    int totalRT = reviews.length;
    int totalRatingValue = reviews.fold(0, (sum, review) => sum + review.rating);
    double averageRating = totalRT == 0 ? 0.0 : totalRatingValue / totalRT;

    List<String> colorNames = parseProductColors(productData.colors);

    final String? rawClosingTime = productData.vendor?.closingTime;
    final String closingTimeLabel = (rawClosingTime == null || rawClosingTime.isEmpty)
        ? "Hours not set"
        : "Closes at ${rawClosingTime.toAmPm()}";

    final bool isActive = (productData.vendor?.status ?? "").toLowerCase() == "active";

    return AnimatedSwitcher(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 400),
      switchInCurve: Curves.easeOut,
      switchOutCurve: Curves.easeIn,
      child: Scaffold(
        key: _scaffoldKey,
        extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
        backgroundColor: ch.surface,
        appBar: AppBar(
          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
          elevation: 0,
          scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
          excludeHeaderSemantics: true,
          centerTitle: true,
          leading: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: _T.s12),
            child: _RoundIconButton(
              icon: Icons.arrow_back_ios_new_rounded,
              onTap: () => NavigationHelper.pop(context),
            ),
          ),
          title: Text(
            'Product',
            style: _T.display(context, size: 13, weight: 600, letterSpacing: -0.1),
          ),
        ),
        bottomNavigationBar: SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(_T.s16, 0, _T.s16, _T.s12),
            child: Container(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_T.s8),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
                boxShadow: _T.softShadow(context, opacity: 0.10),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Badge(
                    isLabelVisible: sharedProvider.cartItems.isNotEmpty,
                    alignment: Alignment.topRight,
                    backgroundColor: _T.accent,
                    label: Text(
                      sharedProvider.cartItems.length.toString(),
                      style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: Colors.white),
                    ),
                    child: _RoundIconButton(
                      icon: Icons.shopping_bag_outlined,
                      filled: true,
                      onTap: () => NavigationHelper.push(context, HomePage(initScreen: 2)),
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: _T.s12),
                  Expanded(
                    child: _PillActionButton(
                      label: addedToCart ? 'Added to Bag' : 'Add to Bag',
                      icon: addedToCart ? Icons.check_rounded : Icons.add_shopping_cart_rounded,
                      background: addedToCart
                          ? ch.surfaceContainerHighest
                          : const Color(0xFFEE6C55),
                      foreground: addedToCart ? const Color(0xFF30A15F) : Colors.white,
                      disabled: addedToCart,
                      onTap: () {
                        if (productStorages.isEmpty) {
                          ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                            const SnackBar(
                              content: Text("No storage options available for this product."),
                            ),
                          );
                          return;
                        }

                        setState(() => buynow = false);

                        final colors = parseProductColors(productData.colors);
                        sharedProvider.colorSL = colors.isNotEmpty ? colors.first : "";

                        showModalBottomSheet(
                          context: context,
                          isScrollControlled: true,
                          backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                          builder: (BuildContext context) {
                            return StorageOMD(
                              ch: ch,
                              sharedProvider: sharedProvider,
                              buynow: buynow,
                              buyNow: buyNow,
                            );
                          },
                        ).then((value) => sharedProvider.togglePV());
                      },
                    ),
                  ),
                  const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                  Expanded(
                    child: sharedProvider.userData == null
                        ? _PillActionButton(
                            label: 'Sign in',
                            icon: Icons.arrow_forward_rounded,
                            gradient: const LinearGradient(
                              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                              colors: [Color(0xFF00E787), Color(0xFF00DD88)],
                            ),
                            foreground: Colors.white,
                            onTap: () => NavigationHelper.push(context, EmailSignin()),
                          )
                        : _PillActionButton(
                            label: 'Buy Now',
                            icon: Icons.bolt_rounded,
                            gradient: const LinearGradient(
                              begin: Alignment.topLeft,
                              end: Alignment.bottomRight,
                              colors: [Color(0xFF00E787), Color(0xFF00DD88)],
                            ),
                            foreground: Colors.white,
                            onTap: () {
                              if (productStorages.isEmpty) {
                                ScaffoldMessenger.of(context).showSnackBar(
                                  const SnackBar(
                                    content:
                                        Text("No storage options available for this product."),
                                  ),
                                );
                                return;
                              }

                              sharedProvider.selectStorage(productStorages.first);

                              final colors = parseProductColors(productData.colors);
                              sharedProvider.colorSL = colors.isNotEmpty ? colors.first : "";

                              setState(() => buynow = true);

                              showModalBottomSheet(
                                context: context,
                                isScrollControlled: true,
                                backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                                builder: (BuildContext context) {
                                  return StorageOMD(
                                    ch: ch,
                                    sharedProvider: sharedProvider,
                                    buynow: buynow,
                                    buyNow: buyNow,
                                  );
                                },
                              ).then((value) => sharedProvider.togglePV());
                            },
                          ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        body: ListView(
          padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          children: [
            // ---------------------------------------------------------
            // HERO — signature element. One large, quiet stage for the
            // product: soft gradient card, centered device shot, minimal
            // paging dots. No blur bands or busy overlays.
            // ---------------------------------------------------------
            _HeroStage(
              images: productImages,
              fallback: productData.image,
              pageController: pageController,
              currentPage: cp,
              onPageChanged: (v) => setState(() => cp = v),
              fullName: productData.fullName,
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),

            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: Text(
                productData.fullName,
                softWrap: true,
                style: _T.display(context, size: 26, weight: 700, letterSpacing: -0.6, height: 1.1),
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),

            // Colors
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: _SectionLabel(icon: Icons.palette_outlined, label: 'Available Colors'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: colorNames.isEmpty
                  ? Text(
                      "No color options listed",
                      style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.55)),
                    )
                  : Wrap(
                      spacing: _T.s8,
                      runSpacing: _T.s8,
                      children: colorNames.map((name) {
                        return Container(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s12, vertical: _T.s8),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
                          ),
                          child: Row(
                            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                            children: [
                              Container(
                                width: 12,
                                height: 12,
                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                  color: _swatchFor(name),
                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                  border: Border.all(color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.12)),
                                ),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                              Text(
                                name,
                                style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 500, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        );
                      }).toList(),
                    ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),

            // Specification
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: _SectionLabel(icon: Icons.description_outlined, label: 'Specification'),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: Text.rich(
                TextSpan(
                  text: showAll
                      ? (productData.deviceSpecification ?? "")
                      : (productData.deviceSpecification ?? "").split(" ").take(20).join(" "),
                  style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.85))
                      .copyWith(height: 1.5),
                  children: [
                    WidgetSpan(
                      child: SizedBox(
                        height: 22,
                        child: TextButton(
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s4),
                            minimumSize: Size.zero,
                            tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                          ),
                          onPressed: () => setState(() => showAll = !showAll),
                          child: Text(
                            showAll ? " Show less" : ' Show more',
                            style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 600, color: _T.accent),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),

            // Storage options
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: _SectionLabel(
                icon: Icons.sd_storage_outlined,
                label: '${productStorages.length} Storage Options',
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s4),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: Text(
                productStorages.isEmpty
                    ? "No storage/pricing options available yet"
                    : "Tap an option to view payment details",
                style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.6)),
              ),
            ),
            if (productStorages.isNotEmpty)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: _T.s12),
                child: SizedBox(
                  height: 84,
                  child: ListView.separated(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
                    scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                    itemCount: productStorages.length,
                    separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(width: _T.s12),
                    itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                      final item = productStorages[index];
                      return Container(
                        decoration: BoxDecoration(
                          color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rTile),
                        ),
                        clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
                        child: StorageCard(
                          data: item,
                          enabled: selectedSTG == index,
                          select: () async {
                            setState(() => selectedSTG = index);
                            sharedProvider.getProductPB(item.id);
                            showModalBottomSheet(
                              context: context,
                              isScrollControlled: true,
                              backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
                              builder: (BuildContext context) {
                                return DDp(ch: ch, selectedSTG: selectedSTG);
                              },
                            );
                          },
                          staticObj: true,
                          tn: 7,
                          selectTenure: (x) {},
                          colors: productData.colors,
                          cl: sharedProvider.colorSL,
                          selectColor: (x) {},
                          breakdowns: sharedProvider.breakDownPrice?.breakdowns,
                        ),
                      );
                    },
                  ),
                ),
              ),

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s32),

            // Vendor card
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: Container(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_T.s16),
                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                  color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rTile),
                ),
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Row(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Expanded(
                          child: Column(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                            children: [
                              Text(
                                productData.vendor?.name ?? "Unknown Vendor",
                                style: _T.display(context, size: 16, weight: 700, letterSpacing: -0.2),
                              ),
                              const SizedBox(height: _T.s4),
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Container(
                                    width: 7,
                                    height: 7,
                                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                      shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                      color: isActive
                                          ? const Color(0xFF30D158)
                                          : ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.4),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                  const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                                  Flexible(
                                    child: Text(
                                      "${(productData.vendor?.status ?? "Unknown").toUpperCase()} • $closingTimeLabel",
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                      style: ptstyle(
                                          size: 11, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.6)),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                              if (sharedProvider.userData != null) ...[
                                const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                                Text(
                                  productData.vendor?.location ?? "",
                                  overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                  maxLines: 2,
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                      size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.55)),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                        if (sharedProvider.userData != null)
                          _GhostButton(
                            label: 'Visit',
                            onTap: () {
                              final vendorId = productData.vendor?.id;
                              if (vendorId == null) return;
                              sharedProvider.getVendorDetails(vendorId);
                              widget.fromStor
                                  ? NavigationHelper.pop(context)
                                  : NavigationHelper.push(context, StoreInfoPage(store_id: vendorId));
                            },
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    if (sharedProvider.userData != null) ...[
                      const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),
                      Row(
                        children: [
                          Expanded(
                            child: _GhostButton(
                              icon: Icons.call_outlined,
                              label: 'Call',
                              expand: true,
                              onTap: () => callPhone(productData.vendor?.phoneNumber),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(width: _T.s12),
                          Expanded(
                            child: _GhostButton(
                              icon: Icons.chat_bubble_outline_rounded,
                              label: 'WhatsApp',
                              expand: true,
                              onTap: () async {
                                final phone = productData.vendor?.phoneNumber;
                                if (phone == null || phone.toString().isEmpty) return;
                                final Uri whatsAppContact = Uri.parse("https://wa.me/233$phone");
                                if (await canLaunchUrl(whatsAppContact)) {
                                  await launchUrl(whatsAppContact);
                                } else {
                                  throw 'Could not launch $whatsAppContact';
                                }
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ],
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),

            if (sharedProvider.userData != null) ...[
              const SizedBox(height: _T.s32),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        _SectionLabel(icon: Icons.reviews_outlined, label: 'Reviews & Rating'),
                        const SizedBox(height: _T.s12),
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Icon(Icons.star_rounded, color: const Color(0xFFFFB020), size: 18),
                            const SizedBox(width: _T.s4),
                            Text(
                              '${averageRating.toStringAsFixed(1)} · $totalRT Ratings',
                              style: _T.display(context, size: 14, weight: 700, letterSpacing: -0.2),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                    _GhostButton(
                      label: 'View all',
                      onTap: () => NavigationHelper.push(context, ReviewsPage(reviews: reviews)),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(height: _T.s12),
              if (reviews.isEmpty)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20, vertical: _T.s8),
                  child: Text(
                    "No reviews yet",
                    style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.55)),
                  ),
                ),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
                child: Column(
                  children: [
                    for (var review in reviews.take(3))
                      _buildReviewCard(
                        name: review.customer?.name ?? "Anonymous",
                        date: review.createdAt.toString(),
                        rating: review.rating,
                        comment: review.comment,
                        ch: ch,
                        context: context,
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],

            const SizedBox(height: _T.s40),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReviewCard({
    required String name,
    required String date,
    required int rating,
    required String comment,
    required ColorScheme ch,
    required BuildContext context,
  }) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,
      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: _T.s12),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_T.s16),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rTile),
      ),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Text(name, style: _T.display(context, size: 13, weight: 700, letterSpacing: -0.1)),
                    const SizedBox(height: _T.s4),
                    Text(date, style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.55))),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              Row(
                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                children: [
                  for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
                    Icon(
                      Icons.star_rounded,
                      color: i < rating ? const Color(0xFFFFB020) : ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.2),
                      size: 15,
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: _T.s12),
          Text(
            comment,
            style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.85)).copyWith(height: 1.4),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
/// Hero stage: the one memorable moment on this page. A soft, rounded
/// gradient surface holding the product photography, with a restrained
/// dot indicator — no blur bands, no busy overlays.
/// ---------------------------------------------------------------------
class _HeroStage extends StatelessWidget {
  const _HeroStage({
    required this.images,
    required this.fallback,
    required this.pageController,
    required this.currentPage,
    required this.onPageChanged,
    required this.fullName,
  });

  final List<String> images;
  final String fallback;
  final PageController pageController;
  final int currentPage;
  final ValueChanged<int> onPageChanged;
  final String fullName;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final isDark = AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark;

    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(_T.s16, 0, _T.s16, 0),
      child: Container(
        height: 340,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rCard),
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topCenter,
            end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
            colors: isDark
                ? [
                    ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.22),
                    ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                  ]
                : [
                    ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.16),
                    ch.surfaceContainerLowest,
                  ],
          ),
        ),
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 64, bottom: _T.s16),
          child: Column(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: images.isEmpty
                    ? Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(_T.s24),
                        child: Opacity(
                          opacity: 0.3,
                          child: Image.asset(AssetsHelper.dummyImage, fit: BoxFit.contain),
                        ),
                      )
                    : PageView(
                        onPageChanged: onPageChanged,
                        controller: pageController,
                        children: List.generate(
                          images.length,
                          (index) => InkWell(
                            onTap: () => showImagePreview(context, images[index], title: fullName),
                            child: Padding(
                              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s24),
                              child: Hero(
                                tag: 'product_hero_$index',
                                child: Image.network(
                                  images[index],
                                  fit: BoxFit.contain,
                                  errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => Opacity(
                                    opacity: 0.3,
                                    child: Image.asset(AssetsHelper.dummyImage, fit: BoxFit.contain),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
              ),
              if (images.length > 1)
                Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: _T.s12),
                  child: SmoothPageIndicator(
                    controller: pageController,
                    count: images.length,
                    effect: ExpandingDotsEffect(
                      dotWidth: 6,
                      dotHeight: 6,
                      spacing: 6,
                      activeDotColor: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.75),
                      dotColor: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.2),
                    ),
                    onDotClicked: (index) {},
                  ),
                ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _SectionLabel extends StatelessWidget {
  const _SectionLabel({required this.icon, required this.label});
  final IconData icon;
  final String label;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Row(
      children: [
        Icon(icon, size: 14, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5)),
        const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
        Text(
          label,
          style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.55))
              .copyWith(letterSpacing: 0.2),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _RoundIconButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _RoundIconButton({required this.icon, required this.onTap, this.filled = false});
  final IconData icon;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final bool filled;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Material(
      color: filled ? ch.surfaceContainerHigh : ch.surfaceContainerLow,
      shape: const CircleBorder(),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: SizedBox(
          width: 44,
          height: 44,
          child: Icon(icon, size: 19, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _PillActionButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _PillActionButton({
    required this.label,
    required this.icon,
    this.background,
    this.gradient,
    required this.foreground,
    required this.onTap,
    this.disabled = false,
  }) : assert(background != null || gradient != null, 'Provide background or gradient');

  final String label;
  final IconData icon;
  final Color? background;
  final Gradient? gradient;
  final Color foreground;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final bool disabled;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Opacity(
      opacity: disabled ? 0.55 : 1,
      child: Material(
        color: gradient != null ? null : background,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
        clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
        child: InkWell(
          onTap: disabled ? null : onTap,
          child: Container(
            height: 48,
            alignment: Alignment.center,
            decoration: gradient != null ? BoxDecoration(gradient: gradient) : null,
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
              children: [
                Icon(icon, size: 16, color: foreground),
                const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                Flexible(
                  child: Text(
                    label,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 700, color: foreground),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _GhostButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _GhostButton({required this.label, required this.onTap, this.icon, this.expand = false});
  final String label;
  final VoidCallback onTap;
  final IconData? icon;
  final bool expand;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Material(
      color: ch.surfaceContainerHighest.withOpacity(0.6),
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          height: 38,
          width: expand ? double.infinity : null,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              if (icon != null) ...[
                Icon(icon, size: 15, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
                const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
              ],
              Text(label, style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant)),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

              ),

          ),
        ),
      ),
    );

}

class DDp extends StatefulWidget {
  const DDp({super.key, required this.ch, this.selectedSTG = 0});
  final ColorScheme ch;
  final int selectedSTG;

  @override
  State<DDp> createState() => _DDpState();
}

class _DDpState extends State<DDp> {
  SharedProvider? shd;

  ProductStorage? get sOPT {
    final storages = shd?.productData?.productStorages;
    if (storages == null || storages.isEmpty) return null;
    if (widget.selectedSTG < 0 || widget.selectedSTG >= storages.length) return null;
    return storages[widget.selectedSTG];
  }

  dynamic get slMonth {
    final breakDownPrice = shd?.breakDownPrice;
    if (breakDownPrice == null) return null;
    return breakDownPrice.breakdowns.months["${shd!.slTenure}_months"];
  }

  String get initialPayment {
    final m = slMonth;
    if (shd?.breakDownPrice == null || m == null) return "";
    return double.parse(m["initial_payment"].toString()).toCommaSeparated(0, decimalPlaces: 2);
  }

  String get totalPayment {
    final m = slMonth;
    if (shd?.breakDownPrice == null || m == null) return "";
    return double.parse(m["total_price"].toString()).toCommaSeparated(0, decimalPlaces: 2);
  }

  String get dailyPayment {
    final m = slMonth;
    if (shd?.breakDownPrice == null || m == null) return "";
    return double.parse(m["daily_payment"].toString()).toCommaSeparated(0, decimalPlaces: 2);
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((timeStamp) {
      Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context, listen: false).selectTenure(3);
    });
    super.initState();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    shd = sharedProvider;
    return SafeArea(
      top: false,
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.ch.surface,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: Radius.circular(_T.rSheet),
            topRight: Radius.circular(_T.rSheet),
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s12),
            Container(
              width: 36,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.2),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s20),
            Text(
              'STORAGE',
              style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5))
                  .copyWith(letterSpacing: 1),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s4),
            Text(
              sOPT?.storage ?? "—",
              style: _T.display(context, size: 24, weight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.6, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),
            sharedProvider.loadingPBD
                ? const TenureShimmer()
                : sharedProvider.breakDownPrice == null
                    ? Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s24, vertical: _T.s24),
                        child: Text(
                          "Pricing details are unavailable for this storage option right now.",
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 500, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7)),
                        ),
                      )
                    : Column(
                        children: [
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
                            child: SizedBox(
                              height: 40,
                              child: ListView.separated(
                                shrinkWrap: true,
                                scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                                padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                                itemCount: sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months.keys.length,
                                separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                                itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                                  final t =
                                      sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months.keys.toList()[index];
                                  var data = sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months[t];
                                  final bool selected = sharedProvider.slTenure == data["tenure_months"];
                                  return _TenureChip(
                                    label: '${data["tenure_months"]} mo',
                                    selected: selected,
                                    onTap: () => sharedProvider.selectTenure(data["tenure_months"]),
                                  );
                                },
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                          Text(
                            'Tap to switch between monthly tenure options',
                            style: ptstyle(
                                size: 10, weight: 400, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5)),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),
                          Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
                            child: Row(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                              children: [
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Column(
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        'Initial Payment',
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                            size: 10, weight: 600, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                                      Text(
                                        "GHS $initialPayment",
                                        style: _T.display(context,
                                            size: 20, weight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.5, color: const Color(0xFF30A15F)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                                      Text(
                                        "of GHS $totalPayment total",
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                            size: 10, weight: 500, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.45)),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                                SizedBox(
                                  height: 46,
                                  child: VerticalDivider(
                                      thickness: 1, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.1)),
                                ),
                                Expanded(
                                  child: Column(
                                    children: [
                                      Text(
                                        'Daily Payment',
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                            size: 10, weight: 600, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5)),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                                      Text(
                                        "GHS $dailyPayment",
                                        style: _T.display(context,
                                            size: 20, weight: 800, letterSpacing: -0.5, color: _T.accent),
                                      ),
                                      const SizedBox(height: _T.s8),
                                      Text(
                                        'over ${sharedProvider.slTenure * 30} days',
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                            size: 10, weight: 500, color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.45)),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),
                        ],
                      ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TenureChip extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TenureChip({required this.label, required this.selected, required this.onTap});
  final String label;
  final bool selected;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Material(
      color: selected ? _T.accent : ch.surfaceContainerLow,
      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
      child: InkWell(
        onTap: onTap,
        child: Container(
          height: 40,
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
          alignment: Alignment.center,
          child: Text(
            label,
            style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 700, color: selected ? Colors.white : ch.onSurfaceVariant),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class StorageOMD extends StatefulWidget {
  const StorageOMD({super.key, required this.ch, required this.sharedProvider, required this.buynow, this.buyNow});
  final ColorScheme ch;
  final SharedProvider sharedProvider;
  final bool buynow;
  final dynamic buyNow;

  @override
  State<StorageOMD> createState() => _StorageOMDState();
}

class _StorageOMDState extends State<StorageOMD> {
  bool useBundle = false;

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    widget.sharedProvider.addListener(() {
      if (!mounted) return;
      setState(() {});
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return SafeArea(
      top: false,
      child: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.ch.surface,
          borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
            topLeft: Radius.circular(_T.rSheet),
            topRight: Radius.circular(_T.rSheet),
          ),
        ),
        child: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s12),
            Container(
              width: 36,
              height: 4,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.2),
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              child: StorageSelectionList(sharedProvider: widget.sharedProvider),
            ),
            Container(
              height: 52,
              margin: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s20),
              width: double.infinity,
              child: SmartButton(
                // FIX: previously required `selectedStorage != null` only.
                // Add-to-cart also needs a loaded pricing breakdown, or the
                // onPressed handler below has nothing valid to send —
                // disabling until both are ready prevents a dead tap.
                enabled: widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null &&
                    (widget.buynow || widget.sharedProvider.breakDownPrice != null),
                loading: false,
                disabledOpacity: 0.25,
                onPressed: () {
                  if (widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage == null ||
                      widget.sharedProvider.productData == null) {
                    return;
                  }

                  if (widget.buynow) {
                    widget.buyNow(widget.sharedProvider.productData!, widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage!);
                    widget.sharedProvider.togglePV();
                    return;
                  }

                  // FIX: this call previously passed
                  // `widget.sharedProvider.productDataPackages?.toJson()` (a
                  // plain Map with no `.breakdowns` getter) for the
                  // breakDownPrice argument. addToCart() called
                  // `breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.toJson()` while building the
                  // cart item — before the item was ever added to
                  // cartItems — so it threw an unhandled error and the tap
                  // silently did nothing. The correct value is
                  // sharedProvider.breakDownPrice, the actual pricing
                  // breakdown loaded via getProductPB() when a storage
                  // option is selected.
                  widget.sharedProvider.addToCart(
                    widget.sharedProvider.productData!,
                    widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage!,
                    widget.sharedProvider.slTenure,
                    widget.sharedProvider.colorSL,
                    null,
                    widget.sharedProvider.breakDownPrice,
                  );
                  widget.sharedProvider.togglePV();
                  NavigationHelper.pop(context);
                },
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      widget.buynow ? "Continue to Checkout" : "Confirm Device Selection",
                      style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 700),
                    ),
                    const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                    Container(
                      constraints: const BoxConstraints(maxWidth: 120),
                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s8, vertical: 2),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.18),
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
                      ),
                      child: Text(
                        widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null
                            ? widget.sharedProvider.selectedStorage!.storage
                            : "~",
                        overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                        style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 700),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: _T.s24),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class StorageSelectionList extends StatelessWidget {
  const StorageSelectionList({super.key, required this.sharedProvider});
  final SharedProvider sharedProvider;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;

    List<String> colorNames = parseProductColors(sharedProvider.productData?.colors);
    final List<ProductStorage> productStorages = sharedProvider.productData?.productStorages ?? [];
    final bool hasPricing = sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null && sharedProvider.breakDownPrice != null;

    return Column(
      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
      children: [
        if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null)
          Container(
            margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: _T.s8),
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rTile),
            ),
            child: StorageCard(
              data: sharedProvider.selectedStorage!,
              enabled: true,
              select: () {},
              staticObj: false,
              tn: sharedProvider.slTenure,
              colors: sharedProvider.productData?.colors ?? "",
              selectTenure: (x) {
                sharedProvider.selectTenure(x);
              },
              cl: sharedProvider.colorSL,
              selectColor: (x) {
                sharedProvider.selectColor(x);
              },
              breakdowns: sharedProvider.breakDownPrice?.breakdowns,
            ),
          ),
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: _T.s12),
          child: Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: Text(
              "CHOOSE DEVICE STORAGE",
              style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.45))
                  .copyWith(letterSpacing: 0.6),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        SizedBox(
          height: 38,
          width: double.infinity,
          child: productStorages.isEmpty
              ? Align(
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                  child: Text(
                    "No storage options available",
                    style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.6)),
                  ),
                )
              : ListView.separated(
                  separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                  scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
                  itemCount: productStorages.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    var item = productStorages[index];
                    return buildOptionChip(
                      item.storage,
                      () => sharedProvider.selectStorage(item),
                      sharedProvider.selectedStorage == item,
                      ch,
                    );
                  },
                ),
        ),
        if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: _T.s12),
            child: Align(
              alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
              child: Text(
                "CHOOSE SERVICE TENURE",
                style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.45))
                    .copyWith(letterSpacing: 0.6),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        if (hasPricing)
          SizedBox(
            height: 36,
            width: double.infinity,
            child: ListView.separated(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
              itemCount: sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months.keys.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final t = sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months.keys.toList()[index];
                var data = sharedProvider.breakDownPrice!.breakdowns.months[t];
                final bool selected = sharedProvider.slTenure == data["tenure_months"];
                return _TenureChip(
                  label: '${data["tenure_months"]} months',
                  selected: selected,
                  onTap: () => sharedProvider.selectTenure(data["tenure_months"]),
                );
              },
            ),
          )
        else if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null)
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: Text("Loading tenure options...",
                style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.6))),
          ),
        if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null)
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: _T.s12),
            child: Align(
              alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
              child: Text(
                "CHOOSE DEVICE COLOR",
                style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.45))
                    .copyWith(letterSpacing: 0.6),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null && colorNames.isNotEmpty)
          SizedBox(
            height: 36,
            width: double.infinity,
            child: ListView.separated(
              scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
              separatorBuilder: (context, index) => const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
              itemCount: colorNames.length,
              itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                final name = colorNames[index];
                final bool selected = sharedProvider.colorSL == name;
                return GestureDetector(
                  onTap: () => sharedProvider.selectColor.call(name),
                  child: Container(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: selected ? ch.onSurface.withOpacity(0.06) : ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: selected ? ch.onSurface : Colors.transparent,
                        width: 1.4,
                      ),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          width: 10,
                          height: 10,
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: _swatchFor(name),
                            shape: BoxShape.circle,
                            border: Border.all(color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.15)),
                          ),
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(width: _T.s8),
                        Text(
                          name,
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                );
              },
            ),
          )
        else if (sharedProvider.selectedStorage != null)
          Align(
            alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
            child: Text("No color options listed",
                style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.6))),
          ),
        const SizedBox(height: _T.s16),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget buildOptionChip(String label, VoidCallback onPressed, bool active, ColorScheme ch) {
    return GestureDetector(
      onTap: onPressed,
      child: Container(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: _T.s16),
        alignment: Alignment.center,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: active ? ch.onSurface : ch.surfaceContainerLow,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(_T.rPill),
        ),
        child: Text(
          label,
          style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: active ? ch.surface : ch.onSurfaceVariant),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PDShimmer extends StatelessWidget {
  const PDShimmer({super.key, required this.ch});
  final ColorScheme ch;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ch.surface,
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      extendBody: true,
      appBar: AppBar(
        centerTitle: true,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        title: Text('Product details', style: ptstyle(size: 14, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant)),
      ),
      body: Shimmer.fromColors(
        period: const Duration(milliseconds: 900),
        baseColor: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
        highlightColor: ch.surfaceContainerHigh,
        child: Padding(
