class ShoppingCartScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const ShoppingCartScreen({super.key});

  @override
  ShoppingCartScreenState createState() => ShoppingCartScreenState();
}

class ShoppingCartScreenState extends State<ShoppingCartScreen> {
  void pDetails(item,SharedProvider sharedProvider) {
    sharedProvider.getProductDetails(item["id"], context);
    NavigationHelper.push(context, ProductDetailsPage(productId: item["id"]));
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    // selectAll = widget.cartItems.where((x) => x["isSelected"] == false).isEmpty;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      body: Column(
        children: [
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0),
            child: Row(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
              children: [
                SizedBox(
                  height: 30,

                  child: FilledButton.tonal(
                    style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                      elevation: 0,
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
                      overlayColor: ch.primaryContainer,
                      backgroundColor: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                    ),

                    onPressed: () => sharedProvider.selectAllCartItem(),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Icon(
                          sharedProvider.selectAll
                              ? Icons.check_box
                              : Icons.check_box_outline_blank,

                          'Selected All',

                            size: 12,
                            weight: 400,
                            color: ch.primary,
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Spacer(),
                IgnorePointer(
                  ignoring: sharedProvider.cartsToRemove.isEmpty,
                  child: Opacity(
                    opacity: sharedProvider.cartsToRemove.isNotEmpty ? 1 : 0.5,
                    child: SizedBox(
                      height: 30,

                      child: FilledButton.tonal(
                        style: FilledButton.styleFrom(
                          elevation: 0,
                          padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),

                                                ).mode.isDark
                                                ? Color.fromARGB(
                                                  255,
                                                  61,
                                                  29,
                                                  42,
                                                )
                                                : Color.fromARGB(
                                                  255,
                                                  255,
                                                  190,
                                                  190,
                                                ).withOpacity(0.5), // Purple
                                          ],
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                      child: Text(
                                        "+ Bonus",
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                          size: 12,
                                          weight: 500,
                                          color: const Color.fromARGB(
                                            255,
                                            255,
                                            4,
                                            130,
                                          ),
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ],
                                ],
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                );
              },
            ),
          ),
          SDivider(),
          _summaryRow(
            'Selected Items',
            sharedProvider.selectedCount.toString(),
            ch,
          ),
          SizedBox(height: 10),
          if (sharedProvider.userData != null)
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8.0),
              child: SmartButton(
                loading: false,
                enabled: sharedProvider.checked,
                onPressed:
                    () => NavigationHelper.push(
                      context,
                      OrderConfirmation(items: sharedProvider.selectedItems),
                    ),
                child: Text(
                  'Order Selected',
                  style: ptstyle(size: 15, weight: 600),
                ),
              ),
            ),

          SizedBox(height: 10),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _summaryRow(String title, String value, ColorScheme ch) {
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10.0, vertical: 5),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            title,
            style: ptstyle(
              size: title.contains("Initial") ? 16 : 14,
              weight: title.contains("Initial") ? 600 : 400,
              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(
                title.contains("Initial") ? 1 : 0.8,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          Text(
            value,
            style: ptstyle(
              size: title.contains("Initial") ? 16 : 14,
              weight: title.contains("Initial") ? 600 : 400,
              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';

/// Redesign notes:
/// - `colors` from the API is a JSON array string, e.g.
///   `"[\"Black\",\"Silver\",\"Gold\"]"` — parsed with jsonDecode now, with
///   a fallback to the old comma/brace parsing in case an older endpoint
///   still sends `{Black,Silver,Gold}`.
/// - Added a price pill (cheapest monthly plan) and a rating badge, since
///   the real payload has `product_storages` and `rating` that the old
///   card never showed — that's most of what makes a deal card feel like
///   an actual deal instead of a generic listing.
/// - Color chips are now real color swatches (a small dot in the actual
///   color) instead of plain text pills.
/// - ASSUMPTION: `item.productStorages` is assumed to expose camelCase
///   fields (`cashPrice`, `threeMonthPrice`, `fourMonthPrice`,
///   `fiveMonthPrice`, `sixMonthPrice`) as Strings, matching the default
///   json_serializable conversion of `cash_price` etc. If your model
///   names these differently, rename the references inside
///   `_cheapestMonthly()` and `_cashPrice()` below — they're the only
///   two places that touch pricing.
class PromotionCard extends StatefulWidget {
  final String headerTitle;
  final String promotionTitle;
  final String buttonText;
  final String dealSectionTitle;
  final String seeAllText;
  final String imageUrl;

  const PromotionCard({
    super.key,
    required this.headerTitle,
    required this.promotionTitle,
    required this.buttonText,
    required this.dealSectionTitle,
    required this.seeAllText,
    required this.imageUrl,
  });

  @override
  State<PromotionCard> createState() => _PromotionCardState();
}

class _PromotionCardState extends State<PromotionCard> {
  final CarouselSliderController _controller = CarouselSliderController();
  int _currentIndex = 0;

  List<String> _parseColors(String raw) {
    try {
      final decoded = jsonDecode(raw);
      if (decoded is List) {
        return decoded
            .map((e) => e.toString().trim().toUpperCase())
            .where((e) => e.isNotEmpty)
            .toList();
      }
    } catch (_) {
      // fall through to legacy parsing below
    }
    return raw
        .replaceAll("{", "")
        .replaceAll("}", "")
        .replaceAll('"', "")
        .replaceAll("[", "")
        .replaceAll("]", "")
        .split(",")
        .map((e) => e.trim().toUpperCase())
        .where((e) => e.isNotEmpty)
        .toList();
  }

  Color _swatchFor(String name) {
    const map = {
      "BLACK": Colors.black,
      "WHITE": Color(0xFFF5F5F5),
      "SILVER": Color(0xFFC7C9CC),
      "GOLD": Color(0xFFD4AF37),
      "ROSE GOLD": Color(0xFFB76E79),
      "MIDNIGHT": Color(0xFF1D1D2C),
      "STARLIGHT": Color(0xFFF0E6D2),
      "GRAPHITE": Color(0xFF41424C),
      "BLUE": Color(0xFF3B82F6),
      "RED": Color(0xFFEF4444),
      "GREEN": Color(0xFF22C55E),
      "PURPLE": Color(0xFFA855F7),
      "PINK": Color(0xFFEC4899),
      "GRAY": Color(0xFF9CA3AF),
      "GREY": Color(0xFF9CA3AF),
      "YELLOW": Color(0xFFFACC15),
      "ORANGE": Color(0xFFF97316),
      "TEAL": Color(0xFF14B8A6),
      "NAVY": Color(0xFF001F54),
    };
    return map[name] ?? const Color(0xFFB0B3B8);
  }

  String _money(num value) {
    final s = value.round().toString();
    final buf = StringBuffer();
    for (int i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
      final posFromEnd = s.length - i;
      buf.write(s[i]);
      if (posFromEnd > 1 && posFromEnd % 3 == 1) buf.write(",");
    }
    return "GH₵$buf";
  }

  /// Cheapest available monthly plan across this item's storages, or null.
  double? _cheapestMonthly(dynamic item) {
    try {
      final storages = item.productStorages as List;
      if (storages.isEmpty) return null;
      double? min;
      for (final s in storages) {
        for (final raw in [
          s.threeMonthPrice,
          s.fourMonthPrice,
          s.fiveMonthPrice,
          s.sixMonthPrice,
        ]) {
          final v = double.tryParse(raw?.toString() ?? "");
          if (v != null && (min == null || v < min)) min = v;
        }
      }
      return min;
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  double? _cashPrice(dynamic item) {
    try {
      final storages = item.productStorages as List;
      if (storages.isEmpty) return null;
      return double.tryParse(storages.first.cashPrice?.toString() ?? "");
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  double? _rating(dynamic item) {
    try {
      return double.tryParse(item.rating?.toString() ?? "");
    } catch (_) {
      return null;
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    final ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final isDark = AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark;
    final deals = sharedProvider.hotDeals;

    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 5),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: ch.primaryContainer,
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
          ),
          child: Row(
            mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
            children: [
              Icon(Icons.local_fire_department_rounded,
                  size: 14, color: ch.onPrimaryContainer),
              const SizedBox(width: 5),
              Text(
                "Hot Deals — Pay Less For Your Favorite",
                style:
                    ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 700, color: ch.onPrimaryContainer),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        const SizedBox(height: 10),
        CarouselSlider(
          carouselController: _controller,
          options: CarouselOptions(
            pageSnapping: true,
            onPageChanged: (index, reason) =>
                setState(() => _currentIndex = index),
            aspectRatio: 16 / 8.6,
            viewportFraction: 0.92,
            autoPlay: true,
            autoPlayInterval: const Duration(seconds: 5),
          ),
          items: deals.isEmpty
              ? List.generate(3, (index) => const PromotionCardShimmer())
              : deals.map((item) {
                  final colorNames = _parseColors(item.colors);
                  final monthly = _cheapestMonthly(item);
                  final cash = _cashPrice(item);
                  final rating = _rating(item);

                  return Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
                    child: Material(
                      color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
                      elevation: 2,
                      shadowColor: ch.shadow.withOpacity(0.18),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
                      clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
                      child: InkWell(
                        onTap: () {
                          sharedProvider.getProductDetails(item.id, context);
                          NavigationHelper.push(
                            context,
                            ProductDetailsPage(productId: item.id),
                          );
                        },
                        child: Padding(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 14, 14, 10),
                          child: Row(
                            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                            children: [
                              Expanded(
                                child: Column(
                                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                                  crossAxisAlignment:
                                      CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                  children: [
                                    Text(
                                      item.fullName,
                                      maxLines: 1,
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                      style: ptstyle(
                                        size: 16,
                                        weight: 700,
                                        color: ch.onSurface,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    const SizedBox(height: 4),
                                    Text(
                                      item.deviceSpecification
                                          .toSentenceCase(),
                                      maxLines: 2,
                                      overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                                      style: ptstyle(
                                        size: 12,
                                        weight: 400,
                                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                    if (colorNames.isNotEmpty) ...[
                                      const SizedBox(height: 8),
                                      Row(
                                        children: [
                                          ...colorNames.take(4).map(
                                            (name) => Padding(
                                              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(
                                                  right: 5),
                                              child: Container(
                                                width: 16,
                                                height: 16,
                                                decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                                  color: _swatchFor(name),
                                                  shape: BoxShape.circle,
                                                  border: Border.all(
                                                    color: ch.outlineVariant,
                                                    width: 1,
                                                  ),
                                                ),
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                          if (colorNames.length > 4)
                                            Text(
                                              "+${colorNames.length - 4}",
                                              style: ptstyle(
                                                size: 10,
                                                weight: 600,
                                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                              ),
                                            ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                    const SizedBox(height: 10),
                                    Wrap(
                                      crossAxisAlignment:
                                          WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                                      spacing: 6,
                                      runSpacing: 4,
                                      children: [
                                        if (monthly != null)
                                          Container(
                                            padding:
                                                const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                                    horizontal: 8,

                                            decoration: BoxDecoration(

                                            ),
                                          ),

                                          AssetsHelper.dummyImage,

class PromotionCardShimmer extends StatelessWidget {
  const PromotionCardShimmer({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 4),
      child: Material(
        color: ch.surfaceContainerLow,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(18),
        clipBehavior: Clip.antiAlias,
        child: Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Column(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    _shimmerBlock(ch, height: 18, width: 180),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    _shimmerBlock(ch, height: 12, width: 140),
                    const SizedBox(height: 8),
                    _shimmerBlock(ch, height: 12, width: 100),
                    const SizedBox(height: 12),
                    _shimmerBlock(ch, height: 20, width: 100, radius: 20),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              _shimmerBlock(ch, height: 100, width: 100, radius: 50),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _shimmerBlock(
    ColorScheme ch, {
    required double height,
    required double width,
    double radius = 5,
  }) {
    return Shimmer.fromColors(
      baseColor: ch.surfaceContainerHighest,
      highlightColor: ch.surfaceContainerHigh,
      child: Container(
        height: height,
        width: width,
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: ch.surfaceContainerHighest,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(radius),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class HomePage extends StatefulWidget {
  const HomePage({super.key, this.initScreen = 0});
  final int initScreen;

  @override
  State<HomePage> createState() => _HomePageState();
}

class _HomePageState extends State<HomePage> {
  final List<Map> _pages = [
    {0: "FlexiPhones ", 1: Home()},
    {0: "Browse", 1: Browse()},
    {0: "Cart", 1: ShoppingCartScreen()},
    {0: "Orders", 1: Orders()},
    {0: "Profile", 1: Profile()},
  ];
  final List<Map> _pages2 = [
    {0: "FlexiPhones ", 1: Home()},
    {0: "Browse", 1: Browse()},
    {0: "Cart", 1: ShoppingCartScreen()},
  ];
  SharedProvider? shdP;

  int _selectedIndex = 0;

  Timer? tm;

  void _onTabTapped(int index) {
    setState(() {
      _selectedIndex = index;
    });
  }

  @override
  void initState() {
    //
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((timeStamp) async {
      _onTabTapped(widget.initScreen);
      if (shdP!.userData != null) {
        await shdP!.checkINIT(context);
        await shdP!.initializeNotifications();
        tm = Timer.periodic(Duration(seconds: 10), (timer) {
          if (shdP != null) {
            shdP!.getNotifications();
          }
        });
      }
    });

    super.initState();
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    tm?.cancel();
    super.dispose();
  }

  // Define the key globally
  final GlobalKey<ScaffoldState> scaffoldKey = GlobalKey<ScaffoldState>();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    shdP = sharedProvider;
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    var pageTitle = Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10.0),
      child: Text(
        sharedProvider.search
            ? "Search"
            : (sharedProvider.userData == null
                    ? _pages2[_selectedIndex][0]
                    : _pages[_selectedIndex][0])
                as String,
        style: ptstyle(
          size: 14,
          weight: 600,
          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.8),
        ),
      ),
    );
    var searchButton = AbsorbPointer(
      absorbing: sharedProvider.userData == null,
      child: Opacity(
        opacity: sharedProvider.userData == null ? 0.3 : 1.0,
        child: IconButton.filledTonal(
          style: ButtonStyle(
            backgroundColor: WidgetStatePropertyAll(ch.surfaceContainerLow),
          ),
          onPressed: () {
            sharedProvider.getFilteredProducts();
            sharedProvider.enableSearching();
          },
          icon: Icon(Icons.search),
        ),
      ),
    );

    return GestureDetector(
      behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
      onTap: () => FocusScope.of(context).unfocus(),
      child: UpgradeAlert(
        child: Scaffold(
          key: scaffoldKey,
          drawer: NotificationDrawer(),
          appBar: PreferredSize(
            preferredSize: Size.fromHeight(kToolbarHeight),
            child: AppBar(
              flexibleSpace: Stack(
                children: [
                  Bgw4(),
                  Container(
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      gradient: LinearGradient(
                        colors: [
                          (AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                  ? ch.surface
                                  : ch.primaryFixed)
                              .withOpacity(0.5),
                          (AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                  ? ch.surface
                                  : ch.primaryFixed)
                              .withOpacity(0.2),
                          (AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                  ? ch.surface
                                  : ch.primaryFixed)
                              .withOpacity(0.05),
                        ],
                        begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                        end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                      ),
                    ),

class NTFCnt extends StatefulWidget {
  const NTFCnt({super.key, required this.ch, required this.data});

  final ColorScheme ch;
  final NotificationData data;

  @override
  State<NTFCnt> createState() => _NTFCntState();
}

class _NTFCntState extends State<NTFCnt> {
  bool more = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    read() {
      if (widget.data.status.toLowerCase() == "unread") {
        sharedProvider.readNotification(widget.data.id);
      }
      setState(() {
        more = !more;
      });
    }

    return InkWell(
      radius: 10,
      onTap: () => read(),
      child: Container(
        margin: EdgeInsets.only(left: 10, right: 10, bottom: 10),
        padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: widget.ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),

          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
        ),
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Padding(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4.0),
              child: Icon(
                Icons.notification_important_outlined,
                size: 15,
                color: widget.ch.onSurface.withOpacity(0.3),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 8),
            Expanded(
              child: Column(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Row(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          widget.data.data.title,
                          style: ptstyle(
                            size: 12,
                            weight: 600,
                            color: widget.ch.onSurface.withOpacity(0.5),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),

                      if (widget.data.status.toLowerCase() == "unread")
                        Icon(
                          Icons.circle,
                          size: 10.0,
                          color: widget.ch.primary,
                        ),
                    ],
                  ),
                  Text(
                    widget.data.data.message,
                    maxLines: more ? null : 1,
                    overflow: more ? null : TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: ptstyle(
                      size: 12,
                      weight: 400,
                      color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                    ),
                  ),
                  Row(
                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                    children: [
                      Expanded(
                        child: Text(
                          widget.data.status.toLowerCase() == "unread"
                              ? "...${DateTime.parse(widget.data.createdAt).toAgo()}"
                              : DateTime.parse(
                                widget.data.createdAt,
                              ).formatDate(),
                          maxLines: 1,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: ptstyle(
                            size: 12,
                            weight: 400,
                            color: widget.ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(
                        height: 25,
                        child: TextButton(
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5),
                          ),
                          onPressed: () => read(),
                          child: Text(
                            more
                                ? "collapse"
                                : (widget.data.status.toLowerCase() == "unread"
                                    ? "Read"
                                    : "Expand"),
                            style: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 600),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\screens\layout\components\bottom_nav.dart ===================
// lib/widgets/custom_bottom_nav_bar.dart

import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class CustomBottomNavBar extends StatelessWidget {
  final int currentIndex;
  final bool ofl;
  final Function(int) onTap;
  final List cartItems;

  /// TODO: backend not ready — this is driven by
  /// SharedProvider.isAgentAccount, which is currently hardcoded.
  /// Once the backend exposes a real "is this user an agent" flag,
  /// nothing here needs to change — just the getter it reads from.
  final bool showAgentTab;
  final VoidCallback? onAgentTap;

  const CustomBottomNavBar({
    super.key,
    required this.currentIndex,
    required this.onTap,
    required this.cartItems,
    required this.ofl,
    this.showAgentTab = false,
    this.onAgentTap,
  });

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    double sz = 30;

    // Agent tab only ever shows for logged-in users.
    final bool includeAgentTab = showAgentTab && !ofl;
    // Index of the agent tab, once ofl's normal items are accounted for.
    final int agentTabIndex = ofl ? 3 : 5;

    return BottomNavigationBar(
      elevation: ofl ? 0 : null,
      backgroundColor:
          AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
              ? ch.surface
              : const Color.fromARGB(255, 250, 252, 255),
      currentIndex: currentIndex,
      onTap: (index) {
        if (includeAgentTab && index == agentTabIndex) {
          onAgentTap?.call();
          return;
        }
        onTap(index);
      },
      type: BottomNavigationBarType.fixed,
      enableFeedback: false,

      unselectedLabelStyle: ptstyle(size: 10, weight: 300),
      selectedLabelStyle: ptstyle(size: 11, weight: 400),
      selectedItemColor: ch.primary,
      unselectedItemColor: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7),
      items: [
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.home, size: sz),
          label: 'Home',
        ),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Icon(Icons.dashboard, size: sz),
          label: 'Browse',
        ),
        BottomNavigationBarItem(
          icon: Badge(
            isLabelVisible: cartItems.isNotEmpty,
            alignment: Alignment.topRight,
            label: Text(
              cartItems.length.toString(),
              style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 200),
            ),
            child: Icon(Icons.shopping_cart, size: sz),
          ),
          label: 'Cart',
        ),
        if (!ofl) ...[
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.receipt_long, size: sz),
            label: 'Order',
          ),
          const BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.person_outline),
            label: 'Profile',
          ),
        ],
        if (includeAgentTab)
          BottomNavigationBarItem(
            icon: Icon(Icons.workspace_premium_outlined, size: sz),
            label: 'Agent',
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

                          weight: 600,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),

                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.transparent,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),

                      child: Text(

                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 600),
                      ),

                        focusNode: ghCardFocusNode2,
                        enabled: !sharedProvider.ghCardVrLoading,
                        maxLength: 9,
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          vertical: 0,
                          horizontal: 2,
                        ),
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        label: "",
                        hintText: "0 0 0 0 0 0 0 0 0",
                        controller: sharedProvider.ghCardEditingController,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 16,
                          weight: 600,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                        onChange: (x) {
                          setState(() {
                            sharedProvider.ghCardValidation =
                                x.isEmpty
                                    ? false
                                    : !"$x-${sharedProvider.ghCardEditingController2.text.trim()}"
                                        .isValidGhanaCardDigits;
                          });

                          if (x.length == 9) {
                            ghCardFocusNode.requestFocus();
                          }
                        },
                        error: sharedProvider.ghCardValidation,
                      ),
                    ),

                    Container(
                      height: 55,
                      alignment: Alignment.center,
                      padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        color: Colors.transparent,
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),

                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 600),
                      ),
                    ),

                    SizedBox(
                      width: 40,
                      child: FilledInput(
                        maxLength: 1,
                        focusNode: ghCardFocusNode,
                        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                          vertical: 0,
                          horizontal: 0,
                        ),
                        enabled: !sharedProvider.ghCardVrLoading,
                        label: "",
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 16,
                          weight: 600,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        hintText: "0",
                        controller: sharedProvider.ghCardEditingController2,
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.number,
                        onChange: (x) {
                          setState(() {
                            sharedProvider.ghCardValidation =
                                x.isEmpty
                                    ? false
                                    : !"${sharedProvider.ghCardEditingController.text.trim()}-$x"
                                        .isValidGhanaCardDigits;
                          });
                          if (x.length == 1) {
                            ghCardFocusNode.unfocus();
                          }
                          if (x.isEmpty) {
                            ghCardFocusNode2.requestFocus();
                          }
                        },
                        error: sharedProvider.ghCardValidation,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 30),

              SizedBox(
                height: 47,
                child: SmartButton(
                  enabled:
                      !sharedProvider.ghCardValidation &&
                      sharedProvider.ghCardEditingController.text.isNotEmpty,
                  loading: sharedProvider.ghCardVrLoading,
                  onPressed: () => sharedProvider.validateGHC(context),
                  child: Text("Verify", style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400)),
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\screens\onboarding\verification_success.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/assets_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/layout/layout.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/smart_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:lottie/lottie.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class VerificationSuccess extends StatelessWidget {
  const VerificationSuccess({super.key});

  @override
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    return Scaffold(
      body: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.all(20.0),
        child: ListView(
          children: [
            SizedBox(height: 80),

            Center(
              child: Lottie.asset(
                AssetsHelper.checkLottie,
                repeat: false,
                reverse: true,
                height: 250,
                width: 250,
                fit: BoxFit.contain,
              ),
            ),

            SizedBox(height: 20),

            Text(
              "Ghana Card verified",
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: ptstyle(size: 25, weight: 600),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              "You have successfully linked your Ghana Card, proceed to make an order now",
              textAlign: TextAlign.center,
              style: ptstyle(
                size: 12,
                weight: 300,
                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 50),
            SizedBox(
              height: 47,
              child: SmartButton(
                enabled: true,
                loading: false,
                onPressed: () {
                  NavigationHelper.pushAndRemoveUntil(context, HomePage());
                },
                child: Text("Continue", style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400)),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}


=================== FILE: lib\screens\onboarding\agent\agent_application_success.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/bgw.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/smart_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AgentApplicationSuccess extends StatelessWidget {
  const AgentApplicationSuccess({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ch.surface,
      appBar: AppBar(automaticallyImplyLeading: false, toolbarHeight: 20),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          Bgw(),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24.0),
            child: Column(
              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
              children: [
                Container(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    color: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),
                    shape: BoxShape.circle,
                  ),
                  child: Icon(
                    Icons.check_circle_outline_rounded,
                    size: 60,
                    color: ch.primary,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 24),
                Text(
                  "Application Submitted!",
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: ptstyle(size: 20, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurface),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text(
                  "Thanks for applying to become a FlexiPhones agent. "
                  "We're reviewing your details and will reach out shortly "
                  "with next steps.",
                  textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 12,
                    weight: 300,
                    color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 40),
                SizedBox(
                  height: 47,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: SmartButton(
                    enabled: true,
                    loading: false,
                    onPressed: () {
                      NavigationHelper.pop(context);
                      NavigationHelper.pop(context);
                    },
                    child: Text(
                      "Back to Profile",
                      style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

=================== FILE: lib\screens\onboarding\agent\agent_landing.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/agent/agent_signup.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/email_phone/email_signin.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';

class AgentLanding extends StatelessWidget {
  const AgentLanding({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final benefits = const [
      {
        "icon": Icons.storefront_outlined,
        "title": "Vendors you onboard",
        "subtitle": "up to 9% commission",
      },
      {
        "icon": Icons.phone_iphone_rounded,
        "title": "Customer downloads",
        "subtitle": "points + cash rewards",
      },
      {
        "icon": Icons.schedule_rounded,
        "title": "Payouts to MTN MoMo",
        "subtitle": "weekly, no minimum",
      },
    ];

    return Scaffold(
      body: Container(
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          gradient: LinearGradient(
            begin: Alignment.topRight,
            end: Alignment.bottomLeft,
            colors: [Color(0xFF120A06), Color(0xFF3B2011), Color(0xFF1A0F08)],
          ),
        ),
        child: SafeArea(
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 24),
            child: Column(
              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
              children: [
                SizedBox(height: 6),
                IconButton(
                  onPressed: () => NavigationHelper.pop(context),
                  icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: Colors.white70),
                  padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 26),
                Text(
                  "AGENT PROGRAMME",
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 11,
                    weight: 700,
                    color: Colors.orangeAccent.withOpacity(0.9),
                  ).copyWith(letterSpacing: 3),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 16),
                Text(
                  "Earn from every phone you help move.",
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 30,
                    weight: 700,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ).copyWith(height: 1.2),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 14),
                Text(
                  "Sign up vendors. Refer customers. Get paid on every device financed through your link.",
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 13,
                    weight: 300,
                    color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
                  ).copyWith(height: 1.4),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 30),
                ...benefits.map(
                  (b) => Container(
                    margin: EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 12),
                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(14),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.06),
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
                      border: Border.all(
                        color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.08),
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Row(
                      children: [
                        Container(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.all(10),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.orangeAccent.withOpacity(0.15),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          ),
                          child: Icon(
                            b["icon"] as IconData,
                            color: Colors.orangeAccent,
                            size: 20,
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(width: 14),
                        Column(
                          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              b["title"] as String,
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 13,
                                weight: 600,
                                color: Colors.white,
                              ),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(height: 2),
                            Text(
                              b["subtitle"] as String,
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 11,
                                weight: 300,
                                color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.6),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                Spacer(),
                SizedBox(
                  height: 52,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  child: ElevatedButton(
                    style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                      backgroundColor: Colors.white,
                      shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
                      ),
                      elevation: 0,
                    ),
                    onPressed:
                        () => NavigationHelper.push(context, AgentSignup()),
                    child: Text(
                      "Become an agent",
                      style: ptstyle(
                        size: 14,
                        weight: 700,
                        color: Colors.black,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 16),
                Center(
                  child: GestureDetector(
                    onTap:
                        () => NavigationHelper.push(context, EmailSignin()),
                    child: RichText(
                      text: TextSpan(
                        text: "Already an agent? ",
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 12,
                          weight: 300,
                          color: Colors.white.withOpacity(0.7),
                        ),
                        children: [
                          TextSpan(
                            text: "Sign in",
                            style: ptstyle(
                              size: 12,
                              weight: 700,
                              color: Colors.orangeAccent,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 24),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

=================== FILE: lib\screens\onboarding\agent\agent_signup.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/value_validation.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/bgw.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/input_field.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

const _agentGreen = Color(0xFF12362C);

class AgentSignup extends StatefulWidget {
  const AgentSignup({super.key});

  @override
  State<AgentSignup> createState() => _AgentSignupState();
}

class _AgentSignupState extends State<AgentSignup> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
      Provider.of<SharedProvider>(
        context,
        listen: false,
      ).prefillAgentFieldsFromUser();
    });
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    final ghanaCardLinked = sharedProvider.agentGhanaCardAlreadyLinked;

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ch.surface,
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          Bgw(),
          SafeArea(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.all(24),
              children: [
                IconButton(
                  onPressed: () => Navigator.pop(context),
                  icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_back, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
                  padding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                  alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 10),
                Text(
                  "Become an Agent",
                  style: ptstyle(size: 24, weight: 700, color: ch.onSurface),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 6),
                Text(
                  "We need this to pay you and stay compliant. All agent features are free — no plans.",
                  style: ptstyle(
                    size: 12,
                    weight: 300,
                    color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 26),
                Form(
                  key: sharedProvider.agentFormKey,
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      OutlineInput(
                        controller: sharedProvider.agentFullNameController,
                        label: "Full name",
                        hintText: "eg: Kwame Adjei",
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.text,
                        validator: (value) {
                          if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                            return "Please enter your full name";
                          }
                          return null;
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      Text(
                        "Phone (MTN MoMo receives payouts)",
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 11,
                          weight: 400,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 6),
                      Row(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Container(
                            height: 50,
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              border: Border.all(
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.3),
                              ),
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                            ),
                            child: Row(
                              children: [
                                Text("🇬🇭", style: TextStyle(fontSize: 16)),
                                SizedBox(width: 6),
                                Text(
                                  "+233",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 13,
                                    weight: 400,
                                    color: ch.onSurface,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                          SizedBox(width: 10),
                          Expanded(
                            child: OutlineInput(
                              controller: sharedProvider.agentPhoneController,
                              label: "",
                              hintText: "24 555 0181",
                              maxLength: 9,
                              keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
                              validator: (value) {
                                if (value == null ||
                                    value.isEmpty ||
                                    !value.isValidPhoneNumber) {
                                  return "Invalid 9 digit phone number";
                                }
                                return null;
                              },
                            ),
                          ),
                        ],
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      OutlineInput(
                        controller: sharedProvider.agentRegionController,
                        label: "Region",
                        hintText: "eg: Kumasi",
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.text,
                        validator: (value) {
                          if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                            return "Please enter your region";
                          }
                          return null;
                        },
                      ),
                      const SizedBox(height: 16),
                      if (ghanaCardLinked)
                        Container(
                          padding: EdgeInsets.all(14),
                          decoration: BoxDecoration(
                            color: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.08),
                            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            border: Border.all(
                              color: Colors.green.withOpacity(0.3),
                            ),
                          ),
                          child: Row(
                            children: [
                              Icon(
                                Icons.verified_rounded,
                                color: Colors.green,
                                size: 20,
                              ),
                              SizedBox(width: 10),
                              Expanded(
                                child: Text(
                                  "Ghana Card already linked to your account — no need to resend it.",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 12,
                                    weight: 400,
                                    color: ch.onSurface,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        )
                      else ...[
                        OutlineInput(
                          controller: sharedProvider.agentGhanaCardController,
                          label: "Ghana Card number",
                          hintText: "GHA-XXXXXXXXX-X",
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.text,
                        ),
                        const SizedBox(height: 16),
                        Text(
                          "Ghana Card photo",
                          style: ptstyle(
                            size: 11,
                            weight: 400,
                            color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 6),
                        InkWell(
                          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                          onTap: sharedProvider.agentPhotoPicking
                              ? null
                              : () => sharedProvider.pickGhanaCardPhoto(),
                          child: Container(
                            width: double.infinity,
                            padding: EdgeInsets.all(18),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                              border: Border.all(
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.3),
                              ),
                            ),
                            child: sharedProvider.agentGhanaCardPhoto != null
                                ? Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Icon(
                                        Icons.check_circle,
                                        color: Colors.green,
                                      ),
                                      SizedBox(width: 10),
                                      Text(
                                        "Photo selected",
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                          size: 12,
                                          weight: 500,
                                          color: ch.onSurface,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  )
                                : Row(
                                    children: [
                                      Icon(
                                        Icons.upload_rounded,
                                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                      ),
                                      SizedBox(width: 10),
                                      Column(
                                        crossAxisAlignment:
                                            CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                                        children: [
                                          Text(
                                            "Tap to upload",
                                            style: ptstyle(
                                              size: 12,
                                              weight: 600,
                                              color: ch.onSurface,
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                          Text(
                                            "Front side, clear, well-lit",
                                            style: ptstyle(
                                              size: 11,
                                              weight: 300,
                                              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                            ),
                                          ),
                                        ],
                                      ),
                                    ],
                                  ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                      const SizedBox(height: 34),
                      SizedBox(
                        height: 50,
                        width: double.infinity,
                        child: ElevatedButton(
                          style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                            backgroundColor: _agentGreen,
                            shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
                            ),
                            elevation: 0,
                          ),
                          onPressed: sharedProvider.agentSubmitting
                              ? null
                              : () =>
                                  sharedProvider.submitAgentApplication(
                                    context,
                                  ),
                          child: sharedProvider.agentSubmitting
                              ? SizedBox(
                                  height: 20,
                                  width: 20,
                                  child: CircularProgressIndicator(
                                    strokeWidth: 2,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                  ),
                                )
                              : Text(
                                  "Submit Application",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 13,
                                    weight: 700,
                                    color: Colors.white,
                                  ),
                                ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/value_validation.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/assets_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/email_phone/init_ss_screen.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/onboarding/forgot_password.dart';

import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/glass_card.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/input_field.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/smart_button.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class EmailSignin extends StatefulWidget {
  const EmailSignin({super.key});

  @override
  State<EmailSignin> createState() => _EmailSigninState();
}

class _EmailSigninState extends State<EmailSignin> {
  final formKey_1 = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  bool showPass = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    return Scaffold(
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      extendBody: true,
      appBar: AppBar(
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        toolbarHeight: 45,
        iconTheme: IconThemeData(color: ch.onSurface),
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          // Your existing wave background, unchanged
          Bgw(),

          // Decorative glow orbs
          Positioned(
            top: 60,
            right: -50,
            child: GlowOrb(color: ch.primary, size: 200),
          ),
          Positioned(
            bottom: 80,
            left: -60,
            child: GlowOrb(color: ch.primaryFixed, size: 220),
          ),

          SafeArea(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 10, 20, 30),
              children: [
                Center(
                  child: Image.asset(
                    AssetsHelper.signinImage,
                    height: 160,
                    width: 180,
                    fit: BoxFit.contain,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 6),
                Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        "Sign In",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 26,
                          weight: 700,
                          color: ch.onSurface,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text(
                        "Enter your email and password to sign in to your flexiphones account",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 12,
                          weight: 400,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 24),

                GlassCard(
                  child: Form(
                    key: formKey_1,
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                      children: [
                        OutlineInput(
                          focusNode: sharedProvider.emailFocusNode,
                          enabled: !sharedProvider.emailVerification,
                          label: "Your Email",
                          hintText: "eg: name@example.com ...",
                          controller: sharedProvider.emailEditingController,
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.emailAddress,
                          validator: (value) {
                            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                              return "Please enter your email";
                            }
                            if (!value.isValidEmail) {
                              return "Invalid Email provided";
                            }
                            return null;
                          },
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 16),
                        OutlineInput(
                          controller: sharedProvider.passwordController,
                          label: "Password",
                          hintText: "Enter your password",
                          keyboardType: TextInputType.text,
                          obscureText: !showPass,
                          suffixIcon: Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(5.0),
                            child: IconButton(
                              onPressed: () {
                                setState(() {
                                  showPass = !showPass;
                                });
                              },
                              style:
                                  IconButton.styleFrom(padding: EdgeInsets.zero),
                              icon: AnimatedSwitcher(
                                duration: Duration(milliseconds: 400),
                                child: showPass
                                    ? Icon(Icons.visibility_off_outlined)
                                    : Icon(Icons.visibility_outlined),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                          validator: (value) {
                            if (value == null || value.isEmpty) {
                              return "Please enter a password";
                            }
                            return null;
                          },
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 20),
                        SizedBox(
                          height: 48,
                          child: SmartButton(
                            enabled: !sharedProvider.userUDLoading,
                            loading: sharedProvider.userUDLoading,
                            onPressed: () {
                              FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
                              if (formKey_1.currentState!.validate()) {
                                sharedProvider.emailSignIn(context);
                              }
                            },
                            child: Text(
                              "Continue",
                              style: ptstyle(size: 13, weight: 600),
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 10),

                        // FIX: was a Row (fixed width, no wrapping) — on
                        // narrower phones "Forgotten Password?" + " or " +
                        // "Sign Up Instead?" didn't fit on one line, which
                        // triggered Flutter's yellow/black overflow stripes.
                        // Wrap drops the second link to a new line instead
                        // of overflowing.
                        Wrap(
                          alignment: WrapAlignment.center,
                          crossAxisAlignment: WrapCrossAlignment.center,
                          spacing: 2,
                          runSpacing: 0,
                          children: [
                            TextButton(
                              style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 6,
                                  vertical: 4,
                                ),
                                minimumSize: Size.zero,
                                tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                              ),
                              onPressed: () {
                                NavigationHelper.push(
                                  context,
                                  ForgotPassword(),
                                );
                              },
                              child: Text(
                                "Forgotten Password?",
                                style: ptstyle(
                                  size: 12,
                                  weight: 600,
                                  color: ch.primary,
                                  decoration: TextDecoration.underline,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                            Text(
                              "or",
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 12,
                                weight: 600,
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              ),
                            ),
                            TextButton(
                              style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                  horizontal: 6,
                                  vertical: 4,
                                ),
                                minimumSize: Size.zero,
                                tapTargetSize: MaterialTapTargetSize.shrinkWrap,
                              ),
                              onPressed: () {
                                NavigationHelper.push(
                                  context,
                                  InitUserSSScreen(),
                                );
                              },
                              child: Text(
                                "Sign Up Instead?",
                                style: ptstyle(
                                  size: 12,
                                  weight: 600,
                                  color: ch.primary,
                                  decoration: TextDecoration.underline,
                                ),
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class InitUserSSScreen extends StatefulWidget {
  const InitUserSSScreen({super.key});

  @override
  State<InitUserSSScreen> createState() => _SignupState();
}

class _SignupState extends State<InitUserSSScreen> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    offline();
    super.initState();
  }

  Future<void> offline() async {
    final sharedProvider =
        Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context, listen: false);
    await DS.storage.deleteAll();
    sharedProvider.userData = null;
    await (await SharedPreferences.getInstance()).clear();
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    return Scaffold(
      extendBodyBehindAppBar: true,
      appBar: AppBar(
        automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
        excludeHeaderSemantics: true,
        backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        toolbarHeight: 0,
      ),
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          // Your existing wave background, unchanged
          Bgw(),

          // Decorative glow orbs — this is what the glass "catches"
          Positioned(
            top: -60,
            left: -40,
            child: GlowOrb(color: ch.primary, size: 220),
          ),
          Positioned(
            top: 160,
            right: -60,
            child: GlowOrb(color: ch.primaryFixed, size: 180),
          ),

          SafeArea(
            child: ListView(
              padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(20, 20, 20, 40),
              children: [
                SizedBox(height: 20),
                Center(
                  child: Image.asset(
                    AssetsHelper.signupImage,
                    height: 150,
                    width: 170,
                    fit: BoxFit.contain,
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 10),
                Center(
                  child: Column(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        "Welcome To FlexiPhones",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 24,
                          weight: 700,
                          color: ch.onSurface,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 8),
                      Text(
                        "Sign in and continue with flexiphones",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 12,
                          weight: 400,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
                SizedBox(height: 30),

                GlassCard(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
                    children: [
                      Text(
                        "Already have an account?",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 13,
                          weight: 600,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 14),
                      OutlinedButton.icon(
                        style: OutlinedButton.styleFrom(
                          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 14),
                          backgroundColor: ch.surface.withOpacity(0.35),
                          side: BorderSide(color: ch.primary.withOpacity(0.6)),

class PhoneSigninX extends StatefulWidget {

class _PhoneSigninXState extends State<PhoneSigninX> {
  final formKey_1 = GlobalKey<FormState>();
  bool showPass = false;
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

      onTap: () {

      },

                      ),

                      Center(
                        child: Column(

                              "Verify Phone No.",
                              textAlign: TextAlign.center,

                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 30),
                      CountryCodePhoneInput(
                        controller: sharedProvider.phoneController,
                        hintText: "Enter your phone number",
                        keyboardType: TextInputType.phone,
      
                        onChanged: (value) {},
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 20),
                      Text(
                        "We will send you a One Time Password (OTP) to verify your phone number.",
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 12,
                          weight: 200,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                        ),
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 20),
                      SizedBox(
                        height: 47,
                        width: double.infinity,
      
                        child: SmartButton(
                          enabled: !sharedProvider.userUDLoading,
                          loading: sharedProvider.userUDLoading,
                          onPressed: () {
                            FocusScope.of(context).unfocus();
                            if (formKey_1.currentState!.validate()) {
                              sharedProvider.phoneSignin(context);
                            }
                          },
                          child: Text(
