=================== FILE: lib\screens\orders\orders.dart ===================
import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/value_formating.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/assets_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/st_values.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/models/order_m.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/orders/sub_pages/order_details.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/profile/sub_pages/repayment_calendar.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/loadingList.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/space_divider.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/sum_card.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:grouped_list/grouped_list.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';
import 'package:visibility_detector/visibility_detector.dart';

class Orders extends StatefulWidget {
  const Orders({super.key});

  @override
  State<Orders> createState() => _OrdersState();
}

class _OrdersState extends State<Orders> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    List getOrders() =>
        sharedProvider.showP == 3
            ? sharedProvider.ordersStatusDelivered
            : sharedProvider.showP == 2
            ? sharedProvider.ordersStatusCancelled
            : sharedProvider.showP == 1
            ? sharedProvider.ordersStatusPlaced
            : sharedProvider.orders;
    final list = getOrders();
    list.sort(
      (a, b) =>
          DateTime.parse(a.createdAt).compareTo(DateTime.parse(b.createdAt)),
    );

    return Scaffold(
      extendBody: true,
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      floatingActionButton: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 20),
        child: Row(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            InkWell(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              onTap: () => NavigationHelper.push(context, RepaymentCalendar()),
              child: Material(
                color: ch.primary,
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                ),
                elevation: 2,
                child: Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10.0),
                  child: Row(
                    mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                    children: [
                      Icon(Icons.list_rounded, color: ch.onPrimary, size: 18),
                      SizedBox(width: 5),
                      Text(
                        "Repayments",
                        style: ptstyle(
                          size: 12,
                          weight: 600,
                          color: ch.onPrimary,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
            SizedBox(width: 10),
            Opacity(
              opacity: sharedProvider.refreshing_ ? 0.4 : 1,
              child: AbsorbPointer(
                absorbing: sharedProvider.refreshing_,
                child: IconButton.filled(
                  onPressed: () {
                    sharedProvider.moreData(
                      type: RefreshParam.orders,
                      context: context,
                      refresh: true,
                    );
                  },
                  icon: Icon(Icons.refresh_rounded, size: 20),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),

      body: Stack(
        alignment: Alignment.bottomCenter,
        children: [
          Column(
            children: [
              SizedBox(height: 10),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    SumCard(
                      onTap: () => sharedProvider.switchOrders(0),
                      enable: sharedProvider.showP == 0,
                      ch: ch,
                      clk: true,
                      value: sharedProvider.orders.length.toString(),
                      title: "All Orders",
                    ),
                    SizedBox(width: 10),
                    SumCard(
                      onTap: () => sharedProvider.switchOrders(1),
                      enable: sharedProvider.showP == 1,
                      ch: ch,
                      clk: true,
                      value:
                          sharedProvider.ordersStatusPlaced.length.toString(),
                      title: "Placed",
                    ),
                    SizedBox(width: 10),
                    SumCard(
                      onTap: () => sharedProvider.switchOrders(2),
                      enable: sharedProvider.showP == 2,
                      ch: ch,
                      clk: true,
                      value:
                          sharedProvider.ordersStatusCancelled.length
                              .toString(),
                      title: "Cancelled",
                    ),
                    SizedBox(width: 10),
                    SumCard(
                      onTap: () => sharedProvider.switchOrders(3),
                      enable: sharedProvider.showP == 3,
                      ch: ch,
                      clk: true,
                      value:
                          sharedProvider.ordersStatusDelivered.length
                              .toString(),
                      title: "Delivered",
                    ),
                    //ordersStatusDelivered
                  ],
                ),
              ),

              SizedBox(height: 20),

              getOrders().isEmpty
                  ? Center(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                      children: [
                        SizedBox(height: 50),
                        Icon(
                          Icons.info_rounded,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.1),
                          size: 35,
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 10),
                        Text(
                          "No Orders",
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          style: ptstyle(
                            size: 15,
                            weight: 400,
                            color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  )
                  : Expanded(
                    child: GroupedListView(
                      elements: getOrders(),
                      groupBy: (element) => element.createdAt,
                      footer: VisibilityDetector(
                        onVisibilityChanged: (info) {
                          if (!sharedProvider.refreshing_) {
                            sharedProvider.moreData(
                              type: RefreshParam.orders,
                              context: context,
                            );
                          }
                        },
                        key: UniqueKey(),
                        child: SizedBox(height: 50),
                      ),
                      groupHeaderBuilder:
                          (groupByValue) => Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8.0),
                            child: Row(
                              crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Text(
                                  "Order Id  ",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 10,
                                    weight: 600,
                                    color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Text(
                                  (groupByValue).applicationUuid ?? "~",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 10,
                                    weight: 600,
                                    color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                  ),
                                ),

                                SizedBox(width: 10),

                                Expanded(child: SDivider()),
                                Material(
                                  color: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),
                                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
                                  ),
                                  child: Padding(
                                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      horizontal: 5,
                                      vertical: 2,
                                    ),

                                    child: Text(
                                      DateTime.parse(
                                        (groupByValue).createdAt,
                                      ).formatDateNoTime(),
                                      style: ptstyle(
                                        size: 10,
                                        weight: 400,
                                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                      itemBuilder: (context, item) {
                        //  final item = getOrders()[index]; // Get the current map
                        return OrderCard(item: item);
                      },
                      itemComparator:
                          (item1, item2) => (item1.applicationUuid ?? "MK")
                              .compareTo(item2.applicationUuid ?? "MK"),
                      useStickyGroupSeparators: true, // optional
                      floatingHeader: true, // optional
                      order: GroupedListOrder.DESC, // optional
                      groupStickyHeaderBuilder:
                          (element) => Container(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
                            decoration: BoxDecoration(color: ch.surface),
                            child: Row(
                              mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                              children: [
                                Expanded(child: SDivider()),
                                Material(
                                  color: ch.primaryFixed,
                                  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
                                  ),
                                  child: Padding(
                                    padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                      horizontal: 6.0,
                                      vertical: 1,
                                    ),
                                    child: Text(
                                      DateTime.parse(
                                        (element).createdAt,
                                      ).formatDate(),
                                      style: ptstyle(
                                        size: 10,
                                        weight: 600,
                                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ),
                                Expanded(child: SDivider()),
                              ],
                            ),
                          ),
                    ),
                  ),
            ],
          ),

          if (sharedProvider.refreshing_) LoadingListIndicator(),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class OrderCard extends StatelessWidget {
  final Order item;

  const OrderCard({super.key, required this.item});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return InkWell(
      onTap: () {
        sharedProvider.getOrder(item.id);
        NavigationHelper.push(context, OrderDetailsWidget());
      },
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10, vertical: 8),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Row(
              children: [
                Card(
                  color:
                      AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                          ? ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3)
                          : ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.2),
                  elevation: 0,
                  child: ClipRRect(
                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                    child: Image.network(
                      item.product.image,
                      width: 70,
                      height: 70,
                      fit: BoxFit.contain,
                      errorBuilder:
                          (context, error, stackTrace) => Padding(
                            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(5.0),
                            child: Opacity(
                              opacity:
                                  AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                      ? 0.2
                                      : 0.6,
                              child: Image.asset(
                                AssetsHelper.dummyImage,
                                fit: BoxFit.contain,
                                width: 70,
                                height: 70,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ),

                const SizedBox(width: 10),
                Expanded(
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Row(

=================== FILE: lib\screens\orders\components\call_details.dart ===================
import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/ui_values.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:url_launcher/url_launcher.dart';

void showVendorBottomSheet(BuildContext context, List vendorDetails) {
  var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
  void callPhone(tel) async {
    final Uri phoneUri = Uri(scheme: 'tel', path: tel);

    if (await canLaunchUrl(phoneUri)) {
      await launchUrl(phoneUri);
    } else {
      throw 'Could not launch $phoneUri';
    }
  }

  showModalBottomSheet(
    context: context,
    builder: (context) {
      return SizedBox(
        height: 20.h,
        child: Expanded(
          child: Column(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
            children: [
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 20, right: 20, top: 15),
                child: Text(
                  'Vendor(s) Contact(s)',
                  style: ptstyle(size: 16, weight: 400),
                ),
              ),

              Expanded(
                child: ListView.separated(
                  separatorBuilder:
                      (context, index) => Divider(
                        height: 0,
                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.05),
                      ),
                  itemCount: vendorDetails.length,
                  itemBuilder: (context, index) {
                    final vendor = vendorDetails[index];
                    return ListTile(
                      onTap: () => callPhone(vendor['contact']),
                      title: Text(
                        vendor['name'],
                        style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 600),
                      ),
                      subtitle: Text(
                        vendor['contact'],
                        style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400),
                      ),
                      leading: Icon(
                        Icons.phone_outlined,
                        size: 20,
                        color: ch.primary,
                      ),
                      trailing: Icon(
                        Icons.account_circle,
                        size: 30,
                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.2),
                      ),
                    );
                  },
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      );
    },
  );
}

class OrderDetailsWidget extends StatefulWidget {
  const OrderDetailsWidget({super.key});

  @override
  State<OrderDetailsWidget> createState() => _OrderDetailsWidgetState();
}

class _OrderDetailsWidgetState extends State<OrderDetailsWidget> {
  //      order["statusTimeline"].where((x) => x["completed"] == false).isEmpty;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    related() =>
        sharedProvider.orders
            .where(
              (x) =>
                  x.applicationUuid ==
                      sharedProvider.orderData!.applicationUuid &&
                  x.id != sharedProvider.orderData!.id &&
                  x.status.toLowerCase() == "pending",
            )
            .toList()
            .map((x) => x.product.fullName)
            .toList();
    var deliveryOption =
        sharedProvider.loadingOrder
            ? ""
            : (sharedProvider.orderData?.deliveryOption ?? "pickup")
                .toUpperCase();

    final status = sharedProvider.orderData?.orderStatus.toLowerCase() ?? '';
    final address = sharedProvider.orderData?.deliveryAddress ?? '...';
    final vendor = sharedProvider.orderData?.vendor.name;
    final pickup = deliveryOption.toLowerCase() == 'pickup';

    final message =
        pickup
            ? "${status == 'delivered' ? "You've received" : "Pickup"} your device at $vendor"
            : "Your device ${status == 'delivered' ? "has been delivered" : "will be delivered"} to you at $address by $vendor";

    return Scaffold(
      backgroundColor: ch.surface,
      appBar: AppBar(
        flexibleSpace:
            sharedProvider.loadingOrder
                ? null
                : Container(
                  decoration: BoxDecoration(
                    gradient: LinearGradient(
                      colors: [
                        STV
                            .getDeviceStatusColor(
                              sharedProvider.orderData!.status,
                            )
                            .withOpacity(0.5),
                        STV
                            .getDeviceStatusColor(
                              sharedProvider.orderData!.status,
                            )
                            .withOpacity(0.2),
                        STV
                            .getDeviceStatusColor(
                              sharedProvider.orderData!.status,
                            )
                            .withOpacity(0.01),
                      ],
                      begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                      end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
        excludeHeaderSemantics: true,
        backgroundColor: ch.surface,
        elevation: 0,
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        title: Text(
          "Order Details",
          style: ptstyle(size: 14, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
        ),
        centerTitle: true,

        actions:
            sharedProvider.loadingOrder
                ? []
                : [
                  Container(
                    padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 5, vertical: 2),
                    margin: EdgeInsets.only(right: 10),
                    decoration: BoxDecoration(
                      borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(9),
                      color: STV.getDeliveryStatusColor(
                        sharedProvider.orderData!.orderStatus,
                        ch,
                      ),
                    ),
                    child: Text(
                      "• ${sharedProvider.orderData!.orderStatus.toSentenceCase()} ",
                      style: ptstyle(
                        size: 12,
                        weight: 600,
                        color: Colors.white,
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
      ),
      body:
          sharedProvider.loadingOrder
              ? PDShimmer(ch: ch)
              : ListView(
                children: [
                  const SizedBox(height: 10),
                  _buildProductCard(
                    context,
                    ch,
                    sharedProvider.orderData!,
                    sharedProvider,

                  ],

                    child: Column(

                                    borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                                    side: BorderSide(
                                      color:
                                          AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                              ? ch.primaryFixed
                                              : const Color.fromARGB(
                                                255,
                                                255,
                                                213,
                                                210,
                                              ),
                                      width: 1,
                                    ),
                                  ),

                                  // surfaceTintColor: Colors.redAccent,
                                  // overlayColor: Colors.redAccent,
                                  backgroundColor:
                                      AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                          ? ch.primaryFixed
                                          : const Color.fromARGB(
                                            255,
                                            255,
                                            242,
                                            241,
                                          ),
                                ),
                                onPressed: () async {
                                  showConfirmationDialog(
                                    context: context,
                                    title: "Are you sure?",
                                    content:
                                        "Your ${sharedProvider.orderData!.product.model} order will be cancelled.",
                                    confirmText: "Yes, Cancel",
                                    cancelText: "No",
                                    confirm:
                                        () => sharedProvider.removeOrder(
                                          sharedProvider.orderData!.id,
                                          context,
                                        ),
                                  );
                                },
                                icon: Icon(
                                  Icons.cancel_outlined,
                                  color: Colors.red,
                                ),
                                label: Text(
                                  "Cancel Order",
                                  style: ptstyle(
                                    size: 10,
                                    weight: 500,
                                    color: Colors.red,
                                  ),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  SizedBox(height: 20),
                ],
              ),
    );
  }

  Widget _infoRow(String label, String value, ColorScheme ch) {
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Row(
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
        children: [
          Text(
            "$label:",
            style: ptstyle(size: 10, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant, weight: 400),
          ),
          Flexible(
            child: Text(
              value,
              textAlign: TextAlign.end,
              style: ptstyle(size: 10, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PDShimmer extends StatelessWidget {
  const PDShimmer({super.key, required this.ch});

  final ColorScheme ch;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Shimmer.fromColors(
      period: Duration(milliseconds: 500),
      baseColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.1),
      highlightColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),
      child: Padding(
        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 0),
        child: Column(
          children: [
            // Image Placeholder
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              height: 100,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: Colors.white,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
              ),
            ),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),

            // Title + Price
            Container(height: 100, width: double.infinity, color: Colors.white),
            const SizedBox(height: 10),

            // Specifications (3-4 lines)
            Column(
              children: List.generate(
                10,
                (index) => Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
                  child: Container(
                    height: 14,
                    width: double.infinity,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),

            const SizedBox(height: 10),

            // Pricing options
            Column(
              children: List.generate(
                8,
                (index) => Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 5.0),
                  child: Container(
                    height: 60,
                    width: double.infinity,
                    color: Colors.white,
                  ),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _ProfileState extends State<Profile> {

  /// The backend returns phone as a bare number with the country code
  /// concatenated straight on, e.g. "233250000000" — no separator, no
  /// leading "+". This just makes it readable as "+233 25 000 0000"
  /// without touching what's actually stored.
  String _formatPhone(String raw) {
    var digits = raw.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), '');
    if (digits.length < 9) return raw;
    var local = digits.substring(digits.length - 9);
    var cc = digits.substring(0, digits.length - 9);
    if (cc.isEmpty) cc = "233";
    return "+$cc ${local.substring(0, 2)} ${local.substring(2, 5)} ${local.substring(5)}";
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);

    sharedProvider.checkValidGhCard();
    return Scaffold(
      extendBody: true,
      appBar: AppBar(
        scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
        elevation: 0,
        automaticallyImplyLeading: false,
        backgroundColor: ch.surface.withOpacity(0.8),
        toolbarHeight: 10,
      ),
      backgroundColor: Colors.transparent,
      body: Stack(
        children: [
          Bgw2(),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.all(0.0),
            child: Column(
              children: [
                Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Container(
                      margin: EdgeInsets.only(right: 20, left: 10),
                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(100),
                        border: Border.all(
                          width: 2,
                          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.4),
                        ),
                      ),
                      child: ClipRRect(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadiusGeometry.circular(1000),
                        child: Image.asset(
                          AssetsHelper.profileIMG,
                          height: 40,
                          width: 40,
                          fit: BoxFit.contain,
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    Expanded(
                      child: Column(
                        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                        children: [
                          Text(
                            sharedProvider.userData!.data.name,
                            style: ptstyle(
                              size: 14,
                              weight: 600,
                              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                            ),
                          ),
                          Text(
                            sharedProvider.userData!.data.email,
                            style: ptstyle(
                              size: 10,
                              weight: 400,
                              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                            ),
                          ),
                          SizedBox(height: 3),
                          Row(
                            children: [
                              Icon(
                                Icons.phone_outlined,
                                size: 11,
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7),
                              ),
                              SizedBox(width: 4),
                              Text(
                                _formatPhone(
                                  sharedProvider.userData!.data.phone,
                                ),
                                style: ptstyle(
                                  size: 10,
                                  weight: 400,
                                  color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.7),
                                ),
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),

                    padding: EdgeInsets.all(0),
                    children: List.generate(pages.length, (index) {
                      var page = pages[index];

                                  ),
                                  SizedBox(width: 20),
                                  Text(
                                    page[1] as String,
                                    style: ptstyle(
                                      size: 12,
                                      weight: 400,
                                      color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                                    ),
                                  ),
                                ],
                              ),
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  if (page[3] as bool)
                                    Container(
                                      margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8.0),
                                      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                                        horizontal: 10,
                                        vertical: 1,
                                      ),
                                      decoration: BoxDecoration(
                                        color:
                                            sharedProvider
                                                        .userData!
                                                        .data
                                                        .ghanaCard !=
                                                    null
                                                ? Colors.greenAccent
                                                : Colors.red.withOpacity(0.1),
                                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(80),
                                      ),
                                      child: Text(
                                        sharedProvider
                                                    .userData!
                                                    .data
                                                    .ghanaCard !=
                                                null
                                            ? "Linked!"
                                            : "Unlinked",
                                        style: ptstyle(
                                          size: 12,
                                          weight: 600,
                                          color:
                                              sharedProvider
                                                          .userData!
                                                          .data
                                                          .ghanaCard !=
                                                      null
                                                  ? Colors.white
                                                  : Colors.red,
                                        ),
                                      ),
                                    ),
                                  (page[3] as bool)
                                      ? (sharedProvider
                                                  .userData!
                                                  .data
                                                  .ghanaCard !=
                                              null
                                          ? SizedBox()
                                          : Icon(
                                            Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded,
                                            size: 15,
                                            color: ch.onSurfaceVariant
                                                .withOpacity(0.5),
                                          ))
                                      : Icon(
                                        Icons.arrow_forward_ios_rounded,
                                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(
                                          0.5,
                                        ),
                                        size: 15,
                                      ),
                                ],
                              ),
                            ],
                          ),
                        ),
                      );
                    }),
                  ),
                ),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// A simple banner that advertises becoming a vendor and opens a website when tapped.
class VendorBanner extends StatelessWidget {
  final String title;
  final String subtitle;
  final VoidCallback close;

  const VendorBanner({
    super.key,

    this.title = 'Become a Vendor',

    this.borderRadius = const BorderRadius.all(Radius.circular(12)),
    required this.close,
  });

  Future<void> _openUrl(BuildContext context, String url) async {
    !await launchUrl(Uri.parse(url), mode: LaunchMode.externalApplication);
  }

  @override

      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,

import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/value_validation.dart';

/// The backend returns phone as a bare number with the country code
/// concatenated straight on, e.g. "233250000000" — no separator, no
/// leading "+". This just makes it readable as "+233 25 000 0000"
/// without touching what's actually stored.
String _formatPhone(String raw) {
  var digits = raw.replaceAll(RegExp(r'[^0-9]'), '');
  if (digits.length < 9) return raw;
  var local = digits.substring(digits.length - 9);
  var cc = digits.substring(0, digits.length - 9);
  if (cc.isEmpty) cc = "233";
  return "+$cc ${local.substring(0, 2)} ${local.substring(2, 5)} ${local.substring(5)}";
}

class PersonalProfile extends StatefulWidget {

  State<PersonalProfile> createState() => _PersonalProfileState();
}

class _PersonalProfileState extends State<PersonalProfile> {

              end: Alignment.bottomCenter,

        ),

class UNDi extends StatefulWidget {
  const UNDi({super.key});

  @override
  State<UNDi> createState() => _UNDiState();
}

class _UNDiState extends State<UNDi> {
  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    return AbsorbPointer(
      absorbing: sharedProvider.dialogueLoading,
      child: AlertDialog(
        backgroundColor: ch.surface,
        titlePadding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 20, right: 20, top: 20, bottom: 10),
        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.only(
          left: 20,
          right: 20,
          top: 0,
          bottom: 15,
        ),
        title: Text(
          "Update Your Name",
          style: ptstyle(size: 18, weight: 500, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
        ),
        content: Column(
          mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
          children: [
            OutlineInput(
              label: "",
              controller: sharedProvider.fullNameController,
              maxLength: 80,
              onchange: (value) {
                setState(() {});
              },
            ),
            SizedBox(height: 10),
            Text(
              "At least 6 characters, must contain letters only",
              style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        actions: [
          Opacity(
            opacity: sharedProvider.dialogueLoading ? 0.3 : 1,
            child: ElevatedButton(
              onPressed: () => Navigator.of(context).pop(false),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(elevation: 0),
              child: Text("Cancel"),
            ),
          ),
          SizedBox(width: 5),
          sharedProvider.dialogueLoading
              ? SizedBox(
                height: 20,
                width: 20,
                child: CircularProgressIndicator(),
              )
              : Opacity(
                opacity:
                    sharedProvider.fullNameController.text.length < 5 ||
                            !sharedProvider
                                .fullNameController
                                .text
                                .containsLetters ||
                            sharedProvider.fullNameController.text ==
                                sharedProvider.userData!.data.name
                        ? 0.4
                        : 1,
                child: AbsorbPointer(
                  absorbing:
                      sharedProvider.fullNameController.text.length < 5 ||
                      !sharedProvider.fullNameController.text.containsLetters ||
                      sharedProvider.fullNameController.text ==
                          sharedProvider.userData!.data.name,
                  child: TextButton(
                    onPressed:
                        () => {
                          FocusScope.of(context).unfocus(),
                          sharedProvider.updateName(context),
                        },
                    child: Text("Confirm Update"),
                  ),
                ),
              ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

  void openEmail() async {
    final Uri emailUri = Uri(scheme: 'mailto', path: contacts['email']!);

    if (await canLaunchUrl(emailUri)) {
      await launchUrl(emailUri);
    } else {
      throw 'Could not launch $emailUri';
    }
  }

  void callPhone() async {
    final Uri phoneUri = Uri(scheme: 'tel', path: contacts['phone']!);

    if (await canLaunchUrl(phoneUri)) {
      await launchUrl(phoneUri);
    } else {
      throw 'Could not launch $phoneUri';
    }
  }

  void openUrl() async {
    final Uri url = Uri.https(contacts['website']!);

    if (await canLaunchUrl(url)) {
      await launchUrl(url);
    } else {
      throw 'Could not launch $url';
    }
  }

                          onPressed: () => callPhone(),
                          child: Text(
                            contacts['phone']!,
                            style: ptstyle(
                              size: 12,
                              weight: 400,
                              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              decoration: TextDecoration.underline,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
              SizedBox(height: 10),
              Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  Padding(
                    padding: const EdgeInsets.all(2),
                    child: Icon(
                      Icons.public_outlined,
                      color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                      size: 18,
                    ),
                  ),

                  Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      Padding(
                        padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                        child: Text(
                          "Visit our website",
                          style: ptstyle(
                            size: 12,
                            weight: 400,
                            color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.8),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),

                      SizedBox(
                        height: 30,
                        child: TextButton(
                          style: TextButton.styleFrom(
                            padding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(
                              horizontal: 10,
                              vertical: 0,
                            ),
                          ),
                          onPressed: () => openUrl(),
                          child: Text(
                            contacts['website']!,
                            style: ptstyle(
                              size: 12,
                              weight: 400,
                              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              decoration: TextDecoration.underline,
                            ),
                          ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ],
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget buildExpandableFAQItem(BuildContext context, Map faq, int index) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    bool isExpanded = expandedIndex == index;

    return AnimatedCrossFade(
      firstChild: ListTile(
        contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
        title: Text(
          faq['question'],
          style: ptstyle(
            size: 12,
            weight: 600,
            color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.8),
          ),
        ),
        trailing: Icon(
          Icons.keyboard_arrow_right_rounded,
          color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
        ),
        onTap: () => toggleExpansion(index),
      ),
      secondChild: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          ListTile(
            contentPadding: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),

            title: Text(
              faq['question'],
              style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
            ),
            trailing: Icon(
              Icons.keyboard_arrow_down_rounded,
              color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
            ),
            onTap: () => toggleExpansion(index),
          ),
          Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.only(
              top: 0,
              bottom: 20,
              left: 10,
              right: 10,
            ),
            child: Text(
              faq['answer'],
              style: ptstyle(
                size: 12,
                weight: 400,
                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.8),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
      crossFadeState:
          isExpanded ? CrossFadeState.showSecond : CrossFadeState.showFirst,
      duration: Duration(milliseconds: 300),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:adaptive_theme/adaptive_theme.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/extensions/value_formating.dart';

class StoreInfoPage extends StatelessWidget {
  StoreInfoPage({super.key, required this.store_id});
  final dynamic store_id;

  var deliveryOptions = {
    'pickup': {
      'title': 'Pick up',
      'address': '52 Kofi Annan Rd, Accra',
      'hours': 'Mon - Sat, 8:00AM - 7:00PM',
    },
    'delivery': {
      'title': 'Delivery',
      'address': '52 Kofi Annan Rd, Accra',
      'hours': 'GHS 10-30 • 1 - 2 days',
    },
  };

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    return AnimatedSwitcher(
      duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 500),
      switchInCurve: Curves.easeInOut,
      switchOutCurve: Curves.easeInOut,

      child: Scaffold(
        appBar: AppBar(
          flexibleSpace: Container(
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              gradient: LinearGradient(
                colors: [
                  ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.5),
                  ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.2),
                  ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.05),
                ],
                begin: Alignment.topCenter,
                end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
              ),
            ),
          ),
          scrolledUnderElevation: 0,
          excludeHeaderSemantics: true,
          backgroundColor: ch.surface,
          elevation: 0,
          automaticallyImplyLeading: true,
          title: Text('Store Info', style: ptstyle(size: 15, weight: 400)),
          centerTitle: true,
        ),
        body:
            sharedProvider.loadingVendor
                ? PDShimmer(ch: ch)
                : Padding(
                  padding: const EdgeInsets.all(10.0),
                  child: Column(
                    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                    children: [
                      _buildStoreDetails(
                        ch,
                        sharedProvider.vendorData!,
                        context,
                      ),
                      SizedBox(height: 15),
                      _buildActionButtons(
                        ch,
                        sharedProvider.vendorData!.phoneNumber,
                      ),

                      if (sharedProvider.vendorData!.reviews.isNotEmpty)
                        _buildReviewsSection(
                          ch,
                          sharedProvider.vendorData!.reviews,
                          context,
                        ),
                      SDivider(),
                      Expanded(child: StoreListings()),
                    ],
                  ),
                ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildStoreDetails(
    ColorScheme ch,
    VendorDetails store,
    BuildContext context,
  ) {
    int totalRT = store.reviews.length;
    int totalRatingValue = store.reviews.fold(
      0,
      (sum, review) => sum + review.rating,
    );
    double averageRating = totalRatingValue / totalRT;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Stack(
          alignment: Alignment.topCenter,
          children: [
            ClipRRect(
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
              child: Image.network(
                store.image!,
                fit: BoxFit.cover,
                width: double.infinity,
                height: 90,
                errorBuilder:
                    (context, error, stackTrace) => Opacity(
                      opacity: 0.3,
                      child: Image.asset(
                        AssetsHelper.dummy2Image,
                        fit: BoxFit.cover,
                        width: double.infinity,
                        alignment: Alignment.centerLeft,
                        height: 90,
                      ),
                    ),
              ),
            ),
            Container(
              width: double.infinity,
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                gradient: LinearGradient(
                  colors: [
                    ch.surface,
                    ch.surface.withOpacity(0.9),
                    ch.surface.withOpacity(0.7),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
              child: Row(
                crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                children: [
                  SizedBox(
                    width: 90,
                    height: 90,
                    child: Card(
                      color:
                          AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                              ? ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.5)
                              : ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.05),
                      elevation: 0,
                      child: ClipRRect(
                        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8.0),
                        child: Image.network(
                          store.image!,
                          fit: BoxFit.cover,
                          errorBuilder:
                              (context, error, stackTrace) => Opacity(
                                opacity:
                                    AdaptiveTheme.of(context).mode.isDark
                                        ? 0.2
                                        : 0.6,
                                child: Image.asset(
                                  AssetsHelper.dummy2Image,
                                  fit: BoxFit.cover,
                                ),
                              ),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                  ),
                  SizedBox(width: 10),

                  Expanded(
                    child: Column(
                      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                      children: [
                        Text(store.name, style: ptstyle(size: 16, weight: 600)),
                        SizedBox(height: 5),
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Flexible(
                              child: Text(
                                store.location,
                                style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400),
                              ),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 8),
                            if (averageRating.toString().toLowerCase() != "nan")
                              Row(
                                children: [
                                  Icon(
                                    Icons.star,
                                    color: Colors.orangeAccent,
                                    size: 15,
                                  ),
                                  Text(' $averageRating Ratings'),
                                ],
                              ),
                          ],
                        ),
                        SizedBox(height: 8),
                        Row(
                          children: [
                            Text(
                              store.status.toSentenceCase(),
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 12,
                                weight: 600,
                                color:
                                    store.status.toLowerCase() == "active"
                                        ? Colors.green
                                        : ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              ),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 8),
                            Text(
                              '•',
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 12,
                                weight: 600,
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              ),
                            ),
                            SizedBox(width: 5),
                            // FIX: closingTime is nullable (vendors may not
                            // have set business hours yet — see Vendor
                            // model). Null-aware call + fallback text so
                            // this compiles and renders sensibly either way.
                            Text(
                              'Closes at ${store.closingTime?.toAmPm() ?? "N/A"}',
                              style: ptstyle(
                                size: 12,
                                weight: 400,
                                color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                              ),
                            ),
                          ],
                        ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildActionButtons(ColorScheme ch, contact) {
    return Container(
      margin: EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.2),
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(15),
      ),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 2),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
        mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: FilledButton.tonalIcon(
              onPressed: () async {
                final Uri phoneUri = Uri(scheme: 'tel', path: contact);

                if (await canLaunchUrl(phoneUri)) {
                  await launchUrl(phoneUri);
                } else {
                  throw 'Could not launch $phoneUri';
                }
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.phone_outlined),
              label: Text('Call'),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(width: 10),
          Expanded(
            child: FilledButton.icon(
              onPressed: () async {
                final Uri whatssAppContact = Uri.parse(
                  "https://wa.me/233$contact",
                );

                if (await canLaunchUrl(whatssAppContact)) {
                  await launchUrl(whatssAppContact);
                } else {
                  throw 'Could not launch $whatssAppContact';
                }
              },
              icon: const Icon(Icons.message_outlined),
              label: Text('Chat'),
              style: ElevatedButton.styleFrom(
                shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReviewsSection(
    ColorScheme ch,
    List<Review> reviews,
    BuildContext context,
  ) {
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Row(
          mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
          children: [
            Text(
              'Reviews & Rating',
              style: ptstyle(size: 16, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
            ),
            SizedBox(
              height: 40,
              child: TextButton(
                onPressed:
                    () => NavigationHelper.push(
                      context,
                      ReviewsPage(reviews: reviews),
                    ),
                child: Text(
                  'View all',
                  style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 600, color: Colors.blue),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        Divider(color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.05)),

        for (var review in reviews.take(1))
          _buildReviewCard(
            name: review.customer?.name ?? "User**${review.createdAt.hashCode}",
            date: review.createdAt.toString(),
            rating: review.rating,
            comment: review.comment,
            ch: ch,
          ),
      ],
    );
  }

  Widget _buildReviewCard({
    required String name,
    required String date,
    required int rating,
    required String comment,
    required ColorScheme ch,
  }) {
    return Container(
      width: double.infinity,

      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Row(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,
                  children: [
                    Text(
                      name,
                      style: ptstyle(
                        size: 12,
                        weight: 600,
                        color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                      ),
                    ),

                    Row(
                      mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,
                      children: [
                        for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++)
                          Icon(
                            Icons.star,
                            color:
                                i < rating
                                    ? Colors.orangeAccent
                                    : ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
                            size: 15,
                          ),
                      ],
                    ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 8),

          Text(
            comment,
            maxLines: 1,
            overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
            style: ptstyle(size: 15, weight: 600, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 4),
          Text(
            DateTime.parse(date).formatDate(),
            style: ptstyle(size: 12, weight: 400, color: ch.onSurfaceVariant),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class PDShimmer extends StatelessWidget {
  const PDShimmer({super.key, required this.ch});

  final ColorScheme ch;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    return Shimmer.fromColors(
      period: Duration(milliseconds: 500),
      baseColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.1),
      highlightColor: ch.primaryFixed.withOpacity(0.3),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          // Image Placeholder
          Container(
            width: double.infinity,
            height: 150,
            decoration: BoxDecoration(
              color: Colors.white,
              borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
            ),
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          // Title + Price
          Row(
            children: [
              Container(height: 50, width: 80, color: Colors.white),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Container(
                  height: 50,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
              const SizedBox(width: 10),
              Expanded(
                child: Container(
                  height: 50,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
          const SizedBox(height: 10),

          // Specifications (3-4 lines)
          Column(
            children: List.generate(
              10,
              (index) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4.0),
                child: Container(
                  height: 14,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          const SizedBox(height: 20),

          // Pricing options
          Column(
            children: List.generate(
              3,
              (index) => Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 10.0),
                child: Container(
                  height: 60,
                  width: double.infinity,
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),

          const Spacer(),

          // Bottom Buttons
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(
                child: Container(
                  height: 50,
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(right: 8),
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(
                child: Container(
                  height: 50,
                  margin: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 8),
                  color: Colors.white,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

import 'package:flexiphones/helpers/navigation_helper.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/provider/provider_class.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/screens/browse/sub_pages.dart/product_details.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/generalwidgets.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/product_card.dart';
import 'package:flexiphones/shared_widgets/space_divider.dart';
import 'package:flutter/material.dart';
import 'package:provider/provider.dart';

class StoreListings extends StatelessWidget {
  const StoreListings({super.key});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
      children: [
        Padding(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 10.0),
          child: Row(
            children: [
              Text(
                "${sharedProvider.vendorData!.name}'s",
                style: ptstyle(
                  size: 15,
                  weight: 600,
                  color: ch.onSurfaceVariant.withOpacity(0.5),
                ),
              ),
              Text(
                ' listings',
                style: ptstyle(
                  size: 15,
                  weight: 600,
                  color: ch.onSurfaceVariant,
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
        SizedBox(height: 10),
        Expanded(
          child: ListView.separated(
            separatorBuilder: (context, index) => SizedBox(),
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: 0),
            itemCount: sharedProvider.vendorData!.products.length,
            itemBuilder: (context, index) {
              final product = sharedProvider.vendorData!.products[index];

              return ProductCard(
                status: product.status,
                imageUrl: product.image,
                productName:
                    "${product.brand.toLowerCase()} ${product.model.toLowerCase()}",
                price: product.price,
                initialDeposit: 0.0,
                onPressed: () async {
                  sharedProvider.getProductDetails(product.id, context);
                  NavigationHelper.push(
                    context,
                    ProductDetailsPage(productId: product.id),
                  );
                },
                colors: product.colors,
                deviceSpecification: '',
              );
            },
          ),
        ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

    '+975', // Bhutan

    '+977', // Nepal
    '+992', // Tajikistan
    '+993', // Turkmenistan
    '+994', // Azerbaijan
    '+995', // Georgia
    '+996', // Kyrgyzstan
    '+998', // Uzbekistan
  ];

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final sharedProvider = Provider.of<SharedProvider>(context);
    var ch = Theme.of(context).colorScheme;
    return Opacity(
      opacity: widget.enabled ? 1.0 : 0.6,
      child: AbsorbPointer(
        absorbing: !widget.enabled,
        child: Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: ch.primaryFixedDim.withOpacity(0.15),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
          ),
          child: Row(
            crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.center,
            children: [
              Container(
                padding: EdgeInsets.only(
                  left: 10,
                  right: 10,
                  top: 2,
                  bottom: 2,
                ),
                // decoration: BoxDecoration(
                //   color: ch.primaryContainer.withOpacity(0.5),
                //   borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8),
                // ),
                alignment: Alignment.center,
                child: DropdownButtonHideUnderline(
                  child: DropdownButton<String>(
                    value: sharedProvider.countryCode,
                    menuMaxHeight: 300,

                    onChanged: (String? newValue) {
                      setState(() {
                        sharedProvider.countryCode = newValue!;
                      });
                      // Optional: Notify parent of change
                      widget.onChanged?.call(
                        '${sharedProvider.countryCode} ${widget.controller?.text ?? ""}',
                      );
                    },
                    items:
                        countryCodes.map<DropdownMenuItem<String>>((
                          String code,
                        ) {
                          return DropdownMenuItem<String>(
                            value: code,
                            child: Text(code),
                          );
                        }).toList(),
                    style: ptstyle(
                      size: 12,
                      weight: 600,
                      color: ch.onPrimaryContainer,
                    ),
                    icon: Icon(Icons.arrow_drop_down, color: Colors.grey[600]),
                    isExpanded: false,
                  ),
                ),
              ),

              SizedBox(height: 40, child: VerticalDivider()),
              Expanded(
                child: FilledInput(
                  filled: false,
                  controller: widget.controller,
                  hintText: widget.hintText,
                  error: sharedProvider.phoneInValid,
                  keyboardType: widget.keyboardType,
                  enabled: widget.enabled,
                  maxLength: 9,
                  onChange: (value) {
                    sharedProvider.phoneInValid = false;
                    widget.onChanged?.call(
                      '${sharedProvider.countryCode} $value',
                    );
                  },
                  onSubmitted: widget.onSubmitted,
                  label: '',
                ),
              ),
            ],
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}
